```
    30→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    31→# Grouping key computation
    32→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    33→
    34→def _extract_subtype(finding: dict) -> str | None:
    35→    """Extract the subtype/kind from a finding.
    36→
    37→    Checks detail.kind first, then falls back to parsing the finding ID
    38→    (format: ``detector::file::subtype`` or ``detector::file::symbol::subtype``).
    39→    """
    40→    detail = finding.get("detail") or {}
    41→    kind = detail.get("kind")
    42→    if kind:
    43→        return kind
    44→
    45→    # Parse from finding ID — third segment is often the subtype
    46→    fid = finding.get("id", "")
    47→    parts = fid.split("::")
    48→    if len(parts) >= 3:
    49→        # For IDs like smells::file.py::silent_except, the subtype is parts[2]
    50→        # For IDs like dict_keys::file.py::key::phantom_read, subtype is last part
    51→        candidate = parts[-1]
    52→        # Skip if it looks like a file path or symbol name (contains / or .)
    53→        if "/" not in candidate and "." not in candidate:
    54→            return candidate
    55→    return None
    56→
    57→
    58→def _grouping_key(finding: dict, meta: DetectorMeta | None) -> str | None:
    59→    """Compute a deterministic grouping key for a finding.
    60→
    61→    Returns None if the finding should not be auto-clustered.
    62→    """
    63→    detector = finding.get("detector", "")
    64→
    65→    if meta is None:
    66→        # Unknown detector — group by detector name
    67→        return f"detector::{detector}"
    68→
    69→    # Review findings → group by dimension
    70→    if detector in ("review", "subjective_review"):
    71→        detail = finding.get("detail") or {}
    72→        dimension = detail.get("dimension", "")
    73→        if dimension:
    74→            return f"review::{dimension}"
    75→        return f"detector::{detector}"
    76→
    77→    # Per-file detectors (structural, responsibility_cohesion)
    78→    if meta.needs_judgment and detector in (
    79→        "structural", "responsibility_cohesion",
    80→    ):
    81→        file = finding.get("file", "")
    82→        if file:
    83→            basename = os.path.basename(file)
    84→            return f"file::{detector}::{basename}"
    85→
    86→    # Needs-judgment detectors — group by subtype when available
    87→    if meta.needs_judgment:
    88→        subtype = _extract_subtype(finding)
    89→        if subtype:
    90→            return f"typed::{detector}::{subtype}"
    91→
    92→    # Pure auto-fix (no judgment needed) → all findings by detector
    93→    if meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and not meta.needs_judgment:
    94→        return f"auto::{detector}"
    95→
    96→    # Everything else → by detector
    97→    return f"detector::{detector}"
    98→
    99→
   100→def _cluster_name_from_key(key: str) -> str:
   101→    """Convert a grouping key to a cluster name with auto/ prefix."""
   102→    # auto::unused → auto/unused
   103→    # typed::dict_keys::phantom_read → auto/dict_keys-phantom_read
   104→    # file::structural::big_file.py → auto/structural-big_file.py
   105→    # review::abstraction_fitness → auto/review-abstraction_fitness
   106→    # detector::security → auto/security
   107→    parts = key.split("::")
   108→    if len(parts) == 2:
   109→        prefix_type = parts[0]
   110→        # For review keys, keep the prefix for clarity
   111→        if prefix_type == "review":
   112→            return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}review-{parts[1]}"
   113→        return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}{parts[1]}"
   114→    if len(parts) == 3:
   115→        return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}{parts[1]}-{parts[2]}"
   116→    return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}{key.replace('::', '-')}"
   117→
   118→
   119→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   120→# Description and action generation
   121→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   122→
   123→def _generate_description(
   124→    cluster_name: str,
   125→    members: list[dict],
   126→    meta: DetectorMeta | None,
   127→    subtype: str | None,
   128→) -> str:
   129→    """Generate a human-readable cluster description."""
   130→    count = len(members)
   131→    detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
   132→
   133→    if detector in ("review", "subjective_review"):
   134→        detail = (members[0].get("detail") or {}) if members else {}
   135→        dimension = detail.get("dimension", detector)
   136→        return f"Address {count} {dimension} review findings"
   137→
   138→    if detector == "structural":
   139→        files = {os.path.basename(m.get("file", "")) for m in members}
   140→        if len(files) == 1:
   141→            return f"Decompose {next(iter(files))}"
   142→        return f"Decompose {count} large files"
   143→
   144→    display = meta.display if meta else detector
   145→    if subtype:
   146→        label = subtype.replace("_", " ")
   147→        return f"Fix {count} {label} issues"
   148→
   149→    if meta and meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and not meta.needs_judgment:
   150→        return f"Remove {count} {display} findings"
   151→
   152→    return f"Fix {count} {display} issues"
   153→
   154→
   155→def _subtype_has_fixer(meta: DetectorMeta, subtype: str | None) -> str | None:
   156→    """Check if a subtype maps to a specific fixer."""
   157→    if not meta.fixers or not subtype:
   158→        return None
   159→    # Convention: fixer name is subtype with _ replaced by -
   160→    fixer_name = subtype.replace("_", "-")
   161→    if fixer_name in meta.fixers:
   162→        return fixer_name
   163→    # Also check if subtype is a substring (e.g. "unused" matches "unused-imports")
   164→    for fixer in meta.fixers:
   165→        if subtype in fixer:
   166→            return fixer
   167→    return None
   168→
   169→
   170→def _strip_guidance_examples(guidance: str) -> str:
   171→    """Strip subtype-specific examples from guidance text.
   172→
   173→    Many guidance strings have format "verb — specific examples".
   174→    Strip after the dash to get the core action:
   175→      "fix code smells — dead useEffect, empty if chains" → "fix code smells"
   176→    """
   177→    if " — " in guidance:
   178→        return guidance.split(" — ", 1)[0].strip()
   179→    return guidance
   180→
   181→
   182→_ACTION_TYPE_TEMPLATES: dict[str, str] = {
   183→    "reorganize": "reorganize with desloppify move",
   184→    "refactor": "review and refactor each finding",
   185→    "manual_fix": "review and fix each finding",
   186→}
   187→
   188→
   189→def _generate_action(
   190→    meta: DetectorMeta | None,
   191→    subtype: str | None,
   192→) -> str:
   193→    """Generate an action string from detector metadata.
   194→
   195→    Always returns a non-empty string — every cluster gets an action.
   196→    """
   197→    if meta is None:
   198→        return "review and fix each finding"
   199→
   200→    # For detectors with subtypes, only suggest a fixer if the subtype matches
   201→    if subtype and meta.fixers:
   202→        matched_fixer = _subtype_has_fixer(meta, subtype)
   203→        if matched_fixer:
   204→            return f"desloppify fix {matched_fixer} --dry-run"
   205→    elif meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and meta.fixers and not meta.needs_judgment:
   206→        # Pure auto-fix detector, no subtypes
   207→        return f"desloppify fix {meta.fixers[0]} --dry-run"
   208→
   209→    if meta.tool == "move":
   210→        return "desloppify move"
   211→
   212→    # Guidance available — use it (strip examples for subtyped detectors)
   213→    if meta.guidance:
   214→        if subtype:
   215→            return _strip_guidance_examples(meta.guidance)
   216→        return meta.guidance
   217→
   218→    # Final fallback: action_type template
   219→    return _ACTION_TYPE_TEMPLATES.get(
   220→        meta.action_type, "review and fix each finding"
   221→    )
   222→
   223→
   224→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   225→# Repair
   226→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   227→
   228→def _repair_ghost_cluster_refs(plan: PlanModel, now: str) -> int:
   229→    """Clear override cluster refs that point to non-existent clusters."""
   230→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   231→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
   232→    repaired = 0
   233→    for override in overrides.values():
   234→        cluster_name = override.get("cluster")
   235→        if cluster_name and cluster_name not in clusters:
   236→            override["cluster"] = None
   237→            override["updated_at"] = now
   238→            repaired += 1
   239→    return repaired
   240→
   241→
   242→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   243→# Shared create-or-update helper
   244→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   245→
   246→def _sync_auto_cluster(
   247→    plan: PlanModel,
   248→    clusters: dict,
   249→    existing_by_key: dict[str, str],
   250→    *,
   251→    cluster_key: str,
   252→    cluster_name: str,
   253→    member_ids: list[str],
   254→    description: str,
   255→    action: str,
   256→    now: str,
   257→    optional: bool = False,
   258→) -> int:
   259→    """Create or update an auto-cluster and sync its override entries.
   260→
   261→    Handles the common pattern: check if cluster exists by key, update
   262→    membership/metadata if changed, or create a new cluster.  Always syncs
   263→    override entries for all *member_ids*.
   264→
   265→    Returns 1 if a change was made, 0 otherwise.
   266→    """
   267→    changes = 0
   268→    existing_name = existing_by_key.get(cluster_key)
   269→    if existing_name and existing_name in clusters:
   270→        cluster = clusters[existing_name]
   271→        old_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   272→        new_ids_set = set(member_ids)
   273→        if old_ids != new_ids_set or cluster.get("description") != description or cluster.get("action") != action:
   274→            cluster["finding_ids"] = list(member_ids)
   275→            cluster["description"] = description
   276→            cluster["action"] = action
   277→            cluster["updated_at"] = now
   278→            changes = 1
   279→    else:
   280→        new_cluster: Cluster = {
   281→            "name": cluster_name,
   282→            "description": description,
   283→            "finding_ids": list(member_ids),
   284→            "created_at": now,
   285→            "updated_at": now,
   286→            "auto": True,
   287→            "cluster_key": cluster_key,
   288→            "action": action,
   289→            "user_modified": False,
   290→        }
   291→        if optional:
   292→            new_cluster["optional"] = True
   293→        clusters[cluster_name] = new_cluster
   294→        existing_by_key[cluster_key] = cluster_name
   295→        changes = 1
   296→
   297→    # Sync overrides
   298→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
   299→    current_name = existing_by_key.get(cluster_key, cluster_name)
   300→    for fid in member_ids:
   301→        if fid not in overrides:
   302→            overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   303→        overrides[fid]["cluster"] = current_name
   304→        overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   305→
   306→    return changes
   307→
   308→
   309→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   310→# Main algorithm
   311→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   312→
   313→def _sync_finding_clusters(
   314→    plan: PlanModel,
   315→    findings: dict,
   316→    clusters: dict,
   317→    existing_by_key: dict[str, str],
   318→    active_auto_keys: set[str],
   319→    now: str,
   320→) -> int:
   321→    """Group open findings by detector/subtype and sync auto-clusters."""
   322→    changes = 0
   323→
   324→    # Set of finding IDs in manual (non-auto) clusters
   325→    manual_member_ids: set[str] = set()
   326→    for cluster in clusters.values():
   327→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   328→            manual_member_ids.update(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   329→
   330→    # Collect open, non-suppressed findings and group by key
   331→    groups: dict[str, list[str]] = defaultdict(list)
   332→    finding_data: dict[str, dict] = {}
   333→    for fid, finding in findings.items():
   334→        if finding.get("status") != "open":
   335→            continue
   336→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
   337→            continue
   338→        if fid in manual_member_ids:
   339→            continue
   340→
   341→        detector = finding.get("detector", "")
   342→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   343→        key = _grouping_key(finding, meta)
   344→        if key is None:
   345→            continue
   346→
   347→        groups[key].append(fid)
   348→        finding_data[fid] = finding
   349→
   350→    # Drop singleton groups
   351→    groups = {k: v for k, v in groups.items() if len(v) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE}
   352→
   353→    for key, member_ids in groups.items():
   354→        active_auto_keys.add(key)
   355→        cluster_name = _cluster_name_from_key(key)
   356→
   357→        # Representative finding for metadata
   358→        rep = finding_data.get(member_ids[0], {})
   359→        detector = rep.get("detector", "")
   360→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   361→        members = [finding_data[fid] for fid in member_ids if fid in finding_data]
   362→
   363→        # Extract subtype from grouping key (typed::detector::subtype)
   364→        key_parts = key.split("::")
   365→        subtype = key_parts[2] if len(key_parts) >= 3 else None
   366→
   367→        description = _generate_description(cluster_name, members, meta, subtype)
   368→        action = _generate_action(meta, subtype)
   369→
   370→        # User-modified clusters: merge new members only, keep user edits
   371→        existing_name = existing_by_key.get(key)
   372→        if existing_name and existing_name in clusters:
   373→            cluster = clusters[existing_name]
   374→            if cluster.get("user_modified"):
   375→                existing_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   376→                new_ids = [fid for fid in member_ids if fid not in existing_ids]
   377→                if new_ids:
   378→                    cluster["finding_ids"].extend(new_ids)
   379→                    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   380→                    changes += 1
   381→                # Still sync overrides for all members
   382→                overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
   383→                for fid in member_ids:
   384→                    if fid not in overrides:
   385→                        overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   386→                    overrides[fid]["cluster"] = existing_name
   387→                    overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   388→                continue
   389→
   390→        # Handle name collision (existing auto-cluster with different key)
   391→        if cluster_name in clusters and clusters[cluster_name].get("cluster_key") != key:
   392→            cluster_name = f"{cluster_name}-{len(member_ids)}"
   393→
   394→        changes += _sync_auto_cluster(
   395→            plan, clusters, existing_by_key,
   396→            cluster_key=key,
   397→            cluster_name=cluster_name,
   398→            member_ids=member_ids,
   399→            description=description,
   400→            action=action,
   401→            now=now,
   402→        )
   403→
   404→    return changes
   405→
   406→
   407→def _sync_subjective_clusters(
   408→    plan: PlanModel,
   409→    state: StateModel,
   410→    findings: dict,
   411→    clusters: dict,
   412→    existing_by_key: dict[str, str],
   413→    active_auto_keys: set[str],
   414→    now: str,
   415→    *,
   416→    target_strict: float,
   417→) -> int:
   418→    """Sync unscored, stale, and under-target subjective dimension clusters."""
   419→    changes = 0
   420→
   421→    all_subjective_ids = sorted(
   422→        fid for fid in plan.get("queue_order", [])
   423→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   424→    )
   425→    unscored_state_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   426→    unscored_queue_ids = sorted(
   427→        fid for fid in all_subjective_ids if fid in unscored_state_ids
   428→    )
   429→    stale_state_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   430→    stale_queue_ids = sorted(
   431→        fid for fid in all_subjective_ids
   432→        if fid in stale_state_ids and fid not in unscored_state_ids
   433→    )
   434→
   435→    # -- Initial review cluster (unscored, min size 1) ---------------------
   436→    if len(unscored_queue_ids) >= _MIN_UNSCORED_CLUSTER_SIZE:
   437→        active_auto_keys.add(_UNSCORED_KEY)
   438→        cli_keys = [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in unscored_queue_ids]
   439→        description = f"Initial review of {len(unscored_queue_ids)} unscored subjective dimensions"
   440→        action = f"desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {','.join(cli_keys)}"
   441→        changes += _sync_auto_cluster(
   442→            plan, clusters, existing_by_key,
   443→            cluster_key=_UNSCORED_KEY,
   444→            cluster_name=_UNSCORED_NAME,
   445→            member_ids=unscored_queue_ids,
   446→            description=description,
   447→            action=action,
   448→            now=now,
   449→        )
   450→
   451→    # -- Stale review cluster (previously scored, min size 2) --------------
   452→    if len(stale_queue_ids) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE:
   453→        active_auto_keys.add(_STALE_KEY)
   454→        cli_keys = [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in stale_queue_ids]
   455→        description = f"Re-review {len(stale_queue_ids)} stale subjective dimensions"
   456→        action = (
   457→            "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions "
   458→            + ",".join(cli_keys)
   459→        )
   460→        changes += _sync_auto_cluster(
   461→            plan, clusters, existing_by_key,
   462→            cluster_key=_STALE_KEY,
   463→            cluster_name=_STALE_NAME,
   464→            member_ids=stale_queue_ids,
   465→            description=description,
   466→            action=action,
   467→            now=now,
   468→        )
   469→
   470→    # -- Under-target review cluster (optional, current but below target) ----
   471→    under_target_ids = current_under_target_ids(state, target_strict=target_strict)
   472→    under_target_queue_ids = sorted(under_target_ids)
   473→
   474→    # Prune: remove IDs that were previously in the under-target cluster
   475→    # but are no longer under target (they've improved above threshold).
   476→    prev_ut_cluster = clusters.get(_UNDER_TARGET_NAME, {})
   477→    prev_ut_ids = set(prev_ut_cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   478→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   479→    _ut_prune = [
   480→        fid for fid in prev_ut_ids
   481→        if fid not in under_target_ids
   482→        and fid not in stale_state_ids
   483→        and fid not in unscored_state_ids
   484→        and fid in order
   485→    ]
   486→    for fid in _ut_prune:
   487→        order.remove(fid)
   488→        changes += 1
   489→
   490→    # Guard: only inject under-target items when no objective findings
   491→    # remain open — mirror the guard used by sync_stale_dimensions().
   492→    has_objective_items = any(
   493→        f.get("status") == "open"
   494→        and f.get("detector") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   495→        and not f.get("suppressed")
   496→        for f in findings.values()
   497→    )
   498→
   499→    if not has_objective_items and len(under_target_queue_ids) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE:
   500→        active_auto_keys.add(_UNDER_TARGET_KEY)
   501→        cli_keys = [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in under_target_queue_ids]
   502→        description = (
   503→            f"Consider re-reviewing {len(under_target_queue_ids)} "
   504→            f"dimensions under target score"
   505→        )
   506→        action = (
   507→            "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions "
   508→            + ",".join(cli_keys)
   509→        )
   510→        changes += _sync_auto_cluster(
   511→            plan, clusters, existing_by_key,
   512→            [REDACTED],
   513→            cluster_name=_UNDER_TARGET_NAME,
   514→            member_ids=under_target_queue_ids,
   515→            description=description,
   516→            action=action,
   517→            now=now,
   518→            optional=True,
   519→        )
   520→
   521→        # Ensure under-target IDs are in queue_order (at the back)
   522→        order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   523→        existing_order = set(order)
   524→        for fid in under_target_queue_ids:
   525→            if fid not in existing_order:
   526→                order.append(fid)
   527→
   528→    # Evict under-target IDs from queue when objective backlog has returned.
   529→    if has_objective_items:
   530→        _objective_evict = [
   531→            fid for fid in order
   532→            if fid in under_target_ids
   533→        ]
   534→        for fid in _objective_evict:
   535→            order.remove(fid)
   536→            changes += 1
   537→
   538→    return changes
   539→
   540→
   541→def _prune_stale_clusters(
   542→    plan: PlanModel,
   543→    findings: dict,
   544→    clusters: dict,
   545→    active_auto_keys: set[str],
   546→    now: str,
   547→) -> int:
   548→    """Delete auto-clusters that no longer have matching groups."""
   549→    changes = 0
   550→    for name in list(clusters.keys()):
   551→        cluster = clusters[name]
   552→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   553→            continue
   554→        ck = cluster.get("cluster_key", "")
   555→        if ck in active_auto_keys:
   556→            continue
   557→        if cluster.get("user_modified"):
   558→            # Keep user-modified clusters but prune dead member IDs
   559→            alive = [fid for fid in cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
   560→                     if fid in findings and findings[fid].get("status") == "open"]
   561→            if alive:
   562→                if len(alive) != len(cluster.get("finding_ids", [])):
   563→                    cluster["finding_ids"] = alive
   564→                    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   565→                    changes += 1
   566→                continue
   567→            # All members gone — delete even if user_modified
   568→        # Delete stale cluster
   569→        del clusters[name]
   570→        # Clear cluster refs from overrides
   571→        for fid in cluster.get("finding_ids", []):
   572→            override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(fid)
   573→            if override and override.get("cluster") == name:
   574→                override["cluster"] = None
   575→                override["updated_at"] = now
   576→        if plan.get("active_cluster") == name:
   577→            plan["active_cluster"] = None
   578→        changes += 1
   579→    return changes
   580→
   581→
   582→def auto_cluster_findings(
   583→    plan: PlanModel,
   584→    state: StateModel,
   585→    *,
   586→    target_strict: float = 95.0,
   587→) -> int:
   588→    """Regenerate auto-clusters from current open findings.
   589→
   590→    Returns count of changes made (clusters created, updated, or deleted).
   591→    """
   592→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   593→
   594→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   595→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   596→
   597→    # Map existing auto-clusters by cluster_key
   598→    existing_by_key: dict[str, str] = {}  # cluster_key → cluster_name
   599→    for name, cluster in list(clusters.items()):
   600→        if cluster.get("auto"):
   601→            ck = cluster.get("cluster_key", "")
   602→            if ck:
   603→                existing_by_key[ck] = name
   604→
   605→    now = utc_now()
   606→    active_auto_keys: set[str] = set()
   607→    changes = 0
   608→
   609→    changes += _sync_finding_clusters(
   610→        plan, findings, clusters, existing_by_key, active_auto_keys, now,
   611→    )
   612→    changes += _sync_subjective_clusters(
   613→        plan, state, findings, clusters, existing_by_key, active_auto_keys, now,
   614→        target_strict=target_strict,
   615→    )
   616→    changes += _prune_stale_clusters(
   617→        plan, findings, clusters, active_auto_keys, now,
   618→    )
   619→    changes += _repair_ghost_cluster_refs(plan, now)
   620→
   621→    plan["updated"] = now
   622→    return changes
   623→
   624→
   625→__all__ = [
   626→    "AUTO_PREFIX",
   627→    "auto_cluster_findings",
   628→]
   629→
```

```
1→"""Unified work-queue selection for next/show/plan views."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from typing import TypedDict
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
     9→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
    10→    ALL_STATUSES,
    11→    ATTEST_EXAMPLE,
    12→    build_create_plan_item,
    13→    build_subjective_items,
    14→    build_triage_stage_items,

    16→)

    19→    enrich_with_impact,
    20→    group_queue_items,

    22→    item_sort_key,
    23→)

    28→)
    29→from desloppify.state import StateModel
    30→
    31→
    32→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    33→class QueueBuildOptions:
    34→    """Configuration for queue construction."""
    35→
    36→    count: int | None = 1
    37→    scan_path: str | None = None
    38→    scope: str | None = None
    39→    status: str = "open"
    40→    include_subjective: bool = True
    41→    subjective_threshold: float = 100.0
    42→    chronic: bool = False
    43→    explain: bool = False
    44→    plan: dict | None = None
    45→    include_skipped: bool = False
    46→    cluster: str | None = None
    47→    collapse_clusters: bool = True
    48→
    49→
    50→class WorkQueueResult(TypedDict):
    51→    """Typed shape of the dict returned by :func:`build_work_queue`."""
    52→
    53→    items: list[dict]
    54→    total: int
    55→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]]
    56→    new_ids: set[str]
    57→
    58→
    59→def build_work_queue(
    60→    state: StateModel,
    61→    *,
    62→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
    63→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    64→    """Build ranked queue items + tier metadata."""
    65→    resolved_options = options or QueueBuildOptions()
    66→
    67→    status = resolved_options.status
    68→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
    69→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
    70→    try:
    71→        subjective_threshold_value = float(resolved_options.subjective_threshold)
    72→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    73→        subjective_threshold_value = 100.0
    74→    subjective_threshold_value = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold_value))
    75→
    76→    finding_items = build_finding_items(
    77→        state,
    78→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
    79→        status_filter=status,
    80→        scope=resolved_options.scope,
    81→        chronic=resolved_options.chronic,
    82→    )
    83→
    84→    all_items = list(finding_items)
    85→
    86→    # Count open objective findings for stale-subjective gating
    87→    objective_count = sum(
    88→        1 for item in finding_items
    89→        if item.get("detector") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
    90→    )
    91→
    92→    if (
    93→        resolved_options.include_subjective
    94→        and status in {"open", "all"}
    95→        and not resolved_options.chronic
    96→    ):
    97→        subjective_items = build_subjective_items(
    98→            state,
    99→            state.get("findings", {}),
   100→            threshold=subjective_threshold_value,
   101→        )
   102→        for item in subjective_items:
   103→            if not scope_matches(item, resolved_options.scope):
   104→                continue
   105→            # Non-initial subjective items only surface when objective
   106→            # backlog is drained — stale reruns AND under-target dims
   107→            # should wait until all mechanical work is done.
   108→            if not item.get("initial_review") and objective_count > 0:
   109→                continue
   110→            all_items.append(item)
   111→
   112→    # Inject triage stage items and workflow items when plan requires it
   113→    if resolved_options.plan and status in {"open", "all"}:
   114→        synth_items = build_triage_stage_items(resolved_options.plan, state)
   115→        all_items.extend(synth_items)
   116→        plan_item = build_create_plan_item(resolved_options.plan)
   117→        if plan_item is not None:
   118→            all_items.append(plan_item)
   119→
   120→    enrich_with_impact(all_items, state.get("dimension_scores", {}))
   121→    all_items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   122→
   123→    # Apply living plan ordering if provided
   124→    new_ids: set[str] = set()
   125→    if resolved_options.plan:
   126→        new_ids = _new_item_ids(state)
   127→        all_items = _apply_plan_order(
   128→            all_items,
   129→            resolved_options.plan,
   130→            include_skipped=resolved_options.include_skipped,
   131→            cluster=resolved_options.cluster,
   132→            new_ids=new_ids,
   133→        )
   134→
   135→    # Collapse auto-clusters into meta-items (unless drilling into a cluster)
   136→    should_collapse = (
   137→        resolved_options.collapse_clusters
   138→        and resolved_options.plan
   139→        and not resolved_options.cluster
   140→        and not resolved_options.plan.get("active_cluster")
   141→    )
   142→    if should_collapse:
   143→        all_items = _collapse_clusters(all_items, resolved_options.plan)
   144→
   145→    total = len(all_items)
   146→    if resolved_options.count is not None and resolved_options.count > 0:
   147→        all_items = all_items[: resolved_options.count]
   148→
   149→    if resolved_options.explain:
   150→        for item in all_items:
   151→            item["explain"] = item_explain(item)
   152→
   153→    return {
   154→        "items": all_items,
   155→        "total": total,
   156→        "grouped": group_queue_items(all_items, "item"),
   157→        "new_ids": new_ids,
   158→    }
   159→
   160→
   161→__all__ = [
   162→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   163→    "QueueBuildOptions",
   164→    "WorkQueueResult",
   165→    "build_work_queue",
   166→    "group_queue_items",
   167→]
   168→
```

```
1→"""Helper utilities for work queue item construction."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→from fnmatch import fnmatch
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core.enums import finding_status_tokens
     9→from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
    10→from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    11→    scorecard_subjective_entries,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.intelligence.integrity import (
    14→    is_holistic_subjective_finding,
    15→    unassessed_subjective_dimensions,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.scoring import DISPLAY_NAMES
    18→
    19→ALL_STATUSES = set(finding_status_tokens(include_all=True))
    20→ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY = {"auto_fix": 0, "refactor": 1, "manual_fix": 2, "reorganize": 3}
    21→ATTEST_EXAMPLE = (
    22→    "I have actually [DESCRIBE THE CONCRETE CHANGE YOU MADE] "
    23→    "and I am not gaming the score by resolving without fixing."
    24→)
    25→
    26→
    27→def detail_dict(item: dict) -> dict:
    28→    """Return finding detail as a dict; tolerate legacy/non-dict payloads."""
    29→    detail = item.get("detail")
    30→    return detail if isinstance(detail, dict) else {}
    31→
    32→
    33→def status_matches(item_status: str, status_filter: str) -> bool:
    34→    return status_filter == "all" or item_status == status_filter
    35→
    36→
    37→def is_subjective_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    38→    detector = item.get("detector")
    39→    if detector in {"subjective_assessment"}:
    40→        return True
    41→    if detector == "holistic_review":
    42→        return True
    43→    return False
    44→
    45→
    46→def is_review_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    47→    return item.get("detector") == "review"
    48→
    49→
    50→def is_subjective_queue_item(item: dict) -> bool:
    51→    """True for subjective work items, including collapsed subjective clusters."""
    52→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
    53→        return True
    54→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
    55→        members = item.get("members", [])
    56→        return bool(members) and all(
    57→            m.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" for m in members
    58→        )
    59→    return False
    60→
    61→
    62→def review_finding_weight(item: dict) -> float:
    63→    """Return review issue weight aligned with issues list ordering."""
    64→    confidence = str(item.get("confidence", "low")).lower()
    65→    weight_by_confidence = {
    66→        "high": 1.0,
    67→        "medium": 0.7,
    68→        "low": 0.3,
    69→    }
    70→    weight = weight_by_confidence.get(confidence, 0.3)
    71→    if detail_dict(item).get("holistic"):
    72→        weight *= 10.0
    73→    return float(weight)
    74→
    75→
    76→def scope_matches(item: dict, scope: str | None) -> bool:
    77→    """Apply show-style pattern matching against a queue item."""
    78→    if not scope:
    79→        return True
    80→
    81→    item_id = item.get("id", "")
    82→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
    83→    filepath = item.get("file", "")
    84→    summary = item.get("summary", "")
    85→    dimension = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
    86→    kind = item.get("kind", "")
    87→
    88→    if "*" in scope:
    89→        return any(
    90→            fnmatch(candidate, scope)
    91→            for candidate in (item_id, filepath, detector, dimension, summary)
    92→        )
    93→
    94→    if "::" in scope:
    95→        return item_id.startswith(scope)
    96→
    97→    lowered = scope.lower()
    98→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
    99→        return (
   100→            lowered in item_id.lower()
   101→            or lowered in dimension.lower()
   102→            or lowered in summary.lower()
   103→        )
   104→
   105→    # Hash suffix: 8+ hex chars matches the tail segment of a finding ID.
   106→    if len(lowered) >= 8 and re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]+", lowered):
   107→        return item_id.lower().endswith("::" + lowered)
   108→
   109→    return (
   110→        detector == scope
   111→        or filepath == scope
   112→        or filepath.startswith(scope.rstrip("/") + "/")
   113→    )
   114→
   115→
   116→def slugify(text: str) -> str:
   117→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9_]+", "_", text.lower()).strip("_")
   118→
   119→
   120→def _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(display_name: str) -> str:
   121→    """Map a display label (e.g. 'Mid elegance') to its canonical dimension key."""
   122→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   123→    target = cleaned.lower()
   124→
   125→    for dim_key, label in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
   126→        if str(label).lower() == target:
   127→            return str(dim_key)
   128→    return slugify(cleaned)
   129→
   130→
   131→def _subjective_dimension_aliases(display_name: str) -> set[str]:
   132→    """Return normalized aliases used to match display labels with finding dimension keys."""
   133→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   134→    canonical = _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(cleaned)
   135→    return {
   136→        cleaned.lower(),
   137→        cleaned.replace(" ", "_").lower(),
   138→        slugify(cleaned),
   139→        canonical.lower(),
   140→        slugify(canonical),
   141→    }
   142→
   143→
   144→def supported_fixers_for_item(state: dict, item: dict) -> set[str] | None:
   145→    """Return supported fixers for an item's language when known."""
   146→    lang = str(item.get("lang", "") or "").strip()
   147→    if not lang:
   148→        return None
   149→
   150→    caps = state.get("lang_capabilities", {})
   151→    if not isinstance(caps, dict):
   152→        return None
   153→
   154→    lang_caps = caps.get(lang, {})
   155→    if not isinstance(lang_caps, dict):
   156→        return None
   157→
   158→    fixers = lang_caps.get("fixers")
   159→    if not isinstance(fixers, list):
   160→        return None
   161→    return {fixer for fixer in fixers if isinstance(fixer, str)}
   162→
   163→
   164→def primary_command_for_finding(
   165→    item: dict, *, supported_fixers: set[str] | None = None
   166→) -> str:
   167→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
   168→    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   169→    if meta and meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and meta.fixers:
   170→        available_fixers = [
   171→            fixer
   172→            for fixer in meta.fixers
   173→            if supported_fixers is not None and fixer in supported_fixers
   174→        ]
   175→        if available_fixers:
   176→            return f"desloppify fix {available_fixers[0]} --dry-run"
   177→    if detector == "subjective_review":
   178→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   179→            return "desloppify review --prepare"
   180→        return "desloppify show subjective"
   181→    return f'desloppify plan done "{item.get("id", "")}" --note "<what you did>" --confirm'
   182→
   183→
   184→def build_triage_stage_items(plan: dict, state: dict) -> list[dict]:
   185→    """Build synthetic work items for each ``triage::*`` stage ID in the queue.
   186→
   187→    Returns an empty list when no triage stages are pending.
   188→    """
   189→    from desloppify.app.commands.plan.triage_playbook import (
   190→        TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES,
   191→        TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS,
   192→    )
   193→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (
   194→        TRIAGE_IDS,
   195→        TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS,
   196→    )
   197→
   198→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   199→    order_set = set(order)
   200→    present = order_set & TRIAGE_IDS
   201→    if not present:
   202→        return []
   203→
   204→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   205→    confirmed = set(meta.get("triage_stages", {}).keys())
   206→
   207→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   208→    open_review_count = sum(
   209→        1 for f in findings.values()
   210→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   211→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   212→    )
   213→
   214→    label_map = dict(TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS)
   215→    stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "commit")
   216→
   217→    items: list[dict] = []
   218→    for idx, (sid, name) in enumerate(zip(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS, stage_names)):
   219→        if sid not in present:
   220→            continue
   221→        if name in confirmed:
   222→            continue
   223→
   224→        # Compute blocked_by: dependency stages that are still in the queue
   225→        deps = TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES.get(name, set())
   226→        blocked_by = sorted(
   227→            f"triage::{dep}" for dep in deps
   228→            if f"triage::{dep}" in present and dep not in confirmed
   229→        )
   230→
   231→        cmd = f"desloppify plan triage --stage {name}"
   232→        if name == "commit":
   233→            cmd = 'desloppify plan triage --complete --strategy "..."'
   234→
   235→        items.append({
   236→            "id": sid,
   237→            "tier": 1,
   238→            "confidence": "high",
   239→            "detector": "triage",
   240→            "file": ".",
   241→            "kind": "workflow_stage",
   242→            "stage_name": name,
   243→            "stage_index": idx,
   244→            "summary": f"Triage: {label_map.get(name, name)}",
   245→            "detail": {
   246→                "total_review_findings": open_review_count,
   247→                "stage": name,
   248→                "stage_label": label_map.get(name, name),
   249→            },
   250→            "primary_command": cmd,
   251→            "blocked_by": blocked_by,
   252→            "is_blocked": bool(blocked_by),
   253→        })
   254→    return items
   255→
   256→
   257→def build_create_plan_item(plan: dict) -> dict | None:
   258→    """Build a synthetic work item for ``workflow::create-plan`` if it's in the queue.
   259→
   260→    Returns ``None`` when the item is not pending.
   261→    """
   262→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID
   263→
   264→    if WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   265→        return None
   266→
   267→    return {
   268→        "id": WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID,
   269→        "tier": 1,
   270→        "confidence": "high",
   271→        "detector": "workflow",
   272→        "file": ".",
   273→        "kind": "workflow_action",
   274→        "summary": "Create prioritized plan from review results",
   275→        "detail": {},
   276→        "primary_command": "desloppify plan",
   277→        "blocked_by": [],
   278→        "is_blocked": False,
   279→    }
   280→
   281→
   282→def subjective_strict_scores(state: dict) -> dict[str, float]:
   283→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   284→    if not dim_scores:
   285→        return {}
   286→
   287→    entries = scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   288→    scores: dict[str, float] = {}
   289→    for entry in entries:
   290→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   291→        if not name:
   292→            continue
   293→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   294→        [REDACTED](name)
   295→        aliases = _subjective_dimension_aliases(name)
   296→        for cli_key in entry.get("cli_keys", []):
   297→            key = str(cli_key).strip().lower()
   298→            if not key:
   299→                continue
   300→            aliases.add(key)
   301→            aliases.add(slugify(key))
   302→        aliases.add(dim_key.lower())
   303→        aliases.add(slugify(dim_key))
   304→        for alias in aliases:
   305→            scores[alias] = strict_val
   306→    return scores
   307→
   308→
   309→def _unassessed_entries_from_dim_scores(dim_scores: dict) -> list[dict]:
   310→    """Build scorecard-like entries for unassessed placeholder dimensions.
   311→
   312→    ``scorecard_subjective_entries`` goes through the scorecard pipeline which
   313→    intentionally hides placeholders from display.  This function reads
   314→    ``dimension_scores`` directly so initial-review items are never lost.
   315→    """
   316→    entries: list[dict] = []
   317→    for name, data in dim_scores.items():
   318→        if not isinstance(data, dict):
   319→            continue
   320→        detectors = data.get("detectors", {})
   321→        meta = detectors.get("subjective_assessment")
   322→        if not isinstance(meta, dict):
   323→            continue
   324→        if not meta.get("placeholder"):
   325→            continue
   326→        dim_key = meta.get("dimension_key", "")
   327→        entries.append(
   328→            {
   329→                "name": name,
   330→                "score": float(data.get("score", 0.0)),
   331→                "strict": float(data.get("strict", 0.0)),
   332→                "checks": int(data.get("checks", 0) or 0),
   333→                "issues": int(data.get("issues", 0) or 0),
   334→                "tier": int(data.get("tier", 4) or 4),
   335→                "placeholder": True,
   336→                "stale": False,
   337→                "dimension_key": dim_key,
   338→                "cli_keys": [dim_key] if dim_key else [],
   339→            }
   340→        )
   341→    return entries
   342→
   343→
   344→def build_subjective_items(
   345→    state: dict, findings: dict, *, threshold: float = 100.0
   346→) -> list[dict]:
   347→    """Create synthetic subjective work items."""
   348→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   349→    if not dim_scores:
   350→        return []
   351→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, float(threshold)))
   352→
   353→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   354→
   355→    # The scorecard pipeline hides unassessed placeholders from display.
   356→    # Merge them in so initial-review items always appear in the queue.
   357→    seen_dims: set[str] = {
   358→        str(e.get("dimension_key", "")).lower()
   359→        for e in subjective_entries
   360→        if e.get("dimension_key")
   361→    }
   362→    for entry in _unassessed_entries_from_dim_scores(dim_scores):
   363→        dk = str(entry.get("dimension_key", "")).lower()
   364→        if dk and dk not in seen_dims:
   365→            subjective_entries.append(entry)
   366→            seen_dims.add(dk)
   367→
   368→    if not subjective_entries:
   369→        return []
   370→    unassessed_dims = {
   371→        str(name).strip()
   372→        for name in unassessed_subjective_dimensions(
   373→            dim_scores
   374→        )
   375→    }
   376→
   377→    # Review findings are keyed by raw dimension name (snake_case).
   378→    review_open_by_dim: dict[str, int] = {}
   379→    for finding in findings.values():
   380→        if finding.get("status") != "open" or finding.get("detector") != "review":
   381→            continue
   382→        dim_key = str(detail_dict(finding).get("dimension", "")).strip().lower()
   383→        if not dim_key:
   384→            continue
   385→        review_open_by_dim[dim_key] = review_open_by_dim.get(dim_key, 0) + 1
   386→
   387→    items: list[dict] = []
   388→    def _prepare_command(
   389→        cli_keys: list[str],
   390→        *,
   391→        force_review_rerun: bool = False,
   392→    ) -> str:
   393→        command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   394→        if cli_keys:
   395→            command += " --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   396→        if force_review_rerun:
   397→            command += " --force-review-rerun"
   398→        return command
   399→
   400→    for entry in subjective_entries:
   401→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   402→        if not name:
   403→            continue
   404→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   405→        if strict_val >= threshold:
   406→            continue
   407→
   408→        [REDACTED](name)
   409→        aliases = set(_subjective_dimension_aliases(name))
   410→        cli_keys = [
   411→            str(key).strip().lower()
   412→            for key in entry.get("cli_keys", [])
   413→            if str(key).strip()
   414→        ]
   415→        aliases.update(cli_keys)
   416→        aliases.update(slugify(key) for key in cli_keys)
   417→        open_review = sum(review_open_by_dim.get(alias, 0) for alias in aliases)
   418→        is_unassessed = bool(entry.get("placeholder")) or (
   419→            name in unassessed_dims
   420→            or (strict_val <= 0.0 and int(entry.get("issues", 0)) == 0)
   421→        )
   422→        is_stale = bool(entry.get("stale"))
   423→        # If review findings already exist for this dimension, triage/fix them
   424→        # before suggesting another review refresh pass.
   425→        if open_review > 0:
   426→            primary_command = "desloppify show review --status open"
   427→        else:
   428→            primary_command = _prepare_command(cli_keys)
   429→        stale_tag = " [stale — re-review]" if is_stale else ""
   430→        summary = f"Subjective dimension below target: {name} ({strict_val:.1f}%){stale_tag}"
   431→        items.append(
   432→            {
   433→                "id": f"subjective::{slugify(dim_key)}",
   434→                "detector": "subjective_assessment",
   435→                "file": ".",
   436→                "confidence": "medium",
   437→                "summary": summary,
   438→                "detail": {
   439→                    "dimension_name": name,
   440→                    "dimension": dim_key,
   441→                    "issues": int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
   442→                    "strict_score": strict_val,
   443→                    "open_review_findings": open_review,
   444→                    "cli_keys": cli_keys,
   445→                },
   446→                "status": "open",
   447→                "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   448→                "primary_command": primary_command,
   449→                "initial_review": is_unassessed,
   450→                "stale_review": is_stale and not is_unassessed,
   451→            }
   452→        )
   453→    return items
   454→
   455→
   456→__all__ = [
   457→    "ALL_STATUSES",
   458→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   459→    "build_create_plan_item",
   460→    "build_subjective_items",
   461→    "build_triage_stage_items",
   462→    "detail_dict",
   463→    "is_review_finding",
   464→    "is_subjective_finding",
```

```
    21→
    22→def enrich_with_impact(items: list[dict], dimension_scores: dict) -> None:
    23→    """Stamp ``estimated_impact`` on each item based on dimension-level headroom.
    24→
    25→    Impact = ``overall_per_point * headroom`` where headroom = ``100 - score``.
    26→    Items in dimensions with more score headroom sort first.
    27→    """
    28→    if not dimension_scores:
    29→        for item in items:
    30→            item["estimated_impact"] = 0.0
    31→        return
    32→
    33→    from desloppify.engine._scoring.results.health import compute_health_breakdown
    34→    from desloppify.engine._scoring.results.impact import get_dimension_for_detector
    35→
    36→    breakdown = compute_health_breakdown(dimension_scores)
    37→    entries = breakdown.get("entries", [])
    38→
    39→    # Build lookup: normalized dimension name -> {per_point, headroom}
    40→    dim_impact: dict[str, dict[str, float]] = {}
    41→    for entry in entries:
    42→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
    43→        if not name:
    44→            continue
    45→        per_point = float(entry.get("overall_per_point", 0.0))
    46→        score = float(entry.get("score", 0.0))
    47→        headroom = 100.0 - score
    48→        dim_impact[name.lower()] = {"per_point": per_point, "headroom": headroom}
    49→
    50→    for item in items:
    51→        impact = _compute_item_impact(item, dim_impact, get_dimension_for_detector)
    52→        item["estimated_impact"] = impact
    53→
    54→
    55→def _compute_item_impact(
    56→    item: dict,
    57→    dim_impact: dict[str, dict[str, float]],
    58→    get_dimension_for_detector,
    59→) -> float:
    60→    """Compute impact value for a single queue item."""
    61→    kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
    62→
    63→    # Subjective items (synthetic dimensions or subjective findings):
    64→    # look up by detail.dimension_name
    65→    if kind == "subjective_dimension" or item.get("is_subjective"):
    66→        dim_name = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
    67→        entry = dim_impact.get(dim_name.lower())
    68→        if entry:
    69→            return entry["per_point"] * entry["headroom"]
    70→        return 0.0
    71→
    72→    # Mechanical findings: use detector -> dimension mapping
    73→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
    74→    if detector:
    75→        dimension = get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
    76→        if dimension:
    77→            entry = dim_impact.get(dimension.name.lower())
    78→            if entry:
    79→                return entry["per_point"] * entry["headroom"]
    80→
    81→    return 0.0
    82→
    83→
    84→def subjective_score_value(item: dict) -> float:
    85→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
    86→        detail = detail_dict(item)
    87→        return float(detail.get("strict_score", item.get("subjective_score", 100.0)))
    88→    return float(item.get("subjective_score", 100.0))

    91→def build_finding_items(
    92→    state: dict,
    93→    *,
    94→    scan_path: str | None,
    95→    status_filter: str,
    96→    scope: str | None,
    97→    chronic: bool,
    98→) -> list[dict]:

   100→    subjective_scores = subjective_strict_scores(state)
   101→    out: list[dict] = []
   102→
   103→    for finding_id, finding in scoped.items():
   104→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
   105→            continue
   106→        if not status_matches(finding.get("status", "open"), status_filter):
   107→            continue
   108→        if chronic and not (
   109→            finding.get("status") == "open" and finding.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2
   110→        ):
   111→            continue
   112→
   113→        item = dict(finding)
   114→        item["id"] = finding_id
   115→        item["kind"] = "finding"
   116→        item["is_review"] = is_review_finding(item)
   117→        item["is_subjective"] = is_subjective_finding(item)
   118→        item["review_weight"] = (
   119→            review_finding_weight(item) if item["is_review"] else None
   120→        )
   121→        subjective_score = None
   122→        if item["is_subjective"]:
   123→            detail = detail_dict(finding)
   124→            dim_name = detail.get("dimension_name", "")
   125→            dim_key = detail.get("dimension", "") or slugify(dim_name)
   126→            subjective_score = subjective_scores.get(
   127→                dim_key, subjective_scores.get(dim_name.lower(), 100.0)
   128→            )
   129→        item["subjective_score"] = subjective_score
   130→        supported_fixers = supported_fixers_for_item(state, item)
   131→        item["primary_command"] = primary_command_for_finding(
   132→            item,
   133→            supported_fixers=supported_fixers,
   134→        )
   135→
   136→        if not scope_matches(item, scope):
   137→            continue
   138→        out.append(item)
   139→
   140→    return out
   141→
   142→
   143→def item_sort_key(item: dict) -> tuple:
   144→    kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   145→
   146→    # Initial-review subjective items: highest priority (tier -3)
   147→    if kind == "subjective_dimension" and item.get("initial_review"):
   148→        return (-3, 0, subjective_score_value(item), item.get("id", ""))
   149→
   150→    # Triage stage items: tier -2, stage order, blocked after unblocked
   151→    if kind == "workflow_stage":
   152→        blocked_penalty = 1 if item.get("is_blocked") else 0
   153→        stage_index = int(item.get("stage_index", 0))
   154→        return (-2, blocked_penalty, stage_index, item.get("id", ""))
   155→
   156→    # Workflow action items (e.g. create-plan): tier -1
   157→    if kind == "workflow_action":
   158→        return (-1, 0, 0, item.get("id", ""))
   159→
   160→    if kind == "cluster":
   161→        # Clusters sort before individual findings, ordered by action type
   162→        action_pri = ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY.get(
   163→            item.get("action_type", "manual_fix"), 3
   164→        )
   165→        return (
   166→            0,
   167→            action_pri,
   168→            -int(item.get("member_count", 0)),
   169→            item.get("id", ""),
   170→        )
   171→
   172→    impact = item.get("estimated_impact", 0.0)
   173→
   174→    if kind == "subjective_dimension" or item.get("is_subjective"):
   175→        return (
   176→            1,
   177→            -impact,
   178→            subjective_score_value(item),
   179→            item.get("id", ""),
   180→        )
   181→
   182→    detail = detail_dict(item)
   183→    review_weight = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
   184→    return (
   185→        1,
   186→        -impact,
   187→        CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(item.get("confidence", "low"), 9),
   188→        -review_weight,
   189→        -int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   190→        item.get("id", ""),
   191→    )
   192→
   193→
   194→def item_explain(item: dict) -> dict:
   195→    kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   196→    if kind == "workflow_stage":
   197→        return {
   198→            "kind": "workflow_stage",
   199→            "stage": item.get("stage_name"),
   200→            "is_blocked": item.get("is_blocked", False),
   201→            "blocked_by": item.get("blocked_by", []),
   202→            "policy": "Triage stages sort by dependency order; blocked stages follow unblocked.",
   203→            "ranking_factors": ["blocked_penalty asc", "stage_index asc"],
   204→        }
   205→
   206→    if kind == "workflow_action":
   207→        return {
   208→            "kind": "workflow_action",
   209→            "policy": "Workflow items sort after triage stages, before findings.",
   210→            "ranking_factors": ["id asc"],
   211→        }
   212→
   213→    if kind == "cluster":
   214→        return {
   215→            "kind": "cluster",
   216→            "estimated_impact": item.get("estimated_impact", 0.0),
   217→            "action_type": item.get("action_type", "manual_fix"),
   218→            "member_count": item.get("member_count", 0),
   219→            "policy": "Clusters sort before individual findings, ordered by action type then size.",
   220→            "ranking_factors": ["action_type asc", "member_count desc", "id asc"],
   221→        }
   222→
   223→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
   224→        initial = item.get("initial_review", False)
   225→        return {
   226→            "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   227→            "estimated_impact": item.get("estimated_impact", 0.0),
   228→            "subjective_score": subjective_score_value(item),
   229→            "initial_review": initial,
   230→            "policy": (
   231→                "Initial review items sort first (onboarding priority)."
   232→                if initial else
   233→                "Sorted by dimension impact (score headroom × weight), then subjective score."
   234→            ),
   235→            "ranking_factors": ["estimated_impact desc", "subjective_score asc", "id asc"],
   236→        }
   237→
   238→    detail = detail_dict(item)
   239→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "low")
   240→    is_subjective = bool(item.get("is_subjective"))
   241→    is_review = bool(item.get("is_review"))
   242→    ranking_factors: list[str]
   243→    if is_subjective:
   244→        ranking_factors = ["estimated_impact desc", "subjective_score asc", "id asc"]
   245→    elif is_review:
   246→        ranking_factors = [
   247→            "estimated_impact desc",
   248→            "confidence asc",
   249→            "review_weight desc",
   250→            "count desc",
   251→            "id asc",
   252→        ]
   253→    else:
   254→        ranking_factors = ["estimated_impact desc", "confidence asc", "count desc", "id asc"]
   255→    explain = {
   256→        "kind": "finding",
   257→        "estimated_impact": item.get("estimated_impact", 0.0),
   258→        "confidence": confidence,
   259→        "confidence_rank": CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(confidence, 9),
   260→        "count": int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   261→        "id": item.get("id", ""),
   262→        "ranking_factors": ranking_factors,
   263→    }
   264→    if is_review:
   265→        explain["review_weight"] = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
   266→    if is_subjective:
   267→        explain["policy"] = (
   268→            "Sorted by dimension impact (score headroom × weight), then subjective score."
   269→        )
   270→        explain["subjective_score"] = subjective_score_value(item)
   271→    return explain
   272→
   273→
   274→def group_queue_items(items: list[dict], group: str) -> dict[str, list[dict]]:
   275→    """Group queue items for alternate output modes."""
   276→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   277→    for item in items:
   278→        if group == "file":
   279→            key = item.get("file", "")
   280→        elif group == "detector":
   281→            key = item.get("detector", "")
   282→        elif group == "cluster":
   283→            plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   284→            key = plan_cluster["name"] if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) else "(unclustered)"
   285→        else:
   286→            key = "items"
   287→        grouped.setdefault(key, []).append(item)
   288→    return grouped
   289→
   290→
   291→__all__ = [
   292→    "build_finding_items",
   293→    "enrich_with_impact",
   294→    "item_explain",
   295→    "item_sort_key",
   296→    "subjective_score_value",
   297→    "group_queue_items",
   298→]
   299→
```

Search the desloppify codebase for ALL "preflight" and "postflight" (or "pre_flight", "post_flight", "pre-flight", "post-flight") patterns. The user suspects these relate to the same subjective gating policy issue.

- Multiple places computing the same thing differently
- Inconsistent naming (stale vs refresh vs needs_review_refresh)

- Dead code or unused parameters

```
1→"""Preflight guards for subjective review reruns.
     2→
     3→Blocks reruns while backlog remains (objective findings and/or subjective queue
     4→work), unless ``--force-review-rerun`` is set. Also clears stale subjective
     5→markers before a new cycle.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→from __future__ import annotations
     9→
    10→import re
    11→import sys
    12→
    13→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    14→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.core import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
    15→from desloppify.state import StateModel, save_state
    16→
    17→from .helpers import parse_dimensions
    18→
    19→
    20→def clear_stale_subjective_entries(
    21→    state: StateModel,
    22→    *,
    23→    dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
    24→) -> list[str]:
    25→    """Clear ``needs_review_refresh`` markers from subjective assessments.
    26→
    27→    When *dimensions* is provided, only those dimensions are cleared;
    28→    otherwise all stale dimensions are cleared.
    29→
    30→    Returns the list of dimension keys that were cleared.
    31→    """
    32→    assessments: dict = state.get("subjective_assessments", {})
    33→    cleared: list[str] = []
    34→    for dim_key, assessment in assessments.items():
    35→        if not isinstance(assessment, dict):
    36→            continue
    37→        if dimensions is not None and dim_key not in dimensions:
    38→            continue
    39→        if assessment.get("needs_review_refresh"):
    40→            assessment.pop("needs_review_refresh", None)
    41→            assessment.pop("stale_since", None)
    42→            assessment.pop("refresh_reason", None)
    43→            cleared.append(dim_key)
    44→    return cleared
    45→
    46→
    47→def _scored_dimensions(state: StateModel) -> list[str]:
    48→    """Return dimension keys that already have a nonzero subjective score."""
    49→    assessments: dict = state.get("subjective_assessments", {})
    50→    scored: list[str] = []
    51→    for dim_key, assessment in assessments.items():
    52→        if isinstance(assessment, dict):
    53→            if assessment.get("score", 0):
    54→                scored.append(dim_key)
    55→        elif isinstance(assessment, (int, float)) and assessment:
    56→            scored.append(dim_key)
    57→    return sorted(scored)
    58→
    59→
    60→def _normalize_dimension_key(raw: object) -> str:
    61→    """Normalize a dimension key/name to canonical snake_case."""
    62→    text = str(raw or "").strip().lower().replace(" ", "_")
    63→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9_]+", "_", text).strip("_")
    64→
    65→
    66→def _count_open_subjective_queue_items(
    67→    state: StateModel,
    68→    *,
    69→    dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
    70→) -> int:
    71→    """Count open synthetic subjective queue items for rerun gating."""
    72→    normalized_dims = (
    73→        {_normalize_dimension_key(dim) for dim in dimensions}
    74→        if dimensions is not None
    75→        else None
    76→    )
    77→    queue = build_work_queue(
    78→        state,
    79→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
    80→            status="open",
    81→            include_subjective=True,
    82→            count=None,
    83→        ),
    84→    )
    85→    count = 0
    86→    for item in queue.get("items", []):
    87→        if item.get("kind") != "subjective_dimension":
    88→            continue
    89→        # Stale reruns are not blocking backlog — they ARE the work
    90→        if item.get("stale_review"):
    91→            continue
    92→        if normalized_dims is None:
    93→            count += 1
    94→            continue
    95→        detail = item.get("detail")
    96→        raw_dim = detail.get("dimension", "") if isinstance(detail, dict) else ""
    97→        item_dim = _normalize_dimension_key(raw_dim)
    98→        if item_dim and item_dim in normalized_dims:
    99→            count += 1
   100→    return count
   101→
   102→
   103→def _blocking_scored_dimensions(
   104→    state: StateModel,
   105→    *,
   106→    dimensions: set[str] | None,
   107→    normalized_dimensions: set[str] | None,
   108→) -> list[str]:
   109→    scored_dims = _scored_dimensions(state)
   110→    if dimensions is None:
   111→        return scored_dims
   112→    normalized = normalized_dimensions or set()
   113→    return sorted(
   114→        dim
   115→        for dim in scored_dims
   116→        if _normalize_dimension_key(dim) in normalized
   117→    )
   118→
   119→
   120→def _objective_and_subjective_backlog(
   121→    state: StateModel,
   122→    *,
   123→    blocking_dims: list[str],
   124→) -> tuple[int, int]:
   125→    objective_result = build_work_queue(
   126→        state,
   127→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   128→            status="open",
   129→            include_subjective=False,
   130→            count=None,
   131→        ),
   132→    )
   133→    objective_total = objective_result["total"]
   134→    subjective_total = _count_open_subjective_queue_items(
   135→        state,
   136→        dimensions=set(blocking_dims),
   137→    )
   138→    return objective_total, subjective_total
   139→
   140→
   141→def _print_backlog_blocked_message(
   142→    *,
   143→    blocking_dims: list[str],
   144→    objective_total: int,
   145→    subjective_total: int,
   146→    dimensions: set[str] | None,
   147→) -> None:
   148→    print(
   149→        colorize(
   150→            "  Blocked: rerun requires drained backlog "
   151→            f"(objective: {objective_total}, subjective: {subjective_total}).",
   152→            "red",
   153→        ),
   154→        file=sys.stderr,
   155→    )
   156→    print(
   157→        colorize(
   158→            f"  Scored dimensions: {', '.join(blocking_dims)}",
   159→            "yellow",
   160→        ),
   161→        file=sys.stderr,
   162→    )
   163→    if objective_total > 0:
   164→        print(
   165→            colorize(
   166→                f"  Open objective finding(s): {objective_total}",
   167→                "yellow",
   168→            ),
   169→            file=sys.stderr,
   170→        )
   171→    if subjective_total > 0:
   172→        print(
   173→            colorize(
   174→                f"  Open subjective queue item(s): {subjective_total}",
   175→                "yellow",
   176→            ),
   177→            file=sys.stderr,
   178→        )
   179→    print("", file=sys.stderr)
   180→    if dimensions is not None:
   181→        unscored = sorted(dimensions - set(blocking_dims))
   182→        if unscored:
   183→            print(
   184→                colorize(
   185→                    f"  Tip: target only unscored dimensions with "
   186→                    f"--dimensions {','.join(unscored)}",
   187→                    "dim",
   188→                ),
   189→                file=sys.stderr,
   190→            )
   191→    print(
   192→        colorize(
   193→            "  Resolve open items first, or override with --force-review-rerun",
   194→            "dim",
   195→        ),
   196→        file=sys.stderr,
   197→    )
   198→
   199→
   200→def review_rerun_preflight(
   201→    state: StateModel,
   202→    args,
   203→    *,
   204→    state_file=None,
   205→    save_fn=save_state,
   206→) -> None:
   207→    """Single entry point: gate check -> clear stale -> save.
   208→
   209→    Exits with code 1 when open backlog exists and ``--force-review-rerun`` is
   210→    not set. On success, clears stale subjective markers for targeted
   211→    dimensions and persists state.
   212→    """
   213→    dimensions = parse_dimensions(args)
   214→    normalized_dimensions = (
   215→        {_normalize_dimension_key(dim) for dim in dimensions}
   216→        if dimensions is not None
   217→        else None
   218→    )
   219→
   220→    # --force-review-rerun bypasses the gate
   221→    if getattr(args, "force_review_rerun", False):
   222→        print(
   223→            colorize(
   224→                "  --force-review-rerun: bypassing rerun backlog checks.",
   225→                "yellow",
   226→            )
   227→        )
   228→    else:
   229→        # Only gate dimensions that are actually targeted by this review run.
   230→        blocking_dims = _blocking_scored_dimensions(
   231→            state,
   232→            dimensions=dimensions,
   233→            normalized_dimensions=normalized_dimensions,
   234→        )
   235→
   236→        # No gate when none of the targeted dimensions have prior scores —
   237→        # this is a first run for these dimensions, not a rerun.
   238→        if blocking_dims:
   239→            objective_total, subjective_total = _objective_and_subjective_backlog(
   240→                state,
   241→                blocking_dims=blocking_dims,
   242→            )
   243→            if objective_total > 0 or subjective_total > 0:
   244→                _print_backlog_blocked_message(
   245→                    blocking_dims=blocking_dims,
   246→                    objective_total=objective_total,
   247→                    subjective_total=subjective_total,
   248→                    dimensions=dimensions,
   249→                )
   250→                sys.exit(1)
   251→
   252→    # Gate passed — clear stale for targeted dims
   253→    cleared = clear_stale_subjective_entries(state, dimensions=dimensions)
   254→    if cleared and state_file:
   255→        save_fn(state, state_file)
   256→        print(
   257→            colorize(
   258→                f"  Cleared stale review markers: {', '.join(sorted(cleared))}",
   259→                "cyan",
   260→            )
   261→        )
   262→
```

```
1→"""Scan preflight guard: warn and gate scan when queue has unfinished items."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→import sys
     7→
     8→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import plan_aware_queue_count
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import state_path
    11→from desloppify.core.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
    12→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    13→from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan, save_plan
    14→
    15→
    16→def scan_queue_preflight(args) -> None:
    17→    """Warn and gate scan when queue has unfinished items."""
    18→    # CI profile always passes
    19→    if getattr(args, "profile", None) == "ci":
    20→        return
    21→
    22→    # --force-rescan with valid attestation bypasses
    23→    if getattr(args, "force_rescan", False):
    24→        attest = getattr(args, "attest", None) or ""
    25→        if "i understand" not in attest.lower():
    26→            print(
    27→                colorize(
    28→                    '  --force-rescan requires --attest "I understand this is not '
    29→                    "the intended workflow and I am intentionally skipping queue "
    30→                    'completion"',
    31→                    "red",
    32→                ),
    33→                file=sys.stderr,
    34→            )
    35→            sys.exit(1)
    36→        print(
    37→            colorize(
    38→                "  --force-rescan: bypassing queue completion check. "
    39→                "Plan-start score will be reset.",
    40→                "yellow",
    41→            )
    42→        )
    43→        # Clear plan_start_scores
    44→        try:
    45→            plan = load_plan()
    46→            if plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
    47→                plan["plan_start_scores"] = {}
    48→                save_plan(plan)
    49→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
    50→            logging.debug("Plan score cleanup skipped: %s", exc)
    51→        return
    52→
    53→    # No plan = no gate (first scan, or user never uses plan)
    54→    try:
    55→        plan = load_plan()
    56→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
    57→        return
    58→    if not plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
    59→        return  # No active cycle
    60→
    61→    # Count plan-aware remaining items
    62→    try:
    63→        state = state_mod.load_state(state_path(args))
    64→        remaining = plan_aware_queue_count(state, plan)
    65→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
    66→        return
    67→    if remaining == 0:
    68→        return  # Queue clear, scan allowed
    69→
    70→    # GATE
    71→    print(
    72→        colorize(
    73→            f"\n  WARNING: {remaining} item{'s' if remaining != 1 else ''}"
    74→            " remaining in your queue.",
    75→            "red",
    76→        ),
    77→        file=sys.stderr,
    78→    )
    79→    print(
    80→        colorize(
    81→            "  The intended workflow is to complete the queue before scanning.",
    82→            "red",
    83→        ),
    84→        file=sys.stderr,
    85→    )
    86→    print(
    87→        colorize(
    88→            "  Work through items with `desloppify next`, then scan when clear.",
    89→            "dim",
    90→        ),
    91→        file=sys.stderr,
    92→    )
    93→    print(
    94→        colorize(
    95→            "\n  To force a rescan (resets your plan-start score):",
    96→            "dim",
    97→        ),
    98→        file=sys.stderr,
    99→    )
   100→    print(
   101→        colorize(
   102→            '    desloppify scan --force-rescan --attest "I understand this is not '
   103→            "the intended workflow and I am intentionally skipping queue "
   104→            'completion"',
   105→            "yellow",
   106→        ),
   107→        file=sys.stderr,
   108→    )
   109→    sys.exit(1)
   110→
   111→
   112→__all__ = ["scan_queue_preflight"]
   113→
```

```
1→"""Queue-policy helpers shared by planning render/select modules."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from typing import Any
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import (
     9→    QueueBuildOptions,
    10→    WorkQueueResult,
    11→    build_work_queue,
    12→)
    13→
    14→
    15→def _subjective_threshold(state: dict[str, Any], *, default: float = 95.0) -> float:
    16→    config = state.get("config", {})
    17→    raw_target = default
    18→    if isinstance(config, dict):
    19→        raw_target = config.get("target_strict_score", default)
    20→    try:
    21→        value = float(raw_target)
    22→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    23→        value = default
    24→    return max(0.0, min(100.0, value))
    25→
    26→
    27→@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
    28→class OpenPlanQueuePolicy:
    29→    count: int | None = None
    30→    scan_path: str | None = None
    31→    include_subjective: bool = True
    32→
    33→
    34→def build_open_plan_queue(
    35→    state: dict[str, Any],
    36→    policy: OpenPlanQueuePolicy | None = None,
    37→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    38→    """Build one open-status queue with consistent planning policy defaults."""
    39→    policy = policy or OpenPlanQueuePolicy()
    40→    resolved_scan_path: str | None
    41→    if isinstance(policy.scan_path, str):
    42→        resolved_scan_path = policy.scan_path
    43→    else:
    44→        raw_state_path = state.get("scan_path")
    45→        resolved_scan_path = raw_state_path if isinstance(raw_state_path, str) else None
    46→    return build_work_queue(
    47→        state,
    48→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
    49→            count=policy.count,
    50→            scan_path=resolved_scan_path,
    51→            status="open",
    52→            include_subjective=policy.include_subjective,
    53→            subjective_threshold=_subjective_threshold(state),
    54→        ),
    55→    )
    56→
    57→
    58→__all__ = ["OpenPlanQueuePolicy", "build_open_plan_queue"]
    59→
```
