```
1→"""Preflight guards for subjective review reruns.
     2→
     3→Blocks reruns while backlog remains (objective findings and/or subjective queue
     4→work), unless ``--force-review-rerun`` is set. Also clears stale subjective
     5→markers before a new cycle.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→from __future__ import annotations
     9→
    10→import re
    11→import sys
    12→
    13→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    14→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.core import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
    15→from desloppify.state import StateModel, save_state
    16→
    17→from .helpers import parse_dimensions
    18→
    19→
    20→def clear_stale_subjective_entries(
    21→    state: StateModel,
    22→    *,
    23→    dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
    24→) -> list[str]:
    25→    """Clear ``needs_review_refresh`` markers from subjective assessments.
    26→
    27→    When *dimensions* is provided, only those dimensions are cleared;
    28→    otherwise all stale dimensions are cleared.
    29→
    30→    Returns the list of dimension keys that were cleared.
    31→    """
    32→    assessments: dict = state.get("subjective_assessments", {})
    33→    cleared: list[str] = []
    34→    for dim_key, assessment in assessments.items():
    35→        if not isinstance(assessment, dict):
    36→            continue
    37→        if dimensions is not None and dim_key not in dimensions:
    38→            continue
    39→        if assessment.get("needs_review_refresh"):
    40→            assessment.pop("needs_review_refresh", None)
    41→            assessment.pop("stale_since", None)
    42→            assessment.pop("refresh_reason", None)
    43→            cleared.append(dim_key)
    44→    return cleared
    45→
    46→
    47→def _scored_dimensions(state: StateModel) -> list[str]:
    48→    """Return dimension keys that already have a nonzero subjective score."""
    49→    assessments: dict = state.get("subjective_assessments", {})
    50→    scored: list[str] = []
    51→    for dim_key, assessment in assessments.items():
    52→        if isinstance(assessment, dict):
    53→            if assessment.get("score", 0):
    54→                scored.append(dim_key)
    55→        elif isinstance(assessment, (int, float)) and assessment:
    56→            scored.append(dim_key)
    57→    return sorted(scored)
    58→
    59→
    60→def _normalize_dimension_key(raw: object) -> str:
    61→    """Normalize a dimension key/name to canonical snake_case."""
    62→    text = str(raw or "").strip().lower().replace(" ", "_")
    63→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9_]+", "_", text).strip("_")
    64→
    65→
    66→def _count_open_subjective_queue_items(
    67→    state: StateModel,
    68→    *,
    69→    dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
    70→) -> int:
    71→    """Count open synthetic subjective queue items for rerun gating."""
    72→    normalized_dims = (
    73→        {_normalize_dimension_key(dim) for dim in dimensions}
    74→        if dimensions is not None
    75→        else None
    76→    )
    77→    queue = build_work_queue(
    78→        state,
    79→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
    80→            status="open",
    81→            include_subjective=True,
    82→            count=None,
    83→        ),
    84→    )
    85→    count = 0
    86→    for item in queue.get("items", []):
    87→        if item.get("kind") != "subjective_dimension":
    88→            continue
    89→        # Stale reruns are not blocking backlog — they ARE the work
    90→        if item.get("stale_review"):
    91→            continue
    92→        if normalized_dims is None:
    93→            count += 1
    94→            continue
    95→        detail = item.get("detail")
    96→        raw_dim = detail.get("dimension", "") if isinstance(detail, dict) else ""
    97→        item_dim = _normalize_dimension_key(raw_dim)
    98→        if item_dim and item_dim in normalized_dims:
    99→            count += 1
   100→    return count
   101→
   102→
   103→def _blocking_scored_dimensions(
   104→    state: StateModel,
   105→    *,
   106→    dimensions: set[str] | None,
   107→    normalized_dimensions: set[str] | None,
   108→) -> list[str]:
   109→    scored_dims = _scored_dimensions(state)
   110→    if dimensions is None:
   111→        return scored_dims
   112→    normalized = normalized_dimensions or set()
   113→    return sorted(
   114→        dim
   115→        for dim in scored_dims
   116→        if _normalize_dimension_key(dim) in normalized
   117→    )
   118→
   119→
   120→def _objective_and_subjective_backlog(
   121→    state: StateModel,
   122→    *,
   123→    blocking_dims: list[str],
   124→) -> tuple[int, int]:
   125→    objective_result = build_work_queue(
   126→        state,
   127→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   128→            status="open",
   129→            include_subjective=False,
   130→            count=None,
   131→        ),
   132→    )
   133→    objective_total = objective_result["total"]
   134→    subjective_total = _count_open_subjective_queue_items(
   135→        state,
   136→        dimensions=set(blocking_dims),
   137→    )
   138→    return objective_total, subjective_total
   139→
   140→
   141→def _print_backlog_blocked_message(
   142→    *,
   143→    blocking_dims: list[str],
   144→    objective_total: int,
   145→    subjective_total: int,
   146→    dimensions: set[str] | None,
   147→) -> None:
   148→    print(
   149→        colorize(
   150→            "  Blocked: rerun requires drained backlog "
   151→            f"(objective: {objective_total}, subjective: {subjective_total}).",
   152→            "red",
   153→        ),
   154→        file=sys.stderr,
   155→    )
   156→    print(
   157→        colorize(
   158→            f"  Scored dimensions: {', '.join(blocking_dims)}",
   159→            "yellow",
   160→        ),
   161→        file=sys.stderr,
   162→    )
   163→    if objective_total > 0:
   164→        print(
   165→            colorize(
   166→                f"  Open objective finding(s): {objective_total}",
   167→                "yellow",
   168→            ),
   169→            file=sys.stderr,
   170→        )
   171→    if subjective_total > 0:
   172→        print(
   173→            colorize(
   174→                f"  Open subjective queue item(s): {subjective_total}",
   175→                "yellow",
   176→            ),
   177→            file=sys.stderr,
   178→        )
   179→    print("", file=sys.stderr)
   180→    if dimensions is not None:
   181→        unscored = sorted(dimensions - set(blocking_dims))
   182→        if unscored:
   183→            print(
   184→                colorize(
   185→                    f"  Tip: target only unscored dimensions with "
   186→                    f"--dimensions {','.join(unscored)}",
   187→                    "dim",
   188→                ),
   189→                file=sys.stderr,
   190→            )
   191→    print(
   192→        colorize(
   193→            "  Resolve open items first, or override with --force-review-rerun",
   194→            "dim",
   195→        ),
   196→        file=sys.stderr,
   197→    )
   198→
   199→
   200→def review_rerun_preflight(
   201→    state: StateModel,
   202→    args,
   203→    *,
   204→    state_file=None,
   205→    save_fn=save_state,
   206→) -> None:
   207→    """Single entry point: gate check -> clear stale -> save.
   208→
   209→    Exits with code 1 when open backlog exists and ``--force-review-rerun`` is
   210→    not set. On success, clears stale subjective markers for targeted
   211→    dimensions and persists state.
   212→    """
   213→    dimensions = parse_dimensions(args)
   214→    normalized_dimensions = (
   215→        {_normalize_dimension_key(dim) for dim in dimensions}
   216→        if dimensions is not None
   217→        else None
   218→    )
   219→
   220→    # --force-review-rerun bypasses the gate
   221→    if getattr(args, "force_review_rerun", False):
   222→        print(
   223→            colorize(
   224→                "  --force-review-rerun: bypassing rerun backlog checks.",
   225→                "yellow",
   226→            )
   227→        )
   228→    else:
   229→        # Only gate dimensions that are actually targeted by this review run.
   230→        blocking_dims = _blocking_scored_dimensions(
   231→            state,
   232→            dimensions=dimensions,
   233→            normalized_dimensions=normalized_dimensions,
   234→        )
   235→
   236→        # No gate when none of the targeted dimensions have prior scores —
   237→        # this is a first run for these dimensions, not a rerun.
   238→        if blocking_dims:
   239→            objective_total, subjective_total = _objective_and_subjective_backlog(
   240→                state,
   241→                blocking_dims=blocking_dims,
   242→            )
   243→            if objective_total > 0 or subjective_total > 0:
   244→                _print_backlog_blocked_message(
   245→                    blocking_dims=blocking_dims,
   246→                    objective_total=objective_total,
   247→                    subjective_total=subjective_total,
   248→                    dimensions=dimensions,
   249→                )
   250→                sys.exit(1)
   251→
   252→    # Gate passed — clear stale for targeted dims
   253→    cleared = clear_stale_subjective_entries(state, dimensions=dimensions)
   254→    if cleared and state_file:
   255→        save_fn(state, state_file)
   256→        print(
   257→            colorize(
   258→                f"  Cleared stale review markers: {', '.join(sorted(cleared))}",
   259→                "cyan",
   260→            )
   261→        )
   262→
```

```
1→"""Scan preflight guard: warn and gate scan when queue has unfinished items."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→import sys
     7→
     8→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import plan_aware_queue_count
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import state_path
    11→from desloppify.core.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
    12→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    13→from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan, save_plan
    14→
    15→
    16→def scan_queue_preflight(args) -> None:
    17→    """Warn and gate scan when queue has unfinished items."""
    18→    # CI profile always passes
    19→    if getattr(args, "profile", None) == "ci":
    20→        return
    21→
    22→    # --force-rescan with valid attestation bypasses
    23→    if getattr(args, "force_rescan", False):
    24→        attest = getattr(args, "attest", None) or ""
    25→        if "i understand" not in attest.lower():
    26→            print(
    27→                colorize(
    28→                    '  --force-rescan requires --attest "I understand this is not '
    29→                    "the intended workflow and I am intentionally skipping queue "
    30→                    'completion"',
    31→                    "red",
    32→                ),
    33→                file=sys.stderr,
    34→            )
    35→            sys.exit(1)
    36→        print(
    37→            colorize(
    38→                "  --force-rescan: bypassing queue completion check. "
    39→                "Plan-start score will be reset.",
    40→                "yellow",
    41→            )
    42→        )
    43→        # Clear plan_start_scores
    44→        try:
    45→            plan = load_plan()
    46→            if plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
    47→                plan["plan_start_scores"] = {}
    48→                save_plan(plan)
    49→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
    50→            logging.debug("Plan score cleanup skipped: %s", exc)
    51→        return
    52→
    53→    # No plan = no gate (first scan, or user never uses plan)
    54→    try:
    55→        plan = load_plan()
    56→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
    57→        return
    58→    if not plan.get("plan_start_scores"):
    59→        return  # No active cycle
    60→
    61→    # Count plan-aware remaining items
    62→    try:
    63→        state = state_mod.load_state(state_path(args))
    64→        remaining = plan_aware_queue_count(state, plan)
    65→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
    66→        return
    67→    if remaining == 0:
    68→        return  # Queue clear, scan allowed
    69→
    70→    # GATE
    71→    print(
    72→        colorize(
    73→            f"\n  WARNING: {remaining} item{'s' if remaining != 1 else ''}"
    74→            " remaining in your queue.",
    75→            "red",
    76→        ),
    77→        file=sys.stderr,
    78→    )
    79→    print(
    80→        colorize(
    81→            "  The intended workflow is to complete the queue before scanning.",
    82→            "red",
    83→        ),
    84→        file=sys.stderr,
    85→    )
    86→    print(
    87→        colorize(
    88→            "  Work through items with `desloppify next`, then scan when clear.",
    89→            "dim",
    90→        ),
    91→        file=sys.stderr,
    92→    )
    93→    print(
    94→        colorize(
    95→            "\n  To force a rescan (resets your plan-start score):",
    96→            "dim",
    97→        ),
    98→        file=sys.stderr,
    99→    )
   100→    print(
   101→        colorize(
   102→            '    desloppify scan --force-rescan --attest "I understand this is not '
   103→            "the intended workflow and I am intentionally skipping queue "
   104→            'completion"',
   105→            "yellow",
   106→        ),
   107→        file=sys.stderr,
   108→    )
   109→    sys.exit(1)
   110→
   111→
   112→__all__ = ["scan_queue_preflight"]
   113→
```

```
1→"""Shared triage guardrail helpers for command entrypoints."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import sys
     6→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     7→
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.display import short_finding_id
     9→from desloppify.core.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
    10→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    11→from desloppify.engine.plan import (
    12→    compute_new_finding_ids,
    13→    is_triage_stale,
    14→    load_plan,
    15→    triage_phase_banner,
    16→)
    17→
    18→
    19→@dataclass
    20→class TriageGuardrailResult:
    21→    """Structured result from triage staleness detection."""
    22→
    23→    is_stale: bool = False
    24→    new_ids: set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
    25→    _plan: dict | None = field(default=None, repr=False)
    26→
    27→
    28→def triage_guardrail_status(
    29→    *,
    30→    plan: dict | None = None,
    31→    state: dict | None = None,
    32→) -> TriageGuardrailResult:
    33→    """Pure detection: is triage stale? Returns structured result, no side effects."""
    34→    try:
    35→        resolved_plan = plan if isinstance(plan, dict) else load_plan()
    36→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
    37→        return TriageGuardrailResult()
    38→
    39→    resolved_state = state or {}
    40→
    41→    if not is_triage_stale(resolved_plan, resolved_state):
    42→        return TriageGuardrailResult(_plan=resolved_plan)
    43→
    44→    new_ids: set[str] = set()
    45→    if resolved_state:
    46→        new_ids = compute_new_finding_ids(resolved_plan, resolved_state)
    47→
    48→    return TriageGuardrailResult(is_stale=True, new_ids=new_ids, _plan=resolved_plan)
    49→
    50→
    51→def print_triage_guardrail_info(
    52→    *,
    53→    plan: dict | None = None,
    54→    state: dict | None = None,
    55→) -> bool:
    56→    """Print yellow info banner if triage is stale. Returns True if banner was shown."""
    57→    result = triage_guardrail_status(plan=plan, state=state)
    58→    if not result.is_stale:
    59→        return False
    60→
    61→    if result.new_ids:
    62→        print(colorize(
    63→            f"  {len(result.new_ids)} new review finding(s) not yet triaged.",
    64→            "yellow",
    65→        ))
    66→
    67→    if result._plan is not None:
    68→        banner = triage_phase_banner(result._plan)
    69→        if banner:
    70→            print(colorize(f"  {banner}", "yellow"))
    71→
    72→    return True
    73→
    74→
    75→def require_triage_current_or_exit(
    76→    *,
    77→    state: dict,
    78→    bypass: bool = False,
    79→    attest: str = "",
    80→) -> None:
    81→    """Gate: exit(1) if triage is stale and not bypassed. Name signals the exit."""
    82→    result = triage_guardrail_status(state=state)
    83→    if not result.is_stale:
    84→        return
    85→
    86→    if bypass and attest and len(attest.strip()) >= 30:
    87→        print(colorize(
    88→            "  Triage guardrail bypassed with attestation.",
    89→            "yellow",
    90→        ))
    91→        return
    92→
    93→    new_ids = result.new_ids
    94→    print(colorize(
    95→        f"\n  BLOCKED: {len(new_ids) or 'some'} new review finding(s) have not been triaged.",
    96→        "red",
    97→    ))
    98→    if new_ids:
    99→        for fid in sorted(new_ids)[:5]:
   100→            f = state.get("findings", {}).get(fid, {})
   101→            print(colorize(f"    * [{short_finding_id(fid)}] {f.get('summary', '')}", "red"))
   102→        if len(new_ids) > 5:
   103→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(new_ids) - 5} more", "dim"))
   104→    print()
   105→    print(colorize("  NEXT STEP: desloppify plan triage", "yellow"))
   106→    print(colorize(
   107→        "  (Review new findings, then either --confirm-existing or re-plan.)",
   108→        "dim",
   109→    ))
   110→    print()
   111→    print(colorize("  View new items:  desloppify plan queue --sort recent", "dim"))
   112→    print(colorize(
   113→        '  To bypass: --force-resolve --attest "I understand the plan may be stale..."',
   114→        "dim",
   115→    ))
   116→    print()
   117→    sys.exit(1)
   118→
   119→
   120→__all__ = [
   121→    "TriageGuardrailResult",
   122→    "print_triage_guardrail_info",
   123→    "require_triage_current_or_exit",
   124→    "triage_guardrail_status",
   125→]
   126→
```

```
    71→def _show_scan_visibility(noise, effective_include_slow: bool) -> None:
    72→    """Print fast-scan and noise budget visibility hints."""
    73→    if not effective_include_slow:
    74→        print(colorize("  * Fast scan — slow phases (duplicates) skipped", "yellow"))
    75→    if noise.budget_warning:
    76→        print(colorize(f"  * {noise.budget_warning}", "yellow"))
    77→    if noise.hidden_total:
    78→        print(
    79→            colorize(
    80→                f"  * {noise.hidden_total} findings hidden (showing {noise.noise_budget}/detector). "
    81→                "Use `desloppify show <detector>` to see all.",
    82→                "dim",
    83→            )
    84→        )
    85→
    86→
    87→def _show_coverage_preflight(runtime) -> None:
    88→    """Print preflight warnings when scan coverage confidence is reduced."""
    89→    warnings = getattr(runtime, "coverage_warnings", []) or []
    90→    if not isinstance(warnings, list) or not warnings:
    91→        return
    92→
    93→    for entry in warnings:
    94→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
    95→            continue
    96→        summary = str(entry.get("summary", "")).strip()
    97→        impact = str(entry.get("impact", "")).strip()
    98→        remediation = str(entry.get("remediation", "")).strip()
    99→        detector = str(entry.get("detector", "")).strip() or "detector"
   100→
```

```
    62→                    strict_val,
    63→                    int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
    64→                )
    65→            )
    66→    low.sort(key=lambda item: item[1])
    67→    return low
```

```
    20→def _enable_live_review_output() -> None:
    21→    """Best-effort: force line-buffered review output for non-TTY runners."""
    22→    for stream_name in ("stdout", "stderr"):
    23→        stream = getattr(sys, stream_name, None)
    24→        reconfigure = getattr(stream, "reconfigure", None)
    25→        if not callable(reconfigure):
    26→            continue
    27→        try:
    28→            reconfigure(line_buffering=True, write_through=True)
    29→        except (OSError, ValueError, TypeError) as exc:
    30→            _ = exc

    39→    )
    40→    sys.exit(1)

    45→    run_batches = bool(getattr(args, "run_batches", False))
    46→    import_run_dir = bool(getattr(args, "import_run_dir", None))

    49→    import_file = getattr(args, "import_file", None)
    50→    validate_import_file = getattr(args, "validate_import_file", None)
    51→    import_mode = bool(import_file) and not external_submit
    52→    flags = [
    53→        merge,
    54→        run_batches,
    55→        import_run_dir,
    56→        external_start,
    57→        external_submit,
    58→        import_mode,
    59→        bool(validate_import_file),
    60→    ]
    61→    return flags, import_file, validate_import_file
    62→
    63→
    64→def _validate_mode_selection_or_exit(
    65→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    66→    *,
    67→    mode_flags: list[bool],

    69→) -> None:
```

```
    48→def cmd_resolve(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    49→    """Resolve finding(s) matching one or more patterns."""
    50→    attestation = getattr(args, "attest", None)
    51→    _validate_resolve_inputs(args, attestation)
    52→
    53→    if args.status == "fixed":
    54→        note = getattr(args, "note", None)
    55→        if not validate_note_length(note):
    56→            show_note_length_requirement(note)
    57→            return
    58→
    59→    state_file = state_path(args)
    60→    state = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
    61→
    62→    if _check_queue_order_guard(state, args.patterns, args.status):
    63→        return
    64→
    65→    if args.status == "fixed":
    66→        require_triage_current_or_exit(
    67→            state=state,
    68→            bypass=bool(getattr(args, "force_resolve", False)),
    69→            attest=getattr(args, "attest", "") or "",
    70→        )
    71→
    72→    _enforce_batch_wontfix_confirmation(
    73→        state,
    74→        args,
    75→        attestation=attestation,
    76→        resolve_all_patterns_fn=_resolve_all_patterns,
    77→    )
    78→    prev = _previous_score_snapshot(state)
    79→    prev_subjective_scores = {
    80→        str(dim): (coerce_assessment_score(payload) or 0.0)
    81→        for dim, payload in (state.get("subjective_assessments") or {}).items()
    82→        if isinstance(dim, str)
    83→    }
    84→
    85→    all_resolved = _resolve_all_patterns(state, args, attestation=attestation)
    86→    if not all_resolved:
    87→        status_label = "resolved" if args.status == "open" else "open"
    88→        print(colorize(f"No {status_label} findings matching: {' '.join(args.patterns)}", "yellow"))
    89→        return
    90→
    91→    _save_state_or_exit(state, state_file)
    92→
    93→    plan = None
    94→    try:
    95→        if has_living_plan():
    96→            plan = load_plan()
    97→            purged = purge_ids(plan, all_resolved)
    98→            append_log_entry(
    99→                plan,
   100→                "resolve",
```

```
1→"""Sync subjective dimensions into the plan queue.
     2→
     3→Two independent sync functions:
     4→
     5→- **sync_unscored_dimensions** — prepend never-scored (placeholder) dimensions
     6→  to the *front* of the queue unconditionally (onboarding priority).
     7→- **sync_stale_dimensions** — append stale (previously-scored) dimensions to
     8→  the *back* of the queue when no objective items remain.
     9→"""
    10→
    11→from __future__ import annotations
    12→
    13→import hashlib
    14→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    15→
    16→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    17→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    18→
    19→SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX = "subjective::"
    20→TRIAGE_ID = "triage::pending"  # deprecated, kept for migration
    21→
    22→TRIAGE_PREFIX = "triage::"
    23→TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS = (
    24→    "triage::observe",
    25→    "triage::reflect",
    26→    "triage::organize",
    27→    "triage::commit",
    28→)
    29→TRIAGE_IDS = set(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS)
    30→WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID = "workflow::create-plan"
    31→WORKFLOW_PREFIX = "workflow::"
    32→SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES = ("triage::", "workflow::", "subjective::")
    33→
    34→# Detectors whose findings are NOT objective mechanical work.
    35→# Used to decide when the objective backlog is drained.
    36→NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS: frozenset[str] = frozenset({
    37→    "review", "concerns", "subjective_review", "subjective_assessment",
    38→})
    39→
    40→
    41→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    42→# Result dataclasses
    43→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    44→
    45→@dataclass
    46→class StaleDimensionSyncResult:
    47→    """What changed during a stale-dimension sync."""
    48→
    49→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    50→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    51→
    52→    @property
    53→    def changes(self) -> int:
    54→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    55→
    56→
    57→@dataclass
    58→class UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
    59→    """What changed during an unscored-dimension sync."""
    60→
    61→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    62→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    63→
    64→    @property
    65→    def changes(self) -> int:
    66→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    67→
    68→
    69→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    70→# ID helpers
    71→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    72→
    73→def _current_stale_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    74→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently stale."""
    75→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import slugify
    76→    from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    77→        scorecard_subjective_entries,
    78→    )
    79→
    80→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    81→    if not dim_scores:
    82→        return set()
    83→
    84→    stale: set[str] = set()
    85→    for entry in scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
    86→        if not entry.get("stale"):
    87→            continue
    88→        dim_key = entry.get("dimension_key", "")
    89→        if dim_key:
    90→            stale.add(f"{SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    91→    return stale
    92→
    93→
    94→def current_unscored_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    95→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently unscored (placeholder).
    96→
    97→    Checks ``subjective_assessments`` first; when that dict is empty
    98→    (common before any reviews have been run), falls through to
    99→    ``dimension_scores`` which carries placeholder metadata from scan.
   100→    """
   101→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import slugify
   102→
   103→    # Primary source: subjective_assessments with placeholder=True
   104→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
   105→    if isinstance(assessments, dict) and assessments:
   106→        unscored: set[str] = set()
   107→        for dim_key, payload in assessments.items():
   108→            if not isinstance(payload, dict):
   109→                continue
   110→            if not payload.get("placeholder"):
   111→                continue
   112→            if dim_key:
   113→                unscored.add(f"{SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX}{slugify(dim_key)}")
   114→        return unscored
   115→
   116→    # Fallback: check dimension_scores directly for placeholder subjective
   117→    # dimensions.  This handles the common case where subjective_assessments
   118→    # hasn't been populated yet but dimension_scores already has placeholder
   119→    # entries from scan.  We can't use scorecard_subjective_entries() here
   120→    # because the scorecard pipeline intentionally hides placeholders.
   121→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   122→    if not dim_scores:
   123→        return set()
   124→
   125→    unscored = set()
   126→    for _name, data in dim_scores.items():
   127→        if not isinstance(data, dict):
   128→            continue
   129→        detectors = data.get("detectors", {})
   130→        meta = detectors.get("subjective_assessment")
   131→        if not isinstance(meta, dict):
   132→            continue
   133→        if not meta.get("placeholder"):
   134→            continue
   135→        dim_key = meta.get("dimension_key", "")
   136→        if dim_key:
   137→            unscored.add(f"{SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX}{slugify(dim_key)}")
   138→    return unscored
   139→
   140→
   141→def current_under_target_ids(
   142→    state: StateModel,
   143→    *,
   144→    target_strict: float = 95.0,
   145→) -> set[str]:
   146→    """Return ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are under target but not stale or unscored.
   147→
   148→    These are dimensions whose assessment is still current (not needing refresh)
   149→    but whose score hasn't reached the target yet.
   150→    """
   151→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import slugify
   152→    from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
   153→        scorecard_subjective_entries,
   154→    )
   155→
   156→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   157→    if not dim_scores:
   158→        return set()
   159→
   160→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   161→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   162→
   163→    under_target: set[str] = set()
   164→    for entry in scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
   165→        if entry.get("placeholder") or entry.get("stale"):
   166→            continue
   167→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   168→        if strict_val >= target_strict:
   169→            continue
   170→        dim_key = entry.get("dimension_key", "")
   171→        if not dim_key:
   172→            continue
   173→        fid = f"{SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX}{slugify(dim_key)}"
   174→        if fid not in stale_ids and fid not in unscored_ids:
   175→            under_target.add(fid)
   176→    return under_target
   177→
   178→
   179→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   180→# Promoted-aware insertion helper
   181→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   182→
   183→def _after_promoted(order: list[str], plan: PlanModel) -> int:
   184→    """Return the insertion index just after the last promoted item in *order*.
   185→
   186→    When no promoted items are present (or none are in *order*), returns 0
   187→    so callers fall back to existing front-of-queue behavior.
   188→    """
   189→    promoted = set(plan.get("promoted_ids", []))
   190→    if not promoted:
   191→        return 0
   192→    last_idx = -1
   193→    for i, fid in enumerate(order):
   194→        if fid in promoted:
   195→            last_idx = i
   196→    return last_idx + 1 if last_idx >= 0 else 0
   197→
   198→
   199→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   200→# Unscored dimension sync (front of queue, unconditional)
   201→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   202→
   203→def sync_unscored_dimensions(
   204→    plan: PlanModel,
   205→    state: StateModel,
   206→) -> UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
   207→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions.
   208→
   209→    1. **Prune** — remove ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are
   210→       no longer unscored AND not stale (avoids pruning stale IDs — that is
   211→       ``sync_stale_dimensions``' responsibility).
   212→    2. **Inject** — unconditionally prepend currently-unscored IDs to the
   213→       *front* of ``queue_order`` so initial reviews are the first priority.
   214→    """
   215→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   216→    result = UnscoredDimensionSyncResult()
   217→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   218→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   219→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   220→
   221→    # --- Cleanup: prune subjective IDs that are no longer unscored --------
   222→    # Only prune IDs that are neither unscored nor stale (stale sync owns those).
   223→    to_remove: list[str] = [
   224→        fid for fid in order
   225→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   226→        and fid not in unscored_ids
   227→        and fid not in stale_ids
   228→    ]
   229→    for fid in to_remove:
   230→        order.remove(fid)
   231→        result.pruned.append(fid)
   232→
   233→    # --- Inject: prepend unscored IDs after any promoted items -------------
   234→    existing = set(order)
   235→    insert_at = _after_promoted(order, plan)
   236→    for uid in reversed(sorted(unscored_ids)):
   237→        if uid not in existing:
   238→            order.insert(insert_at, uid)
   239→            result.injected.append(uid)
   240→
   241→    return result
   242→
   243→
   244→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   245→# Stale dimension sync (back of queue, conditional)
   246→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   247→
   248→def sync_stale_dimensions(
   249→    plan: PlanModel,
   250→    state: StateModel,
   251→) -> StaleDimensionSyncResult:
   252→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with stale subjective dimensions.
   253→
   254→    1. Remove any ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are no
   255→       longer stale and not unscored (avoids pruning IDs owned by
   256→       ``sync_unscored_dimensions``).
   257→    2. If no objective items remain after cleanup, inject all currently-stale
   258→       dimension IDs so the plan surfaces them as actionable work.
   259→    """
   260→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   261→    result = StaleDimensionSyncResult()
   262→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   263→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   264→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   265→
   266→    # --- Cleanup: prune resolved subjective IDs --------------------------
   267→    # Only prune IDs that are neither stale nor unscored.
   268→    to_remove: list[str] = [
   269→        fid for fid in order
   270→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   271→        and fid not in stale_ids
   272→        and fid not in unscored_ids
   273→    ]
   274→    for fid in to_remove:
   275→        order.remove(fid)
   276→        result.pruned.append(fid)
   277→
   278→    # --- Inject: populate when no objective items remain -----------------
   279→    has_real_items = any(
   280→        f.get("status") == "open"
   281→        and f.get("detector") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   282→        and not f.get("suppressed")
   283→        for f in state.get("findings", {}).values()
   284→    )
   285→    if not has_real_items and stale_ids:
   286→        existing = set(order)
   287→        for sid in sorted(stale_ids):
   288→            if sid not in existing:
   289→                order.append(sid)
   290→                result.injected.append(sid)
   291→
   292→    return result
   293→
   294→
   295→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   296→# Triage snapshot hash + sync
   297→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   298→
   299→def review_finding_snapshot_hash(state: StateModel) -> str:
   300→    """Hash open review finding IDs to detect changes.
   301→
   302→    Returns empty string when there are no open review findings.
   303→    """
   304→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   305→    review_ids = sorted(
   306→        fid for fid, f in findings.items()
   307→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   308→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   309→    )
   310→    if not review_ids:
   311→        return ""
   312→    return hashlib.sha256("|".join(review_ids).encode()).hexdigest()[:16]
   313→
   314→
   315→@dataclass
   316→class TriageSyncResult:
   317→    """What changed during a triage sync."""
   318→
   319→    injected: bool = False
   320→    pruned: bool = False
   321→
   322→    @property
   323→    def changes(self) -> int:
   324→        return int(self.injected) + int(self.pruned)
   325→
   326→
   327→def sync_triage_needed(
   328→    plan: PlanModel,
   329→    state: StateModel,
   330→) -> TriageSyncResult:
   331→    """Inject 4 triage stage IDs at front of queue when review findings change.
   332→
   333→    Only injects stages not already confirmed in ``epic_triage_meta``.
   334→    Never auto-prunes — only explicit completion removes them.
   335→
   336→    When findings are *resolved* (current IDs are a subset of previously
   337→    triaged IDs), the snapshot hash is updated silently — no re-triage
   338→    is needed since the user is working through the plan.
   339→    """
   340→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   341→    result = TriageSyncResult()
   342→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   343→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   344→    confirmed = set(meta.get("triage_stages", {}).keys())
   345→
   346→    # Check if any triage stage is already in queue
   347→    already_present = any(sid in order for sid in TRIAGE_IDS)
   348→
   349→    current_hash = review_finding_snapshot_hash(state)
   350→    last_hash = meta.get("finding_snapshot_hash", "")
   351→
   352→    if current_hash and current_hash != last_hash and not already_present:
   353→        # Distinguish "new findings appeared" from "findings were resolved".
   354→        # Only re-triage when genuinely new findings exist.
   355→        findings = state.get("findings", {})
   356→        current_review_ids = {
   357→            fid for fid, f in findings.items()
   358→            if f.get("status") == "open"
   359→            and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   360→        }
   361→        triaged_ids = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
   362→        new_since_triage = current_review_ids - triaged_ids
   363→
   364→        if new_since_triage:
   365→            # New review findings appeared — re-triage needed
   366→            insert_at = _after_promoted(order, plan)
   367→            stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "commit")
   368→            existing = set(order)
   369→            injected_count = 0
   370→            for sid, name in zip(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS, stage_names):
   371→                if name not in confirmed and sid not in existing:
   372→                    order.insert(insert_at + injected_count, sid)
   373→                    injected_count += 1
   374→            if injected_count:
   375→                result.injected = True
   376→        else:
   377→            # Only resolved findings changed the hash — update silently
   378→            meta["finding_snapshot_hash"] = current_hash
   379→            plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
   380→
   381→    return result
   382→
   383→
   384→@dataclass
   385→class CreatePlanSyncResult:
   386→    """What changed during a create-plan sync."""
   387→
   388→    injected: bool = False
   389→
   390→    @property
   391→    def changes(self) -> int:
   392→        return int(self.injected)
   393→
   394→
   395→def sync_create_plan_needed(
   396→    plan: PlanModel,
   397→    state: StateModel,
   398→) -> CreatePlanSyncResult:
   399→    """Inject ``workflow::create-plan`` when reviews complete + objective backlog exists.
   400→
   401→    Only injects when:
   402→    - No unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions remain
   403→    - At least one objective finding exists
   404→    - ``workflow::create-plan`` is not already in the queue
   405→    - No triage stages are pending
   406→    """
   407→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   408→    result = CreatePlanSyncResult()
   409→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   410→
   411→    if WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID in order:
   412→        return result
   413→
   414→    # Don't inject if triage stages are pending
   415→    if any(sid in order for sid in TRIAGE_IDS):
   416→        return result
   417→
   418→    # Check that no unscored dimensions remain
   419→    unscored = current_unscored_ids(state)
   420→    if unscored:
   421→        return result
   422→
   423→    # Check that objective findings exist
   424→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   425→    has_objective = any(
   426→        f.get("status") == "open"
   427→        and f.get("detector") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   428→        for f in findings.values()
   429→    )
   430→    if not has_objective:
   431→        return result
   432→
   433→    # Insert after any subjective items, before findings
   434→    insert_at = 0
   435→    for i, fid in enumerate(order):
   436→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) or fid.startswith(TRIAGE_PREFIX):
   437→            insert_at = i + 1
   438→    order.insert(insert_at, WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID)
   439→    result.injected = True
   440→    return result
   441→
   442→
   443→def compute_new_finding_ids(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
   444→    """Return the set of open review/concerns finding IDs added since last triage.
   445→
   446→    Returns an empty set when no prior triage has recorded ``triaged_ids``.
   447→    """
   448→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   449→    triaged = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", meta.get("synthesized_ids", [])))
   450→    current = {
   451→        fid for fid, f in state.get("findings", {}).items()
   452→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   453→    }
   454→    return current - triaged if triaged else set()
   455→
   456→
   457→def is_triage_stale(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> bool:
   458→    """Side-effect-free check: is triage needed?
   459→
   460→    Returns True when any ``triage::*`` stage ID is in the queue OR
   461→    genuinely *new* review findings appeared since the last triage.
   462→
   463→    When findings are merely resolved (current IDs are a subset of
   464→    previously triaged IDs), triage is NOT stale — the user is working
   465→    through the plan.
   466→    """
   467→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   468→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   469→    if order & TRIAGE_IDS:
   470→        return True
   471→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   472→    last_hash = meta.get("finding_snapshot_hash", "")
   473→    if not last_hash:
   474→        return False
   475→    current_hash = review_finding_snapshot_hash(state)
   476→    if not current_hash or current_hash == last_hash:
   477→        return False
   478→    # Hash changed — check if new findings appeared or only resolutions
   479→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   480→    current_review_ids = {
   481→        fid for fid, f in findings.items()
   482→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   483→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   484→    }
   485→    triaged_ids = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
   486→    new_since_triage = current_review_ids - triaged_ids
   487→    return bool(new_since_triage)
   488→
   489→
   490→
   491→__all__ = [
   492→    "NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS",
   493→    "SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX",
   494→    "TRIAGE_ID",
   495→    "TRIAGE_IDS",
   496→    "TRIAGE_PREFIX",
   497→    "TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS",
   498→    "SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES",
   499→    "WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID",
   500→    "WORKFLOW_PREFIX",
   501→    "CreatePlanSyncResult",
   502→    "StaleDimensionSyncResult",
   503→    "TriageSyncResult",
   504→    "UnscoredDimensionSyncResult",
   505→    "current_under_target_ids",
   506→    "current_unscored_ids",
   507→    "compute_new_finding_ids",

   510→    "sync_create_plan_needed",
   511→    "sync_stale_dimensions",
   512→    "sync_triage_needed",
   513→    "sync_unscored_dimensions",
   514→]
   515→
```

```
1→"""Helper utilities for work queue item construction."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→from fnmatch import fnmatch
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core.enums import finding_status_tokens
     9→from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
    10→from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    11→    scorecard_subjective_entries,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.intelligence.integrity import (
    14→    is_holistic_subjective_finding,
    15→    unassessed_subjective_dimensions,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.scoring import DISPLAY_NAMES
    18→
    19→ALL_STATUSES = set(finding_status_tokens(include_all=True))
    20→ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY = {"auto_fix": 0, "refactor": 1, "manual_fix": 2, "reorganize": 3}
    21→ATTEST_EXAMPLE = (
    22→    "I have actually [DESCRIBE THE CONCRETE CHANGE YOU MADE] "
    23→    "and I am not gaming the score by resolving without fixing."
    24→)
    25→
    26→
    27→def detail_dict(item: dict) -> dict:
    28→    """Return finding detail as a dict; tolerate legacy/non-dict payloads."""
    29→    detail = item.get("detail")
    30→    return detail if isinstance(detail, dict) else {}
    31→
    32→
    33→def status_matches(item_status: str, status_filter: str) -> bool:
    34→    return status_filter == "all" or item_status == status_filter
    35→
    36→
    37→def is_subjective_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    38→    detector = item.get("detector")
    39→    if detector in {"subjective_assessment"}:
    40→        return True
    41→    if detector == "holistic_review":
    42→        return True
    43→    return False
    44→
    45→
    46→def is_review_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    47→    return item.get("detector") == "review"
    48→
    49→
    50→def is_subjective_queue_item(item: dict) -> bool:
    51→    """True for subjective work items, including collapsed subjective clusters."""
    52→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
    53→        return True
    54→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
    55→        members = item.get("members", [])
    56→        return bool(members) and all(
    57→            m.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" for m in members
    58→        )
    59→    return False
    60→
    61→
    62→def review_finding_weight(item: dict) -> float:
    63→    """Return review issue weight aligned with issues list ordering."""
    64→    confidence = str(item.get("confidence", "low")).lower()
    65→    weight_by_confidence = {
    66→        "high": 1.0,
    67→        "medium": 0.7,
    68→        "low": 0.3,
    69→    }
    70→    weight = weight_by_confidence.get(confidence, 0.3)
    71→    if detail_dict(item).get("holistic"):
    72→        weight *= 10.0
    73→    return float(weight)
    74→
    75→
    76→def scope_matches(item: dict, scope: str | None) -> bool:
    77→    """Apply show-style pattern matching against a queue item."""
    78→    if not scope:
    79→        return True
    80→
    81→    item_id = item.get("id", "")
    82→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
    83→    filepath = item.get("file", "")
    84→    summary = item.get("summary", "")
    85→    dimension = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
    86→    kind = item.get("kind", "")
    87→
    88→    if "*" in scope:
    89→        return any(
    90→            fnmatch(candidate, scope)
    91→            for candidate in (item_id, filepath, detector, dimension, summary)
    92→        )
    93→
    94→    if "::" in scope:
    95→        return item_id.startswith(scope)
    96→
    97→    lowered = scope.lower()
    98→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
    99→        return (
   100→            lowered in item_id.lower()
   101→            or lowered in dimension.lower()
   102→            or lowered in summary.lower()
   103→        )
   104→
   105→    # Hash suffix: 8+ hex chars matches the tail segment of a finding ID.
   106→    if len(lowered) >= 8 and re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]+", lowered):
   107→        return item_id.lower().endswith("::" + lowered)
   108→
   109→    return (
   110→        detector == scope
   111→        or filepath == scope
   112→        or filepath.startswith(scope.rstrip("/") + "/")
   113→    )
   114→
   115→
   116→def slugify(text: str) -> str:
   117→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9_]+", "_", text.lower()).strip("_")
   118→
   119→
   120→def _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(display_name: str) -> str:
   121→    """Map a display label (e.g. 'Mid elegance') to its canonical dimension key."""
   122→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   123→    target = cleaned.lower()
   124→
   125→    for dim_key, label in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
   126→        if str(label).lower() == target:
   127→            return str(dim_key)
   128→    return slugify(cleaned)
   129→
   130→
   131→def _subjective_dimension_aliases(display_name: str) -> set[str]:
   132→    """Return normalized aliases used to match display labels with finding dimension keys."""
   133→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   134→    canonical = _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(cleaned)
   135→    return {
   136→        cleaned.lower(),
   137→        cleaned.replace(" ", "_").lower(),
   138→        slugify(cleaned),
   139→        canonical.lower(),
   140→        slugify(canonical),
   141→    }
   142→
   143→
   144→def supported_fixers_for_item(state: dict, item: dict) -> set[str] | None:
   145→    """Return supported fixers for an item's language when known."""
   146→    lang = str(item.get("lang", "") or "").strip()
   147→    if not lang:
   148→        return None
   149→
   150→    caps = state.get("lang_capabilities", {})
   151→    if not isinstance(caps, dict):
   152→        return None
   153→
   154→    lang_caps = caps.get(lang, {})
   155→    if not isinstance(lang_caps, dict):
   156→        return None
   157→
   158→    fixers = lang_caps.get("fixers")
   159→    if not isinstance(fixers, list):
   160→        return None
   161→    return {fixer for fixer in fixers if isinstance(fixer, str)}
   162→
   163→
   164→def primary_command_for_finding(
   165→    item: dict, *, supported_fixers: set[str] | None = None
   166→) -> str:
   167→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
   168→    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   169→    if meta and meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and meta.fixers:
   170→        available_fixers = [
   171→            fixer
   172→            for fixer in meta.fixers
   173→            if supported_fixers is not None and fixer in supported_fixers
   174→        ]
   175→        if available_fixers:
   176→            return f"desloppify fix {available_fixers[0]} --dry-run"
   177→    if detector == "subjective_review":
   178→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   179→            return "desloppify review --prepare"
   180→        return "desloppify show subjective"
   181→    return f'desloppify plan done "{item.get("id", "")}" --note "<what you did>" --confirm'
   182→
   183→
   184→def build_triage_stage_items(plan: dict, state: dict) -> list[dict]:
   185→    """Build synthetic work items for each ``triage::*`` stage ID in the queue.
   186→
   187→    Returns an empty list when no triage stages are pending.
   188→    """
   189→    from desloppify.app.commands.plan.triage_playbook import (
   190→        TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES,
   191→        TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS,
   192→    )
   193→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (
   194→        TRIAGE_IDS,
   195→        TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS,
   196→    )
   197→
   198→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   199→    order_set = set(order)
   200→    present = order_set & TRIAGE_IDS
   201→    if not present:
   202→        return []
   203→
   204→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   205→    confirmed = set(meta.get("triage_stages", {}).keys())
   206→
   207→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   208→    open_review_count = sum(
   209→        1 for f in findings.values()
   210→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   211→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   212→    )
   213→
   214→    label_map = dict(TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS)
   215→    stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "commit")
   216→
   217→    items: list[dict] = []
   218→    for idx, (sid, name) in enumerate(zip(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS, stage_names)):
   219→        if sid not in present:
   220→            continue
   221→        if name in confirmed:
   222→            continue
   223→
   224→        # Compute blocked_by: dependency stages that are still in the queue
   225→        deps = TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES.get(name, set())
   226→        blocked_by = sorted(
   227→            f"triage::{dep}" for dep in deps
   228→            if f"triage::{dep}" in present and dep not in confirmed
   229→        )
   230→
   231→        cmd = f"desloppify plan triage --stage {name}"
   232→        if name == "commit":
   233→            cmd = 'desloppify plan triage --complete --strategy "..."'
   234→
   235→        items.append({
   236→            "id": sid,
   237→            "tier": 1,
   238→            "confidence": "high",
   239→            "detector": "triage",
   240→            "file": ".",
   241→            "kind": "workflow_stage",
   242→            "stage_name": name,
   243→            "stage_index": idx,
   244→            "summary": f"Triage: {label_map.get(name, name)}",
   245→            "detail": {
   246→                "total_review_findings": open_review_count,
   247→                "stage": name,
   248→                "stage_label": label_map.get(name, name),
   249→            },
   250→            "primary_command": cmd,
   251→            "blocked_by": blocked_by,
   252→            "is_blocked": bool(blocked_by),
   253→        })
   254→    return items
   255→
   256→
   257→def build_create_plan_item(plan: dict) -> dict | None:
   258→    """Build a synthetic work item for ``workflow::create-plan`` if it's in the queue.
   259→
   260→    Returns ``None`` when the item is not pending.
   261→    """
   262→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID
   263→
   264→    if WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):

   266→
   267→    return {

   274→        "summary": "Create prioritized plan from review results",

   282→def subjective_strict_scores(state: dict) -> dict[str, float]:
   283→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   284→    if not dim_scores:
   285→        return {}
   286→
   287→    entries = scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   288→    scores: dict[str, float] = {}
   289→    for entry in entries:
   290→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   291→        if not name:
   292→            continue
   293→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   294→        [REDACTED](name)
   295→        aliases = _subjective_dimension_aliases(name)
   296→        for cli_key in entry.get("cli_keys", []):
   297→            key = str(cli_key).strip().lower()
   298→            if not key:
   299→                continue
   300→            aliases.add(key)
   301→            aliases.add(slugify(key))
   302→        aliases.add(dim_key.lower())
   303→        aliases.add(slugify(dim_key))
   304→        for alias in aliases:
   305→            scores[alias] = strict_val
   306→    return scores
   307→
   308→
   309→def _unassessed_entries_from_dim_scores(dim_scores: dict) -> list[dict]:
   310→    """Build scorecard-like entries for unassessed placeholder dimensions.
   311→
   312→    ``scorecard_subjective_entries`` goes through the scorecard pipeline which
   313→    intentionally hides placeholders from display.  This function reads
   314→    ``dimension_scores`` directly so initial-review items are never lost.
   315→    """
   316→    entries: list[dict] = []
   317→    for name, data in dim_scores.items():
   318→        if not isinstance(data, dict):
   319→            continue
   320→        detectors = data.get("detectors", {})
   321→        meta = detectors.get("subjective_assessment")
   322→        if not isinstance(meta, dict):
   323→            continue
   324→        if not meta.get("placeholder"):
   325→            continue
   326→        dim_key = meta.get("dimension_key", "")
   327→        entries.append(
   328→            {
   329→                "name": name,
   330→                "score": float(data.get("score", 0.0)),
   331→                "strict": float(data.get("strict", 0.0)),
   332→                "checks": int(data.get("checks", 0) or 0),
   333→                "issues": int(data.get("issues", 0) or 0),
   334→                "tier": int(data.get("tier", 4) or 4),
   335→                "placeholder": True,
   336→                "stale": False,
   337→                "dimension_key": dim_key,
   338→                "cli_keys": [dim_key] if dim_key else [],
   339→            }
   340→        )
   341→    return entries
   342→
   343→
   344→def build_subjective_items(
   345→    state: dict, findings: dict, *, threshold: float = 100.0
   346→) -> list[dict]:
   347→    """Create synthetic subjective work items."""
   348→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   349→    if not dim_scores:
   350→        return []
   351→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, float(threshold)))
   352→
   353→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   354→
   355→    # The scorecard pipeline hides unassessed placeholders from display.
   356→    # Merge them in so initial-review items always appear in the queue.
   357→    seen_dims: set[str] = {
   358→        str(e.get("dimension_key", "")).lower()
   359→        for e in subjective_entries
   360→        if e.get("dimension_key")
   361→    }
   362→    for entry in _unassessed_entries_from_dim_scores(dim_scores):
   363→        dk = str(entry.get("dimension_key", "")).lower()
   364→        if dk and dk not in seen_dims:
   365→            subjective_entries.append(entry)
   366→            seen_dims.add(dk)
   367→
   368→    if not subjective_entries:
   369→        return []
   370→    unassessed_dims = {
   371→        str(name).strip()
   372→        for name in unassessed_subjective_dimensions(
   373→            dim_scores
   374→        )
   375→    }
   376→
   377→    # Review findings are keyed by raw dimension name (snake_case).
   378→    review_open_by_dim: dict[str, int] = {}
   379→    for finding in findings.values():
   380→        if finding.get("status") != "open" or finding.get("detector") != "review":
   381→            continue
   382→        dim_key = str(detail_dict(finding).get("dimension", "")).strip().lower()
   383→        if not dim_key:
   384→            continue
   385→        review_open_by_dim[dim_key] = review_open_by_dim.get(dim_key, 0) + 1
   386→
   387→    items: list[dict] = []
   388→    def _prepare_command(
   389→        cli_keys: list[str],
   390→        *,
   391→        force_review_rerun: bool = False,
   392→    ) -> str:
   393→        command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   394→        if cli_keys:
   395→            command += " --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   396→        if force_review_rerun:
   397→            command += " --force-review-rerun"
   398→        return command
   399→
   400→    for entry in subjective_entries:
   401→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   402→        if not name:
   403→            continue
   404→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   405→        if strict_val >= threshold:
   406→            continue
   407→
   408→        [REDACTED](name)
   409→        aliases = set(_subjective_dimension_aliases(name))
   410→        cli_keys = [
   411→            str(key).strip().lower()
   412→            for key in entry.get("cli_keys", [])
   413→            if str(key).strip()
   414→        ]
   415→        aliases.update(cli_keys)
   416→        aliases.update(slugify(key) for key in cli_keys)
   417→        open_review = sum(review_open_by_dim.get(alias, 0) for alias in aliases)
   418→        is_unassessed = bool(entry.get("placeholder")) or (
   419→            name in unassessed_dims
   420→            or (strict_val <= 0.0 and int(entry.get("issues", 0)) == 0)
   421→        )
   422→        is_stale = bool(entry.get("stale"))
   423→        # If review findings already exist for this dimension, triage/fix them
   424→        # before suggesting another review refresh pass.
   425→        if open_review > 0:
   426→            primary_command = "desloppify show review --status open"
   427→        else:
   428→            primary_command = _prepare_command(cli_keys)
   429→        stale_tag = " [stale — re-review]" if is_stale else ""
   430→        summary = f"Subjective dimension below target: {name} ({strict_val:.1f}%){stale_tag}"
   431→        items.append(
   432→            {
   433→                "id": f"subjective::{slugify(dim_key)}",
   434→                "detector": "subjective_assessment",
   435→                "file": ".",
   436→                "confidence": "medium",
   437→                "summary": summary,
   438→                "detail": {
   439→                    "dimension_name": name,
   440→                    "dimension": dim_key,
   441→                    "issues": int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
   442→                    "strict_score": strict_val,
   443→                    "open_review_findings": open_review,
   444→                    "cli_keys": cli_keys,
   445→                },
   446→                "status": "open",
   447→                "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   448→                "primary_command": primary_command,
   449→                "initial_review": is_unassessed,
   450→                "stale_review": is_stale and not is_unassessed,
   451→            }
   452→        )
   453→    return items
   454→
   455→
   456→__all__ = [
   457→    "ALL_STATUSES",
   458→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   459→    "build_create_plan_item",
   460→    "build_subjective_items",
```

```
1→"""Shared subjective follow-up rendering helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Protocol
     6→
     7→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     8→
     9→
    10→class SubjectiveFollowup(Protocol):
    11→    """Contract for objects passed to print_subjective_followup."""
    12→
    13→    low_assessed: object  # falsy when no dimensions are below threshold
    14→    threshold_label: str
    15→    rendered: str
    16→    command: str
    17→    integrity_lines: list[tuple[str, str]]
    18→
    19→
    20→def print_subjective_followup(
    21→    followup: SubjectiveFollowup,
    22→    *,
    23→    leading_newline: bool = False,
    24→    objective_backlog: int = 0,
    25→) -> bool:
    26→    """Render common subjective quality/integrity guidance lines.
    27→
    28→    When *objective_backlog* > 0, quality nudges are suppressed entirely — the
    29→    queue already surfaces subjective work when it becomes actionable.
    30→    Integrity warnings are always shown (anti-gaming safeguard).
    31→
    32→    Returns True when any line is rendered.
    33→    """
    34→    printed = False
    35→    prefix = "\n" if leading_newline else ""
    36→    if followup.low_assessed and objective_backlog <= 0:
    37→        print(
    38→            colorize(
    39→                f"{prefix}  Subjective quality (<{followup.threshold_label}%): "
    40→                f"{followup.rendered}",
    41→                "cyan",
    42→            )
    43→        )
    44→        print(
    45→            colorize(
    46→                f"  Next command to improve subjective scores: {followup.command}",
    47→                "dim",
    48→            )
    49→        )
    50→        printed = True
    51→
    52→    if followup.integrity_lines:
    53→        if printed:
    54→            print()
    55→        for style, message in followup.integrity_lines:
    56→            print(colorize(f"  {message}", style))
    57→        printed = True
    58→    return printed
    59→
    60→
    61→__all__ = ["print_subjective_followup"]
    62→
```

```
1→"""Tests for the review-rerun preflight gate (issue #157)."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from types import SimpleNamespace
     6→from unittest.mock import MagicMock, patch
     7→
     8→import pytest
     9→
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.review.helpers import parse_dimensions
    11→from desloppify.app.commands.review.preflight import (
    12→    _scored_dimensions,
    13→    clear_stale_subjective_entries,
    14→    review_rerun_preflight,
    15→)
    16→
    17→
    18→def _make_args(**overrides) -> SimpleNamespace:
    19→    defaults = {"force_review_rerun": False, "dimensions": None}
    20→    defaults.update(overrides)
    21→    return SimpleNamespace(**defaults)
    22→
    23→
    24→def _state_with_prior_review() -> dict:
    25→    """State that has a prior subjective review (nonzero dimension scores)."""
    26→    return {
    27→        "subjective_assessments": {
    28→            "naming_quality": {"score": 82.0},
    29→            "logic_clarity": {"score": 90.0},
    30→        },
    31→    }
    32→
    33→
    34→# -- review_rerun_preflight (gate logic) --------------------------------------
    35→
    36→
    37→_BUILD_WQ = "desloppify.app.commands.review.preflight.build_work_queue"
    38→
    39→
    40→def _wq_result(items: list[dict]) -> dict:
    41→    return {
    42→        "items": items,
    43→        "total": len(items),
    44→        "grouped": {},
    45→    }
    46→
    47→
    48→def test_blocked_when_open_objective_items(capsys):
    49→    """Preflight blocks when open objective findings exist."""
    50→    state = _state_with_prior_review()
    51→    items = [
    52→        {"id": f"f{i}", "summary": f"Finding {i}"} for i in range(3)
    53→    ]
    54→    with patch(_BUILD_WQ, return_value=_wq_result(items)):
    55→        with pytest.raises(SystemExit) as exc:
    56→            review_rerun_preflight(state, _make_args())
    57→        assert exc.value.code == 1
    58→
    59→    err = capsys.readouterr().err
    60→    assert "Open objective finding(s): 3" in err
    61→    assert "--force-review-rerun" in err
    62→
    63→
    64→def test_blocked_when_open_subjective_queue_items(capsys):
    65→    """Preflight blocks when subjective queue work remains on rerun."""
    66→    state = _state_with_prior_review()
    67→    subjective_items = [
    68→        {
    69→            "id": "subjective::naming_quality",
    70→            "kind": "subjective_dimension",
    71→            "detail": {"dimension": "naming_quality"},
    72→        }
    73→    ]
    74→    with patch(
    75→        _BUILD_WQ,
    76→        side_effect=[_wq_result([]), _wq_result(subjective_items)],
    77→    ):
    78→        with pytest.raises(SystemExit) as exc:
    79→            review_rerun_preflight(state, _make_args())
    80→        assert exc.value.code == 1
    81→
    82→    err = capsys.readouterr().err
    83→    assert "objective: 0, subjective: 1" in err

    88→def test_blocked_message_is_concise(capsys):
    89→    """Blocked message does not dump individual finding IDs."""

    92→        {"id": f"f{i}", "summary": f"Finding {i}"} for i in range(8)
    93→    ]

    95→        with pytest.raises(SystemExit):
    96→            review_rerun_preflight(state, _make_args())
    97→

    99→    assert "Open objective finding(s): 8" in err
   100→    # Should NOT dump individual finding IDs

   102→    assert "f7" not in err

   434→                "score": 90.0,

   439→    assert cleared == ["naming_quality"]
   440→

   442→    assert "needs_review_refresh" not in nq

   554→
   555→# -- Mode gating via mock-based dispatch (entrypoint) --------------------------
   556→#
   557→# These tests call cmd_review with patched dependencies and verify that
   558→# review_rerun_preflight is (or is not) invoked depending on the mode.
   559→
   560→
   561→_PREFLIGHT = "desloppify.app.commands.review.entrypoint.review_rerun_preflight"
   562→_RUNTIME = "desloppify.app.commands.review.entrypoint.command_runtime"
   563→_LANG = "desloppify.app.commands.review.entrypoint.resolve_lang"
   564→
   565→
   566→def _review_args(**overrides) -> SimpleNamespace:
   567→    """Build a minimal review args namespace for cmd_review."""
   568→    defaults = {
   569→        "path": ".",
   570→        "lang": "python",
   571→        "force_review_rerun": False,
   572→        "dimensions": None,
   573→        "merge": False,
   574→        "run_batches": False,
   575→        "external_start": False,
   576→        "external_submit": False,
   577→        "import_file": None,
   578→        "validate_import_file": None,
   579→        "session_id": None,
   580→    }
   581→    defaults.update(overrides)
   582→    return SimpleNamespace(**defaults)
   583→
   584→
   585→def _mock_runtime():
   586→    """Return a mock command_runtime result."""
   587→    rt = MagicMock()
   588→    rt.state_path = "/tmp/state.json"
   589→    rt.state = {}
   590→    rt.config = {}
   591→    return rt
   592→
   593→
   594→def test_prepare_calls_preflight():
   595→    """Default (prepare) mode invokes preflight."""
   596→    from desloppify.app.commands.review.entrypoint import cmd_review
   597→
   598→    with (
   599→        patch(_PREFLIGHT) as mock_pf,
   600→        patch(_RUNTIME, return_value=_mock_runtime()),
   601→        patch(_LANG, return_value=MagicMock(name="python")),
   602→        patch("desloppify.app.commands.review.entrypoint.do_prepare"),
   603→    ):
   604→        cmd_review(_review_args())
   605→        mock_pf.assert_called_once()
   606→
   607→
   608→def test_run_batches_calls_preflight():
   609→    """--run-batches mode invokes preflight."""
   610→    from desloppify.app.commands.review.entrypoint import cmd_review
   611→
   612→    with (
   613→        patch(_PREFLIGHT) as mock_pf,
   614→        patch(_RUNTIME, return_value=_mock_runtime()),
   615→        patch(_LANG, return_value=MagicMock(name="python")),
   616→        patch("desloppify.app.commands.review.entrypoint._do_run_batches"),
   617→    ):
   618→        cmd_review(_review_args(run_batches=True))
   619→        mock_pf.assert_called_once()
   620→
   621→
   622→def test_external_start_calls_preflight():
   623→    """--external-start mode invokes preflight."""
   624→    from desloppify.app.commands.review.entrypoint import cmd_review
   625→
   626→    with (
   627→        patch(_PREFLIGHT) as mock_pf,
   628→        patch(_RUNTIME, return_value=_mock_runtime()),
   629→        patch(_LANG, return_value=MagicMock(name="python")),
   630→        patch("desloppify.app.commands.review.entrypoint.do_external_start"),
   631→    ):
   632→        cmd_review(_review_args(external_start=True))
   633→        mock_pf.assert_called_once()
   634→
   635→
   636→def test_import_skips_preflight():
   637→    """--import skips preflight (import is a resolution step that drains backlog)."""
   638→    from desloppify.app.commands.review.entrypoint import cmd_review
   639→
   640→    with (
   641→        patch(_PREFLIGHT) as mock_pf,
   642→        patch(_RUNTIME, return_value=_mock_runtime()),
   643→        patch(_LANG, return_value=MagicMock(name="python")),
```

```
1→"""Auto-clustering algorithm — groups findings into task clusters."""
     2→

    20→_MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE = 2
    21→_STALE_KEY = "subjective::stale"
    22→_STALE_NAME = "auto/stale-review"
    23→_UNSCORED_KEY = "subjective::unscored"
    24→_UNSCORED_NAME = "auto/initial-review"

    26→_UNDER_TARGET_NAME = "auto/under-target-review"
```
