    30→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    31→# Grouping key computation
    32→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    33→
    34→def _extract_subtype(finding: dict) -> str | None:
    35→    """Extract the subtype/kind from a finding.
    36→
    37→    Checks detail.kind first, then falls back to parsing the finding ID
    38→    (format: ``detector::file::subtype`` or ``detector::file::symbol::subtype``).
    39→    """
    40→    detail = finding.get("detail") or {}
    41→    kind = detail.get("kind")
    42→    if kind:
    43→        return kind
    44→
    45→    # Parse from finding ID — third segment is often the subtype
    46→    fid = finding.get("id", "")
    47→    parts = fid.split("::")
    48→    if len(parts) >= 3:
    49→        # For IDs like smells::file.py::silent_except, the subtype is parts[2]
    50→        # For IDs like dict_keys::file.py::key::phantom_read, subtype is last part
    51→        candidate = parts[-1]
    52→        # Skip if it looks like a file path or symbol name (contains / or .)
    53→        if "/" not in candidate and "." not in candidate:
    54→            return candidate
    55→    return None
    56→
    57→
    58→def _grouping_key(finding: dict, meta: DetectorMeta | None) -> str | None:
    59→    """Compute a deterministic grouping key for a finding.
    60→
    61→    Returns None if the finding should not be auto-clustered.
    62→    """
    63→    detector = finding.get("detector", "")
    64→
    65→    if meta is None:
    66→        # Unknown detector — group by detector name
    67→        return f"detector::{detector}"
    68→
    69→    # Review findings → group by dimension
    70→    if detector in ("review", "subjective_review"):
    71→        detail = finding.get("detail") or {}
    72→        dimension = detail.get("dimension", "")
    73→        if dimension:
    74→            return f"review::{dimension}"
    75→        return f"detector::{detector}"
    76→
    77→    # Per-file detectors (structural, responsibility_cohesion)
    78→    if meta.needs_judgment and detector in (
    79→        "structural", "responsibility_cohesion",
    80→    ):
    81→        file = finding.get("file", "")
    82→        if file:
    83→            basename = os.path.basename(file)
    84→            return f"file::{detector}::{basename}"
    85→
    86→    # Needs-judgment detectors — group by subtype when available
    87→    if meta.needs_judgment:
    88→        subtype = _extract_subtype(finding)
    89→        if subtype:
    90→            return f"typed::{detector}::{subtype}"
    91→
    92→    # Pure auto-fix (no judgment needed) → all findings by detector
    93→    if meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and not meta.needs_judgment:
    94→        return f"auto::{detector}"
    95→
    96→    # Everything else → by detector
    97→    return f"detector::{detector}"
    98→
    99→
   100→def _cluster_name_from_key(key: str) -> str:
   101→    """Convert a grouping key to a cluster name with auto/ prefix."""
   102→    # auto::unused → auto/unused
   103→    # typed::dict_keys::phantom_read → auto/dict_keys-phantom_read
   104→    # file::structural::big_file.py → auto/structural-big_file.py
   105→    # review::abstraction_fitness → auto/review-abstraction_fitness
   106→    # detector::security → auto/security
   107→    parts = key.split("::")
   108→    if len(parts) == 2:
   109→        prefix_type = parts[0]
   110→        # For review keys, keep the prefix for clarity
   111→        if prefix_type == "review":
   112→            return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}review-{parts[1]}"
   113→        return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}{parts[1]}"
   114→    if len(parts) == 3:
   115→        return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}{parts[1]}-{parts[2]}"
   116→    return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}{key.replace('::', '-')}"
   117→
   118→
   119→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   120→# Description and action generation
   121→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   122→
   123→def _generate_description(
   124→    cluster_name: str,
   125→    members: list[dict],
   126→    meta: DetectorMeta | None,
   127→    subtype: str | None,
   128→) -> str:
   129→    """Generate a human-readable cluster description."""
   130→    count = len(members)
   131→    detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
   132→
   133→    if detector in ("review", "subjective_review"):
   134→        detail = (members[0].get("detail") or {}) if members else {}
   135→        dimension = detail.get("dimension", detector)
   136→        return f"Address {count} {dimension} review findings"
   137→
   138→    if detector == "structural":
   139→        files = {os.path.basename(m.get("file", "")) for m in members}
   140→        if len(files) == 1:
   141→            return f"Decompose {next(iter(files))}"
   142→        return f"Decompose {count} large files"
   143→
   144→    display = meta.display if meta else detector
   145→    if subtype:
   146→        label = subtype.replace("_", " ")
   147→        return f"Fix {count} {label} issues"
   148→
   149→    if meta and meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and not meta.needs_judgment:
   150→        return f"Remove {count} {display} findings"
   151→
   152→    return f"Fix {count} {display} issues"
   153→
   154→
   155→def _subtype_has_fixer(meta: DetectorMeta, subtype: str | None) -> str | None:
   156→    """Check if a subtype maps to a specific fixer."""
   157→    if not meta.fixers or not subtype:
   158→        return None
   159→    # Convention: fixer name is subtype with _ replaced by -
   160→    fixer_name = subtype.replace("_", "-")
   161→    if fixer_name in meta.fixers:
   162→        return fixer_name
   163→    # Also check if subtype is a substring (e.g. "unused" matches "unused-imports")
   164→    for fixer in meta.fixers:
   165→        if subtype in fixer:
   166→            return fixer
   167→    return None
   168→
   169→
   170→def _strip_guidance_examples(guidance: str) -> str:
   171→    """Strip subtype-specific examples from guidance text.
   172→
   173→    Many guidance strings have format "verb — specific examples".
   174→    Strip after the dash to get the core action:
   175→      "fix code smells — dead useEffect, empty if chains" → "fix code smells"
   176→    """
   177→    if " — " in guidance:
   178→        return guidance.split(" — ", 1)[0].strip()
   179→    return guidance
   180→
   181→
   182→_ACTION_TYPE_TEMPLATES: dict[str, str] = {
   183→    "reorganize": "reorganize with desloppify move",
   184→    "refactor": "review and refactor each finding",
   185→    "manual_fix": "review and fix each finding",
   186→}
   187→
   188→
   189→def _generate_action(
   190→    meta: DetectorMeta | None,
   191→    subtype: str | None,
   192→) -> str:
   193→    """Generate an action string from detector metadata.
   194→
   195→    Always returns a non-empty string — every cluster gets an action.
   196→    """
   197→    if meta is None:
   198→        return "review and fix each finding"
   199→
   200→    # For detectors with subtypes, only suggest a fixer if the subtype matches
   201→    if subtype and meta.fixers:
   202→        matched_fixer = _subtype_has_fixer(meta, subtype)
   203→        if matched_fixer:
   204→            return f"desloppify fix {matched_fixer} --dry-run"
   205→    elif meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and meta.fixers and not meta.needs_judgment:
   206→        # Pure auto-fix detector, no subtypes
   207→        return f"desloppify fix {meta.fixers[0]} --dry-run"
   208→
   209→    if meta.tool == "move":
   210→        return "desloppify move"
   211→
   212→    # Guidance available — use it (strip examples for subtyped detectors)
   213→    if meta.guidance:
   214→        if subtype:
   215→            return _strip_guidance_examples(meta.guidance)
   216→        return meta.guidance
   217→
   218→    # Final fallback: action_type template
   219→    return _ACTION_TYPE_TEMPLATES.get(
   220→        meta.action_type, "review and fix each finding"
   221→    )
   222→
   223→
   224→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   225→# Repair
   226→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   227→
   228→def _repair_ghost_cluster_refs(plan: PlanModel, now: str) -> int:
   229→    """Clear override cluster refs that point to non-existent clusters."""
   230→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   231→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
   232→    repaired = 0
   233→    for override in overrides.values():
   234→        cluster_name = override.get("cluster")
   235→        if cluster_name and cluster_name not in clusters:
   236→            override["cluster"] = None
   237→            override["updated_at"] = now
   238→            repaired += 1
   239→    return repaired
   240→
   241→
   242→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   243→# Shared create-or-update helper
   244→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   245→
   246→def _sync_auto_cluster(
   247→    plan: PlanModel,
   248→    clusters: dict,
   249→    existing_by_key: dict[str, str],
   250→    *,
   251→    cluster_key: str,
   252→    cluster_name: str,
   253→    member_ids: list[str],
   254→    description: str,
   255→    action: str,
   256→    now: str,
   257→    optional: bool = False,
   258→) -> int:
   259→    """Create or update an auto-cluster and sync its override entries.
   260→
   261→    Handles the common pattern: check if cluster exists by key, update
   262→    membership/metadata if changed, or create a new cluster.  Always syncs
   263→    override entries for all *member_ids*.
   264→
   265→    Returns 1 if a change was made, 0 otherwise.
   266→    """
   267→    changes = 0
   268→    existing_name = existing_by_key.get(cluster_key)
   269→    if existing_name and existing_name in clusters:
   270→        cluster = clusters[existing_name]
   271→        old_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   272→        new_ids_set = set(member_ids)
   273→        if old_ids != new_ids_set or cluster.get("description") != description or cluster.get("action") != action:
   274→            cluster["finding_ids"] = list(member_ids)
   275→            cluster["description"] = description
   276→            cluster["action"] = action
   277→            cluster["updated_at"] = now
   278→            changes = 1
   279→    else:
   280→        new_cluster: Cluster = {
   281→            "name": cluster_name,
   282→            "description": description,
   283→            "finding_ids": list(member_ids),
   284→            "created_at": now,
   285→            "updated_at": now,
   286→            "auto": True,
   287→            "cluster_key": cluster_key,
   288→            "action": action,
   289→            "user_modified": False,
   290→        }
   291→        if optional:
   292→            new_cluster["optional"] = True
   293→        clusters[cluster_name] = new_cluster
   294→        existing_by_key[cluster_key] = cluster_name
   295→        changes = 1
   296→
   297→    # Sync overrides
   298→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
   299→    current_name = existing_by_key.get(cluster_key, cluster_name)
   300→    for fid in member_ids:
   301→        if fid not in overrides:
   302→            overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   303→        overrides[fid]["cluster"] = current_name
   304→        overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   305→
   306→    return changes
   307→
   308→
   309→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   310→# Main algorithm
   311→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   312→
   313→def _sync_finding_clusters(
   314→    plan: PlanModel,
   315→    findings: dict,
   316→    clusters: dict,
   317→    existing_by_key: dict[str, str],
   318→    active_auto_keys: set[str],
   319→    now: str,
   320→) -> int:
   321→    """Group open findings by detector/subtype and sync auto-clusters."""
   322→    changes = 0
   323→
   324→    # Set of finding IDs in manual (non-auto) clusters
   325→    manual_member_ids: set[str] = set()
   326→    for cluster in clusters.values():
   327→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   328→            manual_member_ids.update(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   329→
   330→    # Collect open, non-suppressed findings and group by key
   331→    groups: dict[str, list[str]] = defaultdict(list)
   332→    finding_data: dict[str, dict] = {}
   333→    for fid, finding in findings.items():
   334→        if finding.get("status") != "open":
   335→            continue
   336→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
   337→            continue
   338→        if fid in manual_member_ids:
   339→            continue
   340→
   341→        detector = finding.get("detector", "")
   342→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   343→        key = _grouping_key(finding, meta)
   344→        if key is None:
   345→            continue
   346→
   347→        groups[key].append(fid)
   348→        finding_data[fid] = finding
   349→
   350→    # Drop singleton groups
   351→    groups = {k: v for k, v in groups.items() if len(v) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE}
   352→
   353→    for key, member_ids in groups.items():
   354→        active_auto_keys.add(key)
   355→        cluster_name = _cluster_name_from_key(key)
   356→
   357→        # Representative finding for metadata
   358→        rep = finding_data.get(member_ids[0], {})
   359→        detector = rep.get("detector", "")
   360→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   361→        members = [finding_data[fid] for fid in member_ids if fid in finding_data]
   362→
   363→        # Extract subtype from grouping key (typed::detector::subtype)
   364→        key_parts = key.split("::")
   365→        subtype = key_parts[2] if len(key_parts) >= 3 else None
   366→
   367→        description = _generate_description(cluster_name, members, meta, subtype)
   368→        action = _generate_action(meta, subtype)
   369→
   370→        # User-modified clusters: merge new members only, keep user edits
   371→        existing_name = existing_by_key.get(key)
   372→        if existing_name and existing_name in clusters:
   373→            cluster = clusters[existing_name]
   374→            if cluster.get("user_modified"):
   375→                existing_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   376→                new_ids = [fid for fid in member_ids if fid not in existing_ids]
   377→                if new_ids:
   378→                    cluster["finding_ids"].extend(new_ids)
   379→                    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   380→                    changes += 1
   381→                # Still sync overrides for all members
   382→                overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
   383→                for fid in member_ids:
   384→                    if fid not in overrides:
   385→                        overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   386→                    overrides[fid]["cluster"] = existing_name
   387→                    overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   388→                continue
   389→
   390→        # Handle name collision (existing auto-cluster with different key)
   391→        if cluster_name in clusters and clusters[cluster_name].get("cluster_key") != key:
   392→            cluster_name = f"{cluster_name}-{len(member_ids)}"
   393→
   394→        changes += _sync_auto_cluster(
   395→            plan, clusters, existing_by_key,
   396→            cluster_key=key,
   397→            cluster_name=cluster_name,
   398→            member_ids=member_ids,
   399→            description=description,
   400→            action=action,
   401→            now=now,
   402→        )
   403→
   404→    return changes
   405→
   406→
   407→def _sync_subjective_clusters(
   408→    plan: PlanModel,
   409→    state: StateModel,
   410→    findings: dict,
   411→    clusters: dict,
   412→    existing_by_key: dict[str, str],
   413→    active_auto_keys: set[str],
   414→    now: str,
   415→    *,
   416→    target_strict: float,
   417→) -> int:
   418→    """Sync unscored, stale, and under-target subjective dimension clusters."""
   419→    changes = 0
   420→
   421→    all_subjective_ids = sorted(
   422→        fid for fid in plan.get("queue_order", [])
   423→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   424→    )
   425→    unscored_state_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   426→    unscored_queue_ids = sorted(
   427→        fid for fid in all_subjective_ids if fid in unscored_state_ids
   428→    )
   429→    stale_state_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   430→    stale_queue_ids = sorted(
   431→        fid for fid in all_subjective_ids
   432→        if fid in stale_state_ids and fid not in unscored_state_ids
   433→    )
   434→
   435→    # -- Initial review cluster (unscored, min size 1) ---------------------
   436→    if len(unscored_queue_ids) >= _MIN_UNSCORED_CLUSTER_SIZE:
   437→        active_auto_keys.add(_UNSCORED_KEY)
   438→        cli_keys = [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in unscored_queue_ids]
   439→        description = f"Initial review of {len(unscored_queue_ids)} unscored subjective dimensions"
   440→        action = f"desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {','.join(cli_keys)}"
   441→        changes += _sync_auto_cluster(
   442→            plan, clusters, existing_by_key,
   443→            cluster_key=_UNSCORED_KEY,
   444→            cluster_name=_UNSCORED_NAME,
   445→            member_ids=unscored_queue_ids,
   446→            description=description,
   447→            action=action,
   448→            now=now,
   449→        )
   450→
   451→    # -- Stale review cluster (previously scored, min size 2) --------------
   452→    if len(stale_queue_ids) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE:
   453→        active_auto_keys.add(_STALE_KEY)
   454→        cli_keys = [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in stale_queue_ids]
   455→        description = f"Re-review {len(stale_queue_ids)} stale subjective dimensions"
   456→        action = (
   457→            "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions "
   458→            + ",".join(cli_keys)
   459→        )
   460→        changes += _sync_auto_cluster(
   461→            plan, clusters, existing_by_key,
   462→            cluster_key=_STALE_KEY,
   463→            cluster_name=_STALE_NAME,
   464→            member_ids=stale_queue_ids,
   465→            description=description,
   466→            action=action,
   467→            now=now,
   468→        )
   469→
   470→    # -- Under-target review cluster (optional, current but below target) ----
   471→    under_target_ids = current_under_target_ids(state, target_strict=target_strict)
   472→    under_target_queue_ids = sorted(under_target_ids)
   473→
   474→    # Prune: remove IDs that were previously in the under-target cluster
   475→    # but are no longer under target (they've improved above threshold).
   476→    prev_ut_cluster = clusters.get(_UNDER_TARGET_NAME, {})
   477→    prev_ut_ids = set(prev_ut_cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   478→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   479→    _ut_prune = [
   480→        fid for fid in prev_ut_ids
   481→        if fid not in under_target_ids
   482→        and fid not in stale_state_ids
   483→        and fid not in unscored_state_ids
   484→        and fid in order
   485→    ]
   486→    for fid in _ut_prune:
   487→        order.remove(fid)
   488→        changes += 1
   489→
   490→    # Guard: only inject under-target items when no objective findings
   491→    # remain open — mirror the guard used by sync_stale_dimensions().
   492→    has_objective_items = any(
   493→        f.get("status") == "open"
   494→        and f.get("detector") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   495→        and not f.get("suppressed")
   496→        for f in findings.values()
   497→    )
   498→
   499→    if not has_objective_items and len(under_target_queue_ids) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE:
   500→        active_auto_keys.add(_UNDER_TARGET_KEY)
   501→        cli_keys = [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in under_target_queue_ids]
   502→        description = (
   503→            f"Consider re-reviewing {len(under_target_queue_ids)} "
   504→            f"dimensions under target score"
   505→        )
   506→        action = (
   507→            "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions "
   508→            + ",".join(cli_keys)
   509→        )
   510→        changes += _sync_auto_cluster(
   511→            plan, clusters, existing_by_key,
   512→            [REDACTED],
   513→            cluster_name=_UNDER_TARGET_NAME,
   514→            member_ids=under_target_queue_ids,
   515→            description=description,
   516→            action=action,
   517→            now=now,
   518→            optional=True,
   519→        )
   520→
   521→        # Ensure under-target IDs are in queue_order (at the back)
   522→        order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   523→        existing_order = set(order)
   524→        for fid in under_target_queue_ids:
   525→            if fid not in existing_order:
   526→                order.append(fid)
   527→
   528→    # Evict under-target IDs from queue when objective backlog has returned.
   529→    if has_objective_items:
   530→        _objective_evict = [
   531→            fid for fid in order
   532→            if fid in under_target_ids
   533→        ]
   534→        for fid in _objective_evict:
   535→            order.remove(fid)
   536→            changes += 1
   537→
   538→    return changes
   539→
   540→
   541→def _prune_stale_clusters(
   542→    plan: PlanModel,
   543→    findings: dict,
   544→    clusters: dict,
   545→    active_auto_keys: set[str],
   546→    now: str,
   547→) -> int:
   548→    """Delete auto-clusters that no longer have matching groups."""
   549→    changes = 0
   550→    for name in list(clusters.keys()):
   551→        cluster = clusters[name]
   552→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   553→            continue
   554→        ck = cluster.get("cluster_key", "")
   555→        if ck in active_auto_keys:
   556→            continue
   557→        if cluster.get("user_modified"):
   558→            # Keep user-modified clusters but prune dead member IDs
   559→            alive = [fid for fid in cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
   560→                     if fid in findings and findings[fid].get("status") == "open"]
   561→            if alive:
   562→                if len(alive) != len(cluster.get("finding_ids", [])):
   563→                    cluster["finding_ids"] = alive
   564→                    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   565→                    changes += 1
   566→                continue
   567→            # All members gone — delete even if user_modified
   568→        # Delete stale cluster
   569→        del clusters[name]
   570→        # Clear cluster refs from overrides
   571→        for fid in cluster.get("finding_ids", []):
   572→            override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(fid)
   573→            if override and override.get("cluster") == name:
   574→                override["cluster"] = None
   575→                override["updated_at"] = now
   576→        if plan.get("active_cluster") == name:
   577→            plan["active_cluster"] = None
   578→        changes += 1
   579→    return changes
   580→
   581→

   598→    existing_by_key: dict[str, str] = {}  # cluster_key → cluster_name
   599→    for name, cluster in list(clusters.items()):
   600→        if cluster.get("auto"):

1→"""Canonical scorecard projection helpers shared across app/engine code."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine.planning.dimension_rows import (
     8→    scorecard_dimension_rows,
     9→)
    10→from desloppify.scoring import DISPLAY_NAMES
    11→
    12→_COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION: dict[str, str] = {
    13→    "abstraction leverage": "abstraction_fitness",
    14→    "indirection cost": "abstraction_fitness",
    15→    "interface honesty": "abstraction_fitness",
    16→}
    17→
    18→
    19→def dimension_cli_key(dimension_name: str) -> str:
    20→    """Map a display name to a review --dimensions CLI key."""
    21→    cleaned = dimension_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
    22→    lowered = cleaned.lower()
    23→    if lowered in _COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION:
    24→        return _COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION[lowered]
    25→    for key, display in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
    26→        if display.lower() == lowered:
    27→            return key
    28→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]+", "_", lowered).strip("_")
    29→
    30→
    31→def scorecard_dimension_cli_keys(name: str, data: dict) -> list[str]:
    32→    """Map scorecard subjective display names to review --dimensions keys."""
    33→    if name in ("Elegance", "Elegance (combined)"):
    34→        components = (
    35→            data.get("detectors", {})
    36→            .get("subjective_assessment", {})
    37→            .get("components", [])
    38→        )
    39→        if isinstance(components, list) and components:
    40→            keys = [dimension_cli_key(component) for component in components]
    41→        else:
    42→            keys = [
    43→                "high_level_elegance",
    44→                "mid_level_elegance",
    45→                "low_level_elegance",
    46→            ]
    47→    elif name in ("Abstraction fit", "Abstraction fit (combined)"):
    48→        components = (
    49→            data.get("detectors", {})
    50→            .get("subjective_assessment", {})
    51→            .get("components", [])
    52→        )
    53→        if isinstance(components, list) and components:
    54→            keys = [dimension_cli_key(component) for component in components]
    55→        else:
    56→            keys = ["abstraction_fitness"]
    57→    else:
    58→        keys = [dimension_cli_key(name)]
    59→
    60→    seen: set[str] = set()
    61→    ordered: list[str] = []
    62→    for key in keys:
    63→        if not key or key in seen:
    64→            continue
    65→        ordered.append(key)
    66→        seen.add(key)
    67→    return ordered
    68→
    69→
    70→def scorecard_subjective_entries(
    71→    state: dict,
    72→    *,
    73→    dim_scores: dict | None = None,
    74→) -> list[dict]:
    75→    """Return scorecard-subjective entries with score/strict/placeholder metadata."""
    76→    rows = scorecard_dimension_rows(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
    77→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments") or {}
    78→    subjective_display_names = {display.lower() for display in DISPLAY_NAMES.values()}
    79→    subjective_display_names.update({"elegance", "elegance (combined)"})
    80→
    81→    entries: list[dict] = []
    82→    for name, data in rows:
    83→        detectors = data.get("detectors", {})
    84→        lowered_name = str(name).strip().lower()
    85→        is_subjective = (
    86→            "subjective_assessment" in detectors
    87→            or lowered_name in subjective_display_names
    88→        )
    89→        if not is_subjective:
    90→            continue
    91→        score = float(data.get("score", 0.0))
    92→        strict = float(data.get("strict", score))
    93→        assessment_meta = detectors.get("subjective_assessment", {})
    94→        placeholder = bool(
    95→            assessment_meta.get("placeholder")
    96→            or (
    97→                data.get("score", 0) == 0
    98→                and data.get("issues", 0) == 0
    99→                and data.get("checks", 0) == 0
   100→            )
   101→        )
   102→        [REDACTED]("dimension_key", "")
   103→        stale = bool(
   104→            dim_key
   105→            and isinstance(assessments.get(dim_key), dict)
   106→            and assessments[dim_key].get("needs_review_refresh")
   107→        )
   108→        entries.append(
   109→            {
   110→                "name": name,
   111→                "score": score,
   112→                "strict": strict,
   113→                "checks": int(data.get("checks", 0) or 0),
   114→                "issues": int(data.get("issues", 0) or 0),
   115→                "tier": int(data.get("tier", 4) or 4),
   116→                "placeholder": placeholder,
   117→                "stale": stale,
   118→                "dimension_key": dim_key,
   119→                "cli_keys": scorecard_dimension_cli_keys(name, data),
   120→            }
   121→        )
   122→    return entries
   123→
   124→
   125→def scorecard_dimensions_payload(
   126→    state: dict,
   127→    *,
   128→    dim_scores: dict | None = None,
   129→) -> list[dict]:
   130→    """Serialize scorecard rows for JSON/query outputs."""
   131→    subjective_by_name = {
   132→        entry["name"]: entry
   133→        for entry in scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   134→    }
   135→    payload: list[dict] = []
   136→    for name, data in scorecard_dimension_rows(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
   137→        score = float(data.get("score", 0.0))
   138→        strict = float(data.get("strict", score))
   139→        subjective = name in subjective_by_name
   140→        entry = {
   141→            "name": name,
   142→            "score": score,
   143→            "strict": strict,
   144→            "checks": int(data.get("checks", 0) or 0),
   145→            "issues": int(data.get("issues", 0) or 0),
   146→            "tier": int(
   147→                data.get("tier", 4 if subjective else 3) or (4 if subjective else 3)
   148→            ),
   149→            "subjective": subjective,
   150→        }
   151→        if subjective:
   152→            sub = subjective_by_name[name]
   153→            entry["placeholder"] = bool(sub.get("placeholder"))
   154→            entry["cli_keys"] = list(sub.get("cli_keys", []))
   155→        payload.append(entry)
   156→    return payload
   157→
   158→
   159→__all__ = [
   160→    "dimension_cli_key",
   161→    "scorecard_dimension_cli_keys",
   162→    "scorecard_dimension_rows",
   163→    "scorecard_dimensions_payload",
   164→    "scorecard_subjective_entries",
   165→]
   166→

1→"""Post-scan plan reconciliation — handle finding churn."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass, field

     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import EPIC_PREFIX, PlanModel, SupersededEntry, ensure_plan_defaults
     9→from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES
    10→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, utc_now
    11→
    12→SUPERSEDED_TTL_DAYS = 90
    13→
    14→
    15→@dataclass
    16→class ReconcileResult:
    17→    """Summary of changes made during reconciliation."""
    18→
    19→    superseded: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    20→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    21→    resurfaced: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    22→    changes: int = 0
    23→
    24→
    25→def _find_candidates(
    26→    state: StateModel, detector: str, file: str
    27→) -> list[str]:
    28→    """Find open findings that could be remaps for a disappeared finding."""
    29→    candidates: list[str] = []
    30→    for fid, finding in state.get("findings", {}).items():
    31→        if finding.get("status") != "open":
    32→            continue
    33→        if finding.get("detector") == detector and finding.get("file") == file:
    34→            candidates.append(fid)
    35→    return candidates
    36→
    37→
    38→def _is_finding_alive(state: StateModel, finding_id: str) -> bool:
    39→    """Return True if the finding exists and is open."""
    40→    finding = state.get("findings", {}).get(finding_id)
    41→    if finding is None:
    42→        return False
    43→    return finding.get("status") == "open"
    44→
    45→
    46→def _supersede_id(
    47→    plan: PlanModel,
    48→    state: StateModel,
    49→    finding_id: str,
    50→    now: str,
    51→) -> bool:
    52→    """Move a disappeared finding to superseded. Returns True if changed."""
    53→    finding = state.get("findings", {}).get(finding_id)
    54→    detector = ""
    55→    file = ""
    56→    summary = ""
    57→    if finding:
    58→        detector = finding.get("detector", "")
    59→        file = finding.get("file", "")
    60→        summary = finding.get("summary", "")
    61→
    62→    candidates = _find_candidates(state, detector, file) if detector else []
    63→    # Don't include the original in candidates
    64→    candidates = [c for c in candidates if c != finding_id]
    65→
    66→    entry: SupersededEntry = {
    67→        "original_id": finding_id,
    68→        "original_detector": detector,
    69→        "original_file": file,
    70→        "original_summary": summary,
    71→        "status": "superseded",
    72→        "superseded_at": now,
    73→        "remapped_to": None,
    74→        "candidates": candidates[:5],
    75→    }
    76→
    77→    # Preserve any existing override note
    78→    override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(finding_id)
    79→    if override and override.get("note"):
    80→        entry["note"] = override["note"]
    81→
    82→    plan["superseded"][finding_id] = entry
    83→
    84→    # Remove from queue_order, skipped, promoted_ids, cluster finding_ids
    85→    order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    86→    skipped: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
    87→    promoted: list[str] = plan.get("promoted_ids", [])
    88→    if finding_id in order:
    89→        order.remove(finding_id)
    90→    skipped.pop(finding_id, None)
    91→    if finding_id in promoted:
    92→        promoted.remove(finding_id)
    93→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
    94→        ids = cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
    95→        if finding_id in ids:
    96→            ids.remove(finding_id)
    97→
    98→    # Clear stale cluster reference from override
    99→    override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(finding_id)
   100→    if override and override.get("cluster"):

    40→def _render_dimension_legend(
    41→    scorecard_subjective: list[dict],
    42→    state: dict | None = None,
    43→    *,
    44→    objective_backlog: int = 0,
    45→) -> None:

    49→    )
    50→    print(
    51→        colorize(
    52→            "  Action: fix=auto-fixer | move=reorganize | refactor=manual rewrite | manual=review & fix",
    53→            "dim",
    54→        )
    55→    )
    56→    stale_keys = [
    57→        str(e.get("dimension_key"))
    58→        for e in scorecard_subjective
    59→        if e.get("stale") and e.get("dimension_key")
    60→    ]
    61→    if stale_keys:
    62→        print(
    63→            colorize("  [stale] = assessment outdated", "yellow")
    64→        )
    65→        if objective_backlog <= 0:
    66→            n = len(stale_keys)
    67→            dims_arg = ",".join(stale_keys)
    68→            print(
    69→                colorize(
    70→                    f"  {n} stale dimension{'s' if n != 1 else ''}"
    71→                    f": `desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {dims_arg} --force-review-rerun`",
    72→                    "yellow",
    73→                )
    74→            )
    75→

   106→def show_focus_suggestion(
   107→    dim_scores: dict, state: dict, *, plan: dict | None = None
   108→) -> None:
   109→    """Show the lowest-scoring dimension as the focus area."""
   110→    # When plan has an active focus cluster, show that instead
   111→    if plan and plan.get("active_cluster"):
   112→        cluster_name = plan["active_cluster"]
   113→        cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(cluster_name, {})
   114→        remaining = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   115→        desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
   116→        desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
   117→        print(
   118→            colorize(
   119→                f"  Focus: {cluster_name} ({remaining} items remaining){desc_str}",
   120→                "cyan",
   121→            )
   122→        )
   123→        print()
   124→        return
   125→
   126→    scorecard_subjective = _scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores)
   127→    lowest_name = _find_lowest_dimension(dim_scores, scorecard_subjective)
   128→    if not lowest_name:
   129→        return
   130→
   131→    # Determine kind, score, and issue count from the resolved name
   132→    lowest_kind = None
   133→    lowest_score = 101.0
   134→    lowest_issues = 0
   135→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
   136→        if dim.name == lowest_name:
   137→            ds = dim_scores.get(dim.name)
   138→            if ds:
   139→                lowest_score = float(ds.get("strict", ds["score"]))
   140→                lowest_kind = "mechanical"
   141→                lowest_issues = int(ds.get("issues", 0))
   142→            break
   143→    else:
   144→        for entry in scorecard_subjective:
   145→            if entry.get("name") == lowest_name:
   146→                lowest_score = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   147→                lowest_kind = "subjective"
   148→                lowest_issues = 0
   149→                break
   150→
   151→    if lowest_name and lowest_score < 100:
   152→        if lowest_kind == "subjective":
   153→            print(
   154→                colorize(
   155→                    f"  Focus: {lowest_name} ({lowest_score:.1f}%) — re-review to improve",
   156→                    "cyan",
   157→                )
   158→            )
   159→            print()
   160→            return
   161→
   162→        potentials = merge_potentials(state.get("potentials", {}))
   163→        target_dim = next((d for d in DIMENSIONS if d.name == lowest_name), None)
   164→        if target_dim:
   165→            impact = 0.0
   166→            for det in target_dim.detectors:
   167→                impact = compute_score_impact(
   168→                    {
   169→                        k: {
   170→                            "score": v["score"],
   171→                            "tier": v.get("tier", 3),
   172→                            "detectors": v.get("detectors", {}),
   173→                        }
   174→                        for k, v in dim_scores.items()
   175→                        if "score" in v
   176→                    },
   177→                    potentials,
   178→                    det,
   179→                    lowest_issues,
   180→                )
   181→                if impact > 0:
   182→                    break
   183→
   184→            impact_str = f" for +{impact:.1f} pts" if impact > 0 else ""
   185→            print(
   186→                colorize(
   187→                    f"  Focus: {lowest_name} ({lowest_score:.1f}%) — "
   188→                    f"fix {lowest_issues} items{impact_str}",
   189→                    "cyan",
   190→                )
   191→            )
   192→            print()
   193→
   194→

   196→    state: dict,
   197→    dim_scores: dict,
   198→    *,
   199→    target_strict_score: float,
   200→    objective_backlog: int = 0,

1→"""Helper utilities for work queue item construction."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→from fnmatch import fnmatch
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core.enums import finding_status_tokens
     9→from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
    10→from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    11→    scorecard_subjective_entries,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.intelligence.integrity import (
    14→    is_holistic_subjective_finding,
    15→    unassessed_subjective_dimensions,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.scoring import DISPLAY_NAMES
    18→
    19→ALL_STATUSES = set(finding_status_tokens(include_all=True))
    20→ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY = {"auto_fix": 0, "refactor": 1, "manual_fix": 2, "reorganize": 3}
    21→ATTEST_EXAMPLE = (
    22→    "I have actually [DESCRIBE THE CONCRETE CHANGE YOU MADE] "
    23→    "and I am not gaming the score by resolving without fixing."
    24→)
    25→
    26→
    27→def detail_dict(item: dict) -> dict:
    28→    """Return finding detail as a dict; tolerate legacy/non-dict payloads."""
    29→    detail = item.get("detail")
    30→    return detail if isinstance(detail, dict) else {}
    31→
    32→
    33→def status_matches(item_status: str, status_filter: str) -> bool:
    34→    return status_filter == "all" or item_status == status_filter
    35→
    36→
    37→def is_subjective_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    38→    detector = item.get("detector")
    39→    if detector in {"subjective_assessment"}:
    40→        return True
    41→    if detector == "holistic_review":
    42→        return True
    43→    return False
    44→
    45→
    46→def is_review_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    47→    return item.get("detector") == "review"
    48→
    49→
    50→def is_subjective_queue_item(item: dict) -> bool:
    51→    """True for subjective work items, including collapsed subjective clusters."""
    52→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
    53→        return True
    54→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
    55→        members = item.get("members", [])
    56→        return bool(members) and all(
    57→            m.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" for m in members
    58→        )
    59→    return False
    60→
    61→
    62→def review_finding_weight(item: dict) -> float:
    63→    """Return review issue weight aligned with issues list ordering."""
    64→    confidence = str(item.get("confidence", "low")).lower()
    65→    weight_by_confidence = {
    66→        "high": 1.0,
    67→        "medium": 0.7,
    68→        "low": 0.3,
    69→    }
    70→    weight = weight_by_confidence.get(confidence, 0.3)
    71→    if detail_dict(item).get("holistic"):
    72→        weight *= 10.0
    73→    return float(weight)
    74→
    75→
    76→def scope_matches(item: dict, scope: str | None) -> bool:
    77→    """Apply show-style pattern matching against a queue item."""
    78→    if not scope:
    79→        return True
    80→
    81→    item_id = item.get("id", "")
    82→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
    83→    filepath = item.get("file", "")
    84→    summary = item.get("summary", "")
    85→    dimension = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
    86→    kind = item.get("kind", "")
    87→
    88→    if "*" in scope:
    89→        return any(
    90→            fnmatch(candidate, scope)
    91→            for candidate in (item_id, filepath, detector, dimension, summary)
    92→        )
    93→
    94→    if "::" in scope:
    95→        return item_id.startswith(scope)
    96→
    97→    lowered = scope.lower()
    98→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
    99→        return (
   100→            lowered in item_id.lower()
   101→            or lowered in dimension.lower()
   102→            or lowered in summary.lower()
   103→        )
   104→
   105→    # Hash suffix: 8+ hex chars matches the tail segment of a finding ID.
   106→    if len(lowered) >= 8 and re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]+", lowered):
   107→        return item_id.lower().endswith("::" + lowered)
   108→
   109→    return (
   110→        detector == scope
   111→        or filepath == scope
   112→        or filepath.startswith(scope.rstrip("/") + "/")
   113→    )
   114→
   115→
   116→def slugify(text: str) -> str:
   117→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9_]+", "_", text.lower()).strip("_")
   118→
   119→
   120→def _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(display_name: str) -> str:
   121→    """Map a display label (e.g. 'Mid elegance') to its canonical dimension key."""
   122→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   123→    target = cleaned.lower()
   124→
   125→    for dim_key, label in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
   126→        if str(label).lower() == target:
   127→            return str(dim_key)
   128→    return slugify(cleaned)
   129→
   130→
   131→def _subjective_dimension_aliases(display_name: str) -> set[str]:
   132→    """Return normalized aliases used to match display labels with finding dimension keys."""
   133→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   134→    canonical = _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(cleaned)
   135→    return {
   136→        cleaned.lower(),
   137→        cleaned.replace(" ", "_").lower(),
   138→        slugify(cleaned),
   139→        canonical.lower(),
   140→        slugify(canonical),
   141→    }
   142→
   143→
   144→def supported_fixers_for_item(state: dict, item: dict) -> set[str] | None:
   145→    """Return supported fixers for an item's language when known."""
   146→    lang = str(item.get("lang", "") or "").strip()
   147→    if not lang:
   148→        return None
   149→
   150→    caps = state.get("lang_capabilities", {})
   151→    if not isinstance(caps, dict):
   152→        return None
   153→
   154→    lang_caps = caps.get(lang, {})
   155→    if not isinstance(lang_caps, dict):
   156→        return None
   157→
   158→    fixers = lang_caps.get("fixers")
   159→    if not isinstance(fixers, list):
   160→        return None
   161→    return {fixer for fixer in fixers if isinstance(fixer, str)}
   162→
   163→
   164→def primary_command_for_finding(
   165→    item: dict, *, supported_fixers: set[str] | None = None
   166→) -> str:
   167→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
   168→    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   169→    if meta and meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and meta.fixers:
   170→        available_fixers = [
   171→            fixer
   172→            for fixer in meta.fixers
   173→            if supported_fixers is not None and fixer in supported_fixers
   174→        ]
   175→        if available_fixers:
   176→            return f"desloppify fix {available_fixers[0]} --dry-run"
   177→    if detector == "subjective_review":
   178→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   179→            return "desloppify review --prepare"
   180→        return "desloppify show subjective"
   181→    return f'desloppify plan done "{item.get("id", "")}" --note "<what you did>" --confirm'
   182→
   183→
   184→def build_triage_stage_items(plan: dict, state: dict) -> list[dict]:
   185→    """Build synthetic work items for each ``triage::*`` stage ID in the queue.
   186→
   187→    Returns an empty list when no triage stages are pending.
   188→    """
   189→    from desloppify.app.commands.plan.triage_playbook import (
   190→        TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES,
   191→        TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS,
   192→    )
   193→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (
   194→        TRIAGE_IDS,
   195→        TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS,
   196→    )
   197→
   198→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   199→    order_set = set(order)
   200→    present = order_set & TRIAGE_IDS
```

1→"""Unified work-queue selection for next/show/plan views."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from typing import TypedDict
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
     9→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
    10→    ALL_STATUSES,
    11→    ATTEST_EXAMPLE,
    12→    build_create_plan_item,
    13→    build_subjective_items,
    14→    build_triage_stage_items,

    19→    enrich_with_impact,

    21→    item_explain,
    22→    item_sort_key,
    23→)

    26→    collapse_clusters as _collapse_clusters,
    27→    new_item_ids as _new_item_ids,
    28→)
    29→from desloppify.state import StateModel
    30→
    31→
    32→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    33→class QueueBuildOptions:
    34→    """Configuration for queue construction."""
    35→
    36→    count: int | None = 1
    37→    scan_path: str | None = None
    38→    scope: str | None = None
    39→    status: str = "open"
    40→    include_subjective: bool = True
    41→    subjective_threshold: float = 100.0
    42→    chronic: bool = False
    43→    explain: bool = False
    44→    plan: dict | None = None
    45→    include_skipped: bool = False
    46→    cluster: str | None = None
    47→    collapse_clusters: bool = True
    48→
    49→
    50→class WorkQueueResult(TypedDict):
    51→    """Typed shape of the dict returned by :func:`build_work_queue`."""
    52→
    53→    items: list[dict]
    54→    total: int
    55→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]]
    56→    new_ids: set[str]
    57→
    58→
    59→def build_work_queue(
    60→    state: StateModel,
    61→    *,
    62→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
    63→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    64→    """Build ranked queue items + tier metadata."""
    65→    resolved_options = options or QueueBuildOptions()
    66→
    67→    status = resolved_options.status
    68→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
    69→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
    70→    try:
    71→        subjective_threshold_value = float(resolved_options.subjective_threshold)
    72→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    73→        subjective_threshold_value = 100.0
    74→    subjective_threshold_value = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold_value))
    75→
    76→    finding_items = build_finding_items(
    77→        state,
    78→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
    79→        status_filter=status,
    80→        scope=resolved_options.scope,
    81→        chronic=resolved_options.chronic,
    82→    )
    83→
    84→    all_items = list(finding_items)
    85→
    86→    # Count open objective findings for stale-subjective gating
    87→    objective_count = sum(
    88→        1 for item in finding_items
    89→        if item.get("detector") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
    90→    )
    91→
    92→    if (
    93→        resolved_options.include_subjective
    94→        and status in {"open", "all"}
    95→        and not resolved_options.chronic
    96→    ):
    97→        subjective_items = build_subjective_items(
    98→            state,

   100→            threshold=subjective_threshold_value,
   101→        )
   102→        for item in subjective_items:
   103→            if not scope_matches(item, resolved_options.scope):
   104→                continue

   106→            # backlog is drained — stale reruns AND under-target dims
   107→            # should wait until all mechanical work is done.
   108→            if not item.get("initial_review") and objective_count > 0:
   109→                continue
   110→            all_items.append(item)

   113→    if resolved_options.plan and status in {"open", "all"}:
   114→        synth_items = build_triage_stage_items(resolved_options.plan, state)
   115→        all_items.extend(synth_items)
   116→        plan_item = build_create_plan_item(resolved_options.plan)
   117→        if plan_item is not None:
   118→            all_items.append(plan_item)
   119→
   120→    enrich_with_impact(all_items, state.get("dimension_scores", {}))
   121→    all_items.sort(key=item_sort_key)

132→def reconcile_plan_after_scan(
   133→    plan: PlanModel,
   134→    state: StateModel,
   135→) -> ReconcileResult:
   136→    """Reconcile plan against current state after a scan.
   137→
   138→    Finds IDs referenced in the plan that no longer exist or are no longer
   139→    open, moves them to superseded, and prunes old superseded entries.
   140→    """
   141→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   142→    result = ReconcileResult()
   143→    now = utc_now()
   144→    now_dt = datetime.now(UTC)
   145→
   146→    # Collect all finding IDs referenced by the plan
   147→    referenced_ids: set[str] = set()
   148→    referenced_ids.update(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   149→    referenced_ids.update(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   150→    for override_id in plan.get("overrides", {}):
   151→        referenced_ids.add(override_id)
   152→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   153→        referenced_ids.update(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   154→
   155→    # Exclude already-superseded IDs and synthetic IDs (managed by stale_dimensions)
   156→    already_superseded = set(plan.get("superseded", {}).keys())
   157→    referenced_ids -= already_superseded
   158→    referenced_ids = {
   159→        fid for fid in referenced_ids
   160→        if not any(fid.startswith(prefix) for prefix in SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES)
   161→    }
   162→
   163→    # Check each referenced ID
   164→    for fid in sorted(referenced_ids):
   165→        if not _is_finding_alive(state, fid):
   166→            if _supersede_id(plan, state, fid, now):
   167→                result.superseded.append(fid)
   168→                result.changes += 1
   169→
   170→    # Reconcile epic clusters: remove dead findings, delete empty epics
   171→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   172→    epic_names_to_delete: list[str] = []
   173→    for name, cluster in list(clusters.items()):
   174→        if not name.startswith(EPIC_PREFIX):
   175→            continue
   176→        finding_ids = cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
   177→        alive_ids = [fid for fid in finding_ids if _is_finding_alive(state, fid)]
   178→        if alive_ids != finding_ids:
   179→            cluster["finding_ids"] = alive_ids
   180→            result.changes += 1
   181→        if not alive_ids:
   182→            epic_names_to_delete.append(name)
   183→    for name in epic_names_to_delete:
   184→        clusters.pop(name, None)
   185→        result.changes += 1
   186→
   187→    # Resurface stale temporary skips
   188→    scan_count = state.get("scan_count", 0)
   189→    from desloppify.engine._plan.operations import resurface_stale_skips
   190→
   191→    resurfaced = resurface_stale_skips(plan, scan_count)
   192→    if resurfaced:
   193→        result.resurfaced = resurfaced
   194→        result.changes += len(resurfaced)
   195→
   196→    # Prune old superseded entries
   197→    pruned = _prune_old_superseded(plan, now_dt)
   198→    result.pruned = pruned
   199→    result.changes += len(pruned)
   200→
   201→    # Log reconciliation if any changes were made
   202→    if result.changes > 0:
   203→        from desloppify.engine._plan.operations import append_log_entry
   204→
   205→        append_log_entry(
   206→            plan,
   207→            "reconcile",
   208→            finding_ids=result.superseded,
   209→            actor="system",
   210→            detail={
   211→                "superseded_count": len(result.superseded),
   212→                "pruned_count": len(result.pruned),
   213→                "resurfaced_count": len(result.resurfaced),
   214→            },
   215→        )
   216→
   217→    return result
   218→
   219→
   220→@dataclass
   221→class ReviewImportSyncResult:
   222→    """Summary of plan changes after a review import."""
   223→
   224→    new_ids: set[str]
   225→    added_to_queue: list[str]
   226→    triage_injected: bool
   227→
   228→
   229→def sync_plan_after_review_import(
   230→    plan: PlanModel,
   231→    state: StateModel,
   232→) -> ReviewImportSyncResult | None:
   233→    """Sync plan queue after review import. Pure engine function — no I/O.
   234→
   235→    Appends new finding IDs to queue_order and injects triage stages
   236→    if needed. Returns None when there are no new findings to sync.
   237→    """
   238→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (
   239→        compute_new_finding_ids,
   240→        sync_triage_needed,
   241→    )
   242→
   243→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   244→    new_ids = compute_new_finding_ids(plan, state)
   245→    if not new_ids:
   246→        return None
   247→
   248→    # Add new finding IDs to end of queue_order so they have position
   249→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   250→    existing = set(order)
   251→    added: list[str] = []
   252→    for fid in sorted(new_ids):
   253→        if fid not in existing:
   254→            order.append(fid)
   255→            added.append(fid)
   256→
   257→    # Inject triage stages if needed
   258→    triage_result = sync_triage_needed(plan, state)
   259→    triage_injected = bool(
   260→        triage_result and getattr(triage_result, "injected", False)
   261→    )
   262→
   263→    return ReviewImportSyncResult(
   264→        new_ids=new_ids,
   265→        added_to_queue=added,
   266→        triage_injected=triage_injected,
   267→    )
   268→
   269→
   270→__all__ = [
   271→    "ReconcileResult",
   272→    "ReviewImportSyncResult",
   273→    "reconcile_plan_after_scan",
   274→    "sync_plan_after_review_import",
   275→]
   276→

1→"""Ranking and grouping helpers for work queue selection."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
     6→    ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY,
     7→    detail_dict,
     8→    is_review_finding,
     9→    is_subjective_finding,
    10→    primary_command_for_finding,
    11→    review_finding_weight,
    12→    scope_matches,
    13→    slugify,
    14→    status_matches,
    15→    subjective_strict_scores,
    16→    supported_fixers_for_item,
    17→)
    18→from desloppify.engine.planning.common import CONFIDENCE_ORDER
    19→from desloppify.state import path_scoped_findings
    20→
    21→
    22→def enrich_with_impact(items: list[dict], dimension_scores: dict) -> None:
    23→    """Stamp ``estimated_impact`` on each item based on dimension-level headroom.
    24→
    25→    Impact = ``overall_per_point * headroom`` where headroom = ``100 - score``.
    26→    Items in dimensions with more score headroom sort first.
    27→    """
    28→    if not dimension_scores:
    29→        for item in items:
    30→            item["estimated_impact"] = 0.0
    31→        return
    32→
    33→    from desloppify.engine._scoring.results.health import compute_health_breakdown
    34→    from desloppify.engine._scoring.results.impact import get_dimension_for_detector
    35→
    36→    breakdown = compute_health_breakdown(dimension_scores)
    37→    entries = breakdown.get("entries", [])
    38→
    39→    # Build lookup: normalized dimension name -> {per_point, headroom}
    40→    dim_impact: dict[str, dict[str, float]] = {}
    41→    for entry in entries:
    42→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
    43→        if not name:
    44→            continue
    45→        per_point = float(entry.get("overall_per_point", 0.0))
    46→        score = float(entry.get("score", 0.0))
    47→        headroom = 100.0 - score
    48→        dim_impact[name.lower()] = {"per_point": per_point, "headroom": headroom}
    49→
    50→    for item in items:
    51→        impact = _compute_item_impact(item, dim_impact, get_dimension_for_detector)
    52→        item["estimated_impact"] = impact
    53→
    54→
    55→def _compute_item_impact(
    56→    item: dict,
    57→    dim_impact: dict[str, dict[str, float]],
    58→    get_dimension_for_detector,
    59→) -> float:
    60→    """Compute impact value for a single queue item."""
    61→    kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
    62→
    63→    # Subjective items (synthetic dimensions or subjective findings):
    64→    # look up by detail.dimension_name
    65→    if kind == "subjective_dimension" or item.get("is_subjective"):
    66→        dim_name = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
    67→        entry = dim_impact.get(dim_name.lower())
    68→        if entry:
    69→            return entry["per_point"] * entry["headroom"]
    70→        return 0.0
    71→
    72→    # Mechanical findings: use detector -> dimension mapping
    73→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
    74→    if detector:
    75→        dimension = get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
    76→        if dimension:
    77→            entry = dim_impact.get(dimension.name.lower())
    78→            if entry:
    79→                return entry["per_point"] * entry["headroom"]
    80→
    81→    return 0.0
    82→
    83→
    84→def subjective_score_value(item: dict) -> float:
    85→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
    86→        detail = detail_dict(item)
    87→        return float(detail.get("strict_score", item.get("subjective_score", 100.0)))
    88→    return float(item.get("subjective_score", 100.0))
    89→
    90→
    91→def build_finding_items(
    92→    state: dict,
    93→    *,
    94→    scan_path: str | None,
    95→    status_filter: str,
    96→    scope: str | None,
    97→    chronic: bool,
    98→) -> list[dict]:
    99→    scoped = path_scoped_findings(state.get("findings", {}), scan_path)
   100→    subjective_scores = subjective_strict_scores(state)
   101→    out: list[dict] = []
   102→
   103→    for finding_id, finding in scoped.items():
   104→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
   105→            continue
   106→        if not status_matches(finding.get("status", "open"), status_filter):
   107→            continue
   108→        if chronic and not (
   109→            finding.get("status") == "open" and finding.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2
   110→        ):
   111→            continue
   112→
   113→        item = dict(finding)
   114→        item["id"] = finding_id
   115→        item["kind"] = "finding"
   116→        item["is_review"] = is_review_finding(item)
   117→        item["is_subjective"] = is_subjective_finding(item)
   118→        item["review_weight"] = (
   119→            review_finding_weight(item) if item["is_review"] else None
   120→        )
   121→        subjective_score = None
   122→        if item["is_subjective"]:
   123→            detail = detail_dict(finding)
   124→            dim_name = detail.get("dimension_name", "")
   125→            dim_key = detail.get("dimension", "") or slugify(dim_name)
   126→            subjective_score = subjective_scores.get(
   127→                dim_key, subjective_scores.get(dim_name.lower(), 100.0)
   128→            )
   129→        item["subjective_score"] = subjective_score
   130→        supported_fixers = supported_fixers_for_item(state, item)
   131→        item["primary_command"] = primary_command_for_finding(
   132→            item,
   133→            supported_fixers=supported_fixers,
   134→        )
   135→
   136→        if not scope_matches(item, scope):
   137→            continue
   138→        out.append(item)
   139→
   140→    return out
   141→
   142→
   143→def item_sort_key(item: dict) -> tuple:
   144→    kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   145→
   146→    # Initial-review subjective items: highest priority (tier -3)
   147→    if kind == "subjective_dimension" and item.get("initial_review"):
   148→        return (-3, 0, subjective_score_value(item), item.get("id", ""))
   149→
   150→    # Triage stage items: tier -2, stage order, blocked after unblocked
```

1→"""Shared subjective follow-up rendering helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Protocol
     6→
     7→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     8→
     9→
    10→class SubjectiveFollowup(Protocol):
    11→    """Contract for objects passed to print_subjective_followup."""
    12→
    13→    low_assessed: object  # falsy when no dimensions are below threshold
    14→    threshold_label: str
    15→    rendered: str
    16→    command: str
    17→    integrity_lines: list[tuple[str, str]]
    18→
    19→
    20→def print_subjective_followup(
    21→    followup: SubjectiveFollowup,
    22→    *,
    23→    leading_newline: bool = False,
    24→    objective_backlog: int = 0,
    25→) -> bool:
    26→    """Render common subjective quality/integrity guidance lines.
    27→
    28→    When *objective_backlog* > 0, quality nudges are suppressed entirely — the
    29→    queue already surfaces subjective work when it becomes actionable.
    30→    Integrity warnings are always shown (anti-gaming safeguard).
    31→
    32→    Returns True when any line is rendered.
    33→    """
    34→    printed = False
    35→    prefix = "\n" if leading_newline else ""
    36→    if followup.low_assessed and objective_backlog <= 0:
    37→        print(
    38→            colorize(
    39→                f"{prefix}  Subjective quality (<{followup.threshold_label}%): "
    40→                f"{followup.rendered}",
    41→                "cyan",
    42→            )
    43→        )
    44→        print(
    45→            colorize(
    46→                f"  Next command to improve subjective scores: {followup.command}",
    47→                "dim",
    48→            )
    49→        )
    50→        printed = True
    51→
    52→    if followup.integrity_lines:
    53→        if printed:
    54→            print()
    55→        for style, message in followup.integrity_lines:
    56→            print(colorize(f"  {message}", style))
    57→        printed = True
    58→    return printed
    59→
    60→
    61→__all__ = ["print_subjective_followup"]
    62→

1→"""Regression tests for issue #181: subjective nudges gated on objective backlog.
     2→
     3→When the objective queue is non-empty, subjective refresh commands are
     4→suppressed entirely — the queue already surfaces them when actionable.
     5→When the objective queue is empty, actionable commands appear normally.
     6→Integrity warnings always render regardless of backlog.
     7→"""
     8→
     9→from __future__ import annotations
    10→
    11→from types import SimpleNamespace
    12→
    13→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers import subjective as subjective_mod
    14→from desloppify.app.commands.status_parts import render as status_render
    15→
    16→
    17→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    18→# Helpers
    19→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    20→
    21→def _make_followup(**overrides):
    22→    payload = {
    23→        "low_assessed": True,
    24→        "threshold_label": "95",
    25→        "rendered": "dim_a, dim_b",
    26→        "command": "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions dim_a",
    27→        "integrity_lines": [],
    28→    }
    29→    payload.update(overrides)
    30→    return SimpleNamespace(**payload)
    31→
    32→
    33→def _subjective_entry(dimension_key: str, *, stale: bool = False, score: float = 80.0):
    34→    return {
    35→        "name": dimension_key.replace("_", " ").title(),
    36→        "dimension_key": dimension_key,
    37→        "score": score,
    38→        "strict": score,
    39→        "stale": stale,
    40→        "placeholder": False,
    41→    }
    42→
    43→
    44→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    45→# print_subjective_followup — suppressed vs actionable
    46→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    47→
    48→class TestPrintSubjectiveFollowup:
    49→    """Test the shared subjective followup renderer."""
    50→
    51→    def test_actionable_when_no_backlog(self, monkeypatch, capsys):
    52→        monkeypatch.setattr(subjective_mod, "colorize", lambda text, _s: text)
    53→        followup = _make_followup()
    54→        subjective_mod.print_subjective_followup(followup, objective_backlog=0)
    55→        out = capsys.readouterr().out
    56→        assert "Next command to improve subjective scores" in out
    57→
    58→    def test_suppressed_when_backlog_exists(self, monkeypatch, capsys):
    59→        monkeypatch.setattr(subjective_mod, "colorize", lambda text, _s: text)
    60→        followup = _make_followup()
    61→        subjective_mod.print_subjective_followup(followup, objective_backlog=100)
    62→        out = capsys.readouterr().out
    63→        assert "Next command to improve subjective scores" not in out
    64→        assert "Subjective quality" not in out
    65→
    66→    def test_integrity_lines_always_shown(self, monkeypatch, capsys):
    67→        """Integrity warnings must appear regardless of backlog."""
    68→        monkeypatch.setattr(subjective_mod, "colorize", lambda text, _s: text)
    69→        followup = _make_followup(
    70→            low_assessed=False,
    71→            integrity_lines=[("yellow", "Integrity alert: check review coverage")],
    72→        )
    73→        subjective_mod.print_subjective_followup(followup, objective_backlog=500)
    74→        out = capsys.readouterr().out
    75→        assert "Integrity alert" in out
    76→
    77→    def test_default_backlog_zero_backward_compat(self, monkeypatch, capsys):
    78→        """Calling without objective_backlog should behave as before (actionable)."""
    79→        monkeypatch.setattr(subjective_mod, "colorize", lambda text, _s: text)
    80→        followup = _make_followup()
    81→        subjective_mod.print_subjective_followup(followup)
    82→        out = capsys.readouterr().out
    83→        assert "Next command to improve subjective scores" in out
    84→
    85→
    86→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    87→# _render_dimension_legend — stale commands gated
    88→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    89→
    90→class TestRenderDimensionLegend:
    91→    """Test the stale-dimension legend in the status dimension table."""
    92→
    93→    def test_stale_command_shown_when_no_backlog(self, monkeypatch, capsys):
    94→        monkeypatch.setattr(status_render, "colorize", lambda text, _s: text)
    95→        entries = [_subjective_entry("design_coherence", stale=True)]
    96→        status_render._render_dimension_legend(entries, objective_backlog=0)
    97→        out = capsys.readouterr().out
    98→        assert "force-review-rerun" in out
    99→
   100→    def test_stale_tag_only_when_backlog_exists(self, monkeypatch, capsys):

   102→        entries = [_subjective_entry("design_coherence", stale=True)]

1→"""Dimension-table helpers for status rendering."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.app.commands.scan.scan_reporting_presentation import dimension_bar
     6→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     7→from desloppify.core.registry import dimension_action_type
     8→from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
     9→    scorecard_subjective_entries,
    10→)
    11→from desloppify.scoring import DIMENSIONS
    12→
    13→
    14→def scorecard_subjective_entries_for_status(state: dict, dim_scores: dict) -> list[dict]:
    15→    """Return subjective entries aligned to scorecard labels and ordering."""
    16→    return scorecard_subjective_entries(
    17→        state,
    18→        dim_scores=dim_scores,
    19→    )
    20→
    21→
    22→def find_lowest_dimension(
    23→    dim_scores: dict,
    24→    scorecard_subjective: list[dict],
    25→) -> str | None:
    26→    """Return the dimension name with the lowest strict score."""
    27→    lowest_name = None
    28→    lowest_score = 101.0
    29→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
    30→        ds = dim_scores.get(dim.name)
    31→        if not ds:
    32→            continue
    33→        strict_val = ds.get("strict", ds["score"])
    34→        if strict_val < lowest_score:
    35→            lowest_score = strict_val
    36→            lowest_name = dim.name
    37→    for entry in scorecard_subjective:
    38→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    39→        if strict_val < lowest_score:
    40→            lowest_score = strict_val
    41→            lowest_name = entry.get("name")
    42→    return lowest_name
    43→
    44→
    45→def open_review_issue_counts(state: dict) -> dict[str, int]:
    46→    """Count open review findings grouped by subjective dimension key."""
    47→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
    48→    if not isinstance(findings, dict):
    49→        return {}
    50→
    51→    counts: dict[str, int] = {}
    52→    for finding in findings.values():
    53→        if not isinstance(finding, dict):
    54→            continue
    55→        if finding.get("status") != "open" or finding.get("detector") != "review":
    56→            continue
    57→        detail = finding.get("detail", {})
    58→        dimension = ""
    59→        if isinstance(detail, dict):
    60→            dimension = str(detail.get("dimension", "")).strip()
    61→        if not dimension:
    62→            dimension = str(finding.get("dimension", "")).strip()
    63→        if not dimension:
    64→            continue
    65→        counts[dimension] = counts.get(dimension, 0) + 1
    66→    return counts
    67→
    68→
    69→def render_objective_dimensions(
    70→    dim_scores: dict,
    71→    *,
    72→    lowest_name: str | None,
    73→    bar_len: int,
    74→) -> None:
    75→    """Print rows for objective (detector-based) dimensions."""
    76→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
    77→        ds = dim_scores.get(dim.name)
    78→        if not ds:
    79→            continue
    80→        score_val = ds["score"]
    81→        strict_val = ds.get("strict", score_val)
    82→        checks = ds["checks"]
    83→
    84→        bar = dimension_bar(score_val, colorize_fn=colorize, bar_len=bar_len)
    85→        focus = colorize(" ←", "yellow") if dim.name == lowest_name else "  "
    86→        checks_str = f"{checks:>7,}"
    87→        action = dimension_action_type(dim.name)
    88→        print(
    89→            f"  {dim.name:<22} {checks_str}  {score_val:5.1f}%  {strict_val:5.1f}%  {bar}  T{dim.tier}  {action}{focus}"
    90→        )
    91→
    92→
    93→def render_subjective_dimensions(
    94→    scorecard_subjective: list[dict],
    95→    *,
    96→    lowest_name: str | None,
    97→    bar_len: int,
    98→    review_issue_counts: dict[str, int],
    99→) -> None:
   100→    """Print rows for subjective (review-based) dimensions."""
   101→    if not scorecard_subjective:
   102→        return
   103→    print(
   104→        colorize(
   105→            "  ── Subjective Measures (matches scorecard.png) ──────────────────────",
   106→            "dim",
   107→        )
   108→    )
   109→    for entry in scorecard_subjective:
   110→        name = str(entry.get("name", "Unknown"))
   111→        score_val = float(entry.get("score", 0.0))
   112→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", score_val))
   113→        tier = 4
   114→
   115→        bar = dimension_bar(score_val, colorize_fn=colorize, bar_len=bar_len)
   116→        focus = colorize(" ←", "yellow") if name == lowest_name else "  "
   117→        checks_str = f"{'—':>7}"
   118→        stale_tag = colorize(" [stale]", "yellow") if entry.get("stale") else ""
   119→        placeholder_tag = (
   120→            colorize(" [unassessed]", "yellow") if entry.get("placeholder") else ""
   121→        )
   122→        dim_key = str(entry.get("dimension_key", "")).strip()
   123→        cli_keys = [
   124→            str(key).strip()
   125→            for key in entry.get("cli_keys", [])
   126→            if isinstance(key, str) and str(key).strip()
   127→        ]
   128→        if dim_key:
   129→            issue_count = int(review_issue_counts.get(dim_key, 0))
   130→        elif cli_keys:
   131→            issue_count = int(sum(review_issue_counts.get(key, 0) for key in cli_keys))
   132→        else:
   133→            issue_count = 0
   134→        issue_style = "yellow" if strict_val < 95.0 and issue_count == 0 else "dim"
   135→        issue_tag = colorize(f" [open issues: {issue_count}]", issue_style)
   136→        print(
   137→            f"  {name:<22} {checks_str}  {score_val:5.1f}%  {strict_val:5.1f}%  {bar}  T{tier}  {'review'}{focus}{stale_tag}"
   138→            f"{placeholder_tag}{issue_tag}"
   139→        )
   140→
   141→
   142→__all__ = [
   143→    "find_lowest_dimension",
   144→    "open_review_issue_counts",
   145→    "render_objective_dimensions",
   146→    "render_subjective_dimensions",
   147→    "scorecard_subjective_entries_for_status",
   148→]
   149→
   150→

    17→def _plan_with_queue(*ids: str) -> dict:
    18→    plan = empty_plan()
    19→    plan["queue_order"] = list(ids)
    20→    return plan
    21→
    22→
    23→def _state_with_stale_dimensions(*dim_keys: str, score: float = 50.0) -> dict:
    24→    """Build a minimal state with stale subjective dimensions."""
    25→    dim_scores: dict = {}
    26→    assessments: dict = {}
    27→    for dim_key in dim_keys:
    28→        dim_scores[dim_key] = {
    29→            "score": score,
    30→            "strict": score,
    31→            "checks": 1,
    32→            "issues": 0,
    33→            "detectors": {
    34→                "subjective_assessment": {
    35→                    "dimension_key": dim_key,
    36→                    "placeholder": False,
    37→                }
    38→            },
    39→        }
    40→        assessments[dim_key] = {
    41→            "score": score,
    42→            "needs_review_refresh": True,
    43→            "refresh_reason": "mechanical_findings_changed",
    44→            "stale_since": "2025-01-01T00:00:00+00:00",
    45→        }
    46→    return {
    47→        "findings": {},
    48→        "scan_count": 5,
    49→        "dimension_scores": dim_scores,
    50→        "subjective_assessments": assessments,
    51→    }
    52→
    53→
    54→def _state_with_unscored_dimensions(*dim_keys: str) -> dict:
    55→    """Build a minimal state with unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions."""
    56→    dim_scores: dict = {}
    57→    assessments: dict = {}
    58→    for dim_key in dim_keys:
    59→        dim_scores[dim_key] = {
    60→            "score": 0,
    61→            "strict": 0,
    62→            "checks": 1,
    63→            "issues": 0,
    64→            "detectors": {
    65→                "subjective_assessment": {
    66→                    "dimension_key": dim_key,
    67→                    "placeholder": True,
    68→                }
    69→            },
    70→        }
    71→        assessments[dim_key] = {
    72→            "score": 0.0,
    73→            "source": "scan_reset_subjective",
    74→            "placeholder": True,
    75→        }
    76→    return {
    77→        "findings": {},
    78→        "scan_count": 1,
    79→        "dimension_scores": dim_scores,
    80→        "subjective_assessments": assessments,
    81→    }
    82→
    83→
    84→def _state_with_mixed_dimensions(
    85→    unscored: list[str],
    86→    stale: list[str],
    87→) -> dict:
    88→    """Build a state with both unscored and stale dimensions."""
    89→    state = _state_with_unscored_dimensions(*unscored)
    90→    stale_state = _state_with_stale_dimensions(*stale)
    91→    state["dimension_scores"].update(stale_state["dimension_scores"])
    92→    state["subjective_assessments"].update(stale_state["subjective_assessments"])
    93→    return state
    94→
