1→"""Plan-aware queue progress and frozen score display helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→
     7→from desloppify.core.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
     8→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     9→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import is_subjective_queue_item
    10→
    11→
    12→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    13→# QueueBreakdown — single source of truth for queue numbers
    14→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    15→
    16→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    17→class QueueBreakdown:
    18→    """All numbers needed to render the standardized queue display."""
    19→
    20→    queue_total: int = 0
    21→    plan_ordered: int = 0
    22→    skipped: int = 0
    23→    subjective: int = 0
    24→    workflow: int = 0
    25→    focus_cluster: str | None = None
    26→    focus_cluster_count: int = 0
    27→    focus_cluster_total: int = 0
    28→
    29→
    30→def plan_aware_queue_breakdown(
    31→    state: dict,
    32→    plan: dict | None = None,
    33→) -> QueueBreakdown:
    34→    """Build a full :class:`QueueBreakdown` from a single ``build_work_queue`` call."""
    35→    from desloppify.engine.work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
    36→
    37→    result = build_work_queue(
    38→        state,
    39→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
    40→            status="open",
    41→            count=None,
    42→            plan=plan,
    43→            collapse_clusters=True,
    44→            include_skipped=False,
    45→        ),
    46→    )
    47→
    48→    queue_total = result["total"]
    49→
    50→    # Count subjective and workflow items in the queue.
    51→    # Collapsed clusters whose members are all subjective count as subjective.
    52→    items = result.get("items", [])
    53→    subjective = sum(
    54→        1 for item in items
    55→        if is_subjective_queue_item(item)
    56→    )
    57→    workflow = sum(
    58→        1 for item in items
    59→        if item.get("kind") in ("workflow_stage", "workflow_action")
    60→    )
    61→
    62→    # Plan-derived counts
    63→    plan_ordered = 0
    64→    skipped = 0
    65→    if plan:
    66→        skipped = len(plan.get("skipped", {}))
    67→        # plan_ordered = items that are in queue_order minus skipped
    68→        queue_order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    69→        skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    70→        plan_ordered = sum(1 for fid in queue_order if fid not in skipped_ids)
    71→
    72→    # Focus cluster info
    73→    focus_cluster = None
    74→    focus_cluster_count = 0
    75→    focus_cluster_total = 0
    76→    if plan:
    77→        active = plan.get("active_cluster")
    78→        if active:
    79→            focus_cluster = active
    80→            cluster_data = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(active, {})
    81→            focus_cluster_total = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
    82→            # Count how many cluster members are still in the queue
    83→            cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
    84→            open_findings = {
    85→                fid
    86→                for fid, f in state.get("findings", {}).items()
    87→                if f.get("status") == "open"
    88→            }
    89→            focus_cluster_count = len(cluster_member_ids & open_findings)
    90→
    91→    return QueueBreakdown(
    92→        queue_total=queue_total,
    93→        plan_ordered=plan_ordered,
    94→        skipped=skipped,
    95→        subjective=subjective,
    96→        workflow=workflow,
    97→        focus_cluster=focus_cluster,
    98→        focus_cluster_count=focus_cluster_count,
    99→        focus_cluster_total=focus_cluster_total,
   100→    )
   101→
   102→
   103→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   104→# Formatting helpers — single source of truth for queue display
   105→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   106→
   107→def format_queue_headline(breakdown: QueueBreakdown) -> str:
   108→    """The one-line Queue summary. Same format everywhere.
   109→
   110→    Examples::
   111→
   112→        Queue: 1934 items (292 planned · 23 skipped)
   113→        Queue: 1934 items
   114→    """
   115→    n = breakdown.queue_total
   116→    label = f"Queue: {n} item{'s' if n != 1 else ''}"
   117→
   118→    # Parenthesized segments
   119→    segments: list[str] = []
   120→    if breakdown.workflow > 0:
   121→        segments.append(f"{breakdown.workflow} planning step{'s' if breakdown.workflow != 1 else ''}")
   122→    if breakdown.plan_ordered > 0:
   123→        segments.append(f"{breakdown.plan_ordered} planned")
   124→    if breakdown.skipped > 0:
   125→        segments.append(f"{breakdown.skipped} skipped")
   126→    if breakdown.subjective > 0:
   127→        segments.append(f"{breakdown.subjective} subjective")
   128→    if segments:
   129→        sep = " \u00b7 "
   130→        detail = sep.join(segments)
   131→        return f"{label} ({detail})"
   132→    return label
   133→
   134→
   135→def format_queue_block(
   136→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown,
   137→    *,
   138→    frozen_score: float | None = None,
   139→) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
   140→    """Full queue block: focus banner + queue line + contextual hints.
   141→
   142→    Returns a list of ``(text, style)`` pairs ready for ``colorize()``.
   143→    """
   144→    lines: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
   145→
   146→    # Focus banner (prominent, separate)
   147→    if breakdown.focus_cluster:
   148→        focus_line = (
   149→            f"  Focus: `{breakdown.focus_cluster}` "
   150→            f"\u2014 {breakdown.focus_cluster_count}/{breakdown.focus_cluster_total}"

1→"""Lightweight integrity helpers for subjective/review scoring.
     2→
     3→This module intentionally lives outside ``desloppify.intelligence.review`` so command/state
     4→paths can import it without loading the heavier review package.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→from collections.abc import Iterable, Mapping
    10→
    11→from desloppify.scoring import (
    12→    SUBJECTIVE_TARGET_MATCH_TOLERANCE,
    13→    matches_target_score,
    14→)
    15→
    16→__all__ = [
    17→    "SUBJECTIVE_TARGET_MATCH_TOLERANCE",
    18→    "is_holistic_subjective_finding",
    19→    "is_subjective_review_open",
    20→    "matches_target_score",
    21→    "subjective_review_open_breakdown",
    22→    "unassessed_subjective_dimensions",
    23→]
    24→
    25→
    26→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    27→# Internal iteration helper
    28→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    29→
    30→
    31→def _iter_findings(
    32→    findings: Mapping[str, dict] | Iterable[dict],
    33→) -> Iterable[tuple[str, dict]]:
    34→    """Yield (finding_id, finding) pairs from mapping or iterable inputs."""
    35→    if isinstance(findings, Mapping):
    36→        for finding_id, finding in findings.items():
    37→            if isinstance(finding, dict):
    38→                yield str(finding_id), finding
    39→        return
    40→
    41→    for index, finding in enumerate(findings):
    42→        if isinstance(finding, dict):
    43→            yield str(index), finding
    44→
    45→
    46→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    47→# Public helpers (formerly in integrity/review.py)
    48→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    49→
    50→
    51→def is_subjective_review_open(finding: dict) -> bool:
    52→    """Return True when a finding is an open subjective-review signal."""
    53→    return (
    54→        finding.get("status") == "open"
    55→        and finding.get("detector") == "subjective_review"
    56→    )
    57→
    58→
    59→def is_holistic_subjective_finding(finding: dict, *, finding_id: str = "") -> bool:
    60→    """Best-effort check for holistic subjective-review coverage findings."""
    61→    candidate_id = str(finding.get("id") or finding_id or "")
    62→    if "::holistic_unreviewed" in candidate_id or "::holistic_stale" in candidate_id:
    63→        return True
    64→
    65→    summary = str(finding.get("summary", "") or "").lower()
    66→    if "holistic" in summary and "review" in summary:
    67→        return True
    68→
    69→    detail = finding.get("detail", {})
    70→    return bool(detail.get("holistic"))
    71→
    72→
    73→def subjective_review_open_breakdown(
    74→    findings: Mapping[str, dict] | Iterable[dict],
    75→) -> tuple[int, dict[str, int], dict[str, int]]:
    76→    """Return open subjective count plus reason and holistic-reason breakdowns."""
    77→    reason_counts: dict[str, int] = {}
    78→    holistic_reason_counts: dict[str, int] = {}
    79→    total = 0
    80→
    81→    for finding_id, finding in _iter_findings(findings):
    82→        if not is_subjective_review_open(finding):
    83→            continue
    84→
    85→        total += 1
    86→        reason = str(finding.get("detail", {}).get("reason", "other") or "other")
    87→        reason_counts[reason] = reason_counts.get(reason, 0) + 1
    88→
    89→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(finding, finding_id=finding_id):
    90→            holistic_reason_counts[reason] = holistic_reason_counts.get(reason, 0) + 1
    91→
    92→    return total, reason_counts, holistic_reason_counts
    93→
    94→
    95→def unassessed_subjective_dimensions(dim_scores: dict | None) -> list[str]:
    96→    """Return subjective dimension display names that are still 0% placeholders."""
    97→    if not dim_scores:
    98→        return []
    99→
   100→    unassessed: list[str] = []

1→"""Canonical scorecard projection helpers shared across app/engine code."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine.planning.dimension_rows import (
     8→    scorecard_dimension_rows,
     9→)
    10→from desloppify.scoring import DISPLAY_NAMES
    11→
    12→_COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION: dict[str, str] = {
    13→    "abstraction leverage": "abstraction_fitness",
    14→    "indirection cost": "abstraction_fitness",
    15→    "interface honesty": "abstraction_fitness",
    16→}
    17→
    18→
    19→def dimension_cli_key(dimension_name: str) -> str:
    20→    """Map a display name to a review --dimensions CLI key."""
    21→    cleaned = dimension_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
    22→    lowered = cleaned.lower()
    23→    if lowered in _COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION:
    24→        return _COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION[lowered]
    25→    for key, display in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
    26→        if display.lower() == lowered:
    27→            return key
    28→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]+", "_", lowered).strip("_")
    29→
    30→
    31→def scorecard_dimension_cli_keys(name: str, data: dict) -> list[str]:
    32→    """Map scorecard subjective display names to review --dimensions keys."""
    33→    if name in ("Elegance", "Elegance (combined)"):
    34→        components = (
    35→            data.get("detectors", {})
    36→            .get("subjective_assessment", {})
    37→            .get("components", [])
    38→        )
    39→        if isinstance(components, list) and components:
    40→            keys = [dimension_cli_key(component) for component in components]
    41→        else:
    42→            keys = [
    43→                "high_level_elegance",
    44→                "mid_level_elegance",
    45→                "low_level_elegance",
    46→            ]
    47→    elif name in ("Abstraction fit", "Abstraction fit (combined)"):
    48→        components = (
    49→            data.get("detectors", {})
    50→            .get("subjective_assessment", {})
    51→            .get("components", [])
    52→        )
    53→        if isinstance(components, list) and components:
    54→            keys = [dimension_cli_key(component) for component in components]
    55→        else:
    56→            keys = ["abstraction_fitness"]
    57→    else:
    58→        keys = [dimension_cli_key(name)]
    59→
    60→    seen: set[str] = set()
    61→    ordered: list[str] = []
    62→    for key in keys:
    63→        if not key or key in seen:
    64→            continue
    65→        ordered.append(key)
    66→        seen.add(key)
    67→    return ordered
    68→
    69→
    70→def scorecard_subjective_entries(
    71→    state: dict,
    72→    *,
    73→    dim_scores: dict | None = None,
    74→) -> list[dict]:
    75→    """Return scorecard-subjective entries with score/strict/placeholder metadata."""
    76→    rows = scorecard_dimension_rows(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
    77→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments") or {}
    78→    subjective_display_names = {display.lower() for display in DISPLAY_NAMES.values()}
    79→    subjective_display_names.update({"elegance", "elegance (combined)"})
    80→
    81→    entries: list[dict] = []
    82→    for name, data in rows:
    83→        detectors = data.get("detectors", {})
    84→        lowered_name = str(name).strip().lower()
    85→        is_subjective = (
    86→            "subjective_assessment" in detectors
    87→            or lowered_name in subjective_display_names
    88→        )
    89→        if not is_subjective:
    90→            continue
    91→        score = float(data.get("score", 0.0))
    92→        strict = float(data.get("strict", score))
    93→        assessment_meta = detectors.get("subjective_assessment", {})
    94→        placeholder = bool(
    95→            assessment_meta.get("placeholder")
    96→            or (
    97→                data.get("score", 0) == 0
    98→                and data.get("issues", 0) == 0
    99→                and data.get("checks", 0) == 0
   100→            )
   101→        )
   102→        [REDACTED]("dimension_key", "")
   103→        stale = bool(
   104→            dim_key
   105→            and isinstance(assessments.get(dim_key), dict)
   106→            and assessments[dim_key].get("needs_review_refresh")
   107→        )
   108→        entries.append(
   109→            {
   110→                "name": name,
   111→                "score": score,
   112→                "strict": strict,
   113→                "checks": int(data.get("checks", 0) or 0),
   114→                "issues": int(data.get("issues", 0) or 0),
   115→                "tier": int(data.get("tier", 4) or 4),
   116→                "placeholder": placeholder,
   117→                "stale": stale,
   118→                "dimension_key": dim_key,
   119→                "cli_keys": scorecard_dimension_cli_keys(name, data),
   120→            }
   121→        )
   122→    return entries
   123→
   124→
   125→def scorecard_dimensions_payload(
   126→    state: dict,
   127→    *,
   128→    dim_scores: dict | None = None,
   129→) -> list[dict]:
   130→    """Serialize scorecard rows for JSON/query outputs."""
   131→    subjective_by_name = {
   132→        entry["name"]: entry
   133→        for entry in scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   134→    }
   135→    payload: list[dict] = []
   136→    for name, data in scorecard_dimension_rows(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
   137→        score = float(data.get("score", 0.0))
   138→        strict = float(data.get("strict", score))
   139→        subjective = name in subjective_by_name
   140→        entry = {
   141→            "name": name,
   142→            "score": score,
   143→            "strict": strict,
   144→            "checks": int(data.get("checks", 0) or 0),
   145→            "issues": int(data.get("issues", 0) or 0),
   146→            "tier": int(
   147→                data.get("tier", 4 if subjective else 3) or (4 if subjective else 3)
   148→            ),
   149→            "subjective": subjective,
   150→        }
   151→        if subjective:
   152→            sub = subjective_by_name[name]
   153→            entry["placeholder"] = bool(sub.get("placeholder"))
   154→            entry["cli_keys"] = list(sub.get("cli_keys", []))
   155→        payload.append(entry)
   156→    return payload
   157→
   158→
   159→__all__ = [
   160→    "dimension_cli_key",
   161→    "scorecard_dimension_cli_keys",
   162→    "scorecard_dimension_rows",
   163→    "scorecard_dimensions_payload",
   164→    "scorecard_subjective_entries",
   165→]
   166→

    38→def cmd_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    39→    """Show all findings for a file, directory, detector, or pattern."""
    40→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    41→    state = runtime.state
    42→    config = runtime.config
    43→
    44→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    45→        return
    46→
    47→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
    48→    if skill_warning:
    49→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
    50→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(config)
    51→    if config_warning:
    52→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
    53→
    54→    print_triage_guardrail_info(state=state)
    55→

## Summary of Preflight/Postflight/Guardrail Patterns in Desloppify

I've completed a thorough search of the desloppify codebase for all preflight, postflight, and guardrail patterns. Here are my findings:

### PRIMARY GATING MECHANISMS

#### 1. **Review Rerun Preflight** (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/review/preflight.py`)

**What it checks:**
- Blocks `review --prepare`, `review --run-batches`, and `review --external-start` when backlog remains
- Gates on BOTH objective findings AND subjective queue work
- Can be bypassed with `--force-review-rerun`

**Key decision logic:**
- Only gates on dimensions that have prior nonzero scores (a "rerun")
- First-time reviews (no prior scores) skip the gate entirely
- Supports per-dimension targeting via `--dimensions` flag
- **Counts open subjective queue items but explicitly excludes `stale_review: True` items** (line 90)

**Subjectivity handling:**
- Calls `_count_open_subjective_queue_items()` with `include_subjective=True`
- Checks dimension names normalized to snake_case
- Clears `needs_review_refresh` markers after gate passes (lines 253-261)

#### 2. **Scan Queue Preflight** (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/scan/scan_preflight.py`)

**What it checks:**
- Blocks `scan` when plan has active `plan_start_scores` and queue remains
- No subjective-specific logic — purely counts queue items via `plan_aware_queue_count()`
- Bypassed by CI profile or `--force-rescan` with attestation

#### 3. **Triage Guardrail** (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/helpers/guardrails.py`)

**What it checks:**
- Blocks `resolve` (when status == "fixed") if triage is stale (new findings post-plan)
- Shows informational banner on `next`, `status`, `show` commands if triage stale
- Three functions:
  - `triage_guardrail_status()` — pure detection, returns `TriageGuardrailResult`
  - `print_triage_guardrail_info()` — shows yellow info banner if stale
  - `require_triage_current_or_exit()` — exits(1) if stale and not bypassed

### SUBJECTIVE VISIBILITY GATING PATTERN

The codebase has **TWO DISTINCT but related places** making "should we surface subjective work?" decisions:

#### A. **During status/next/show rendering:** Objective backlog gating
- File: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/helpers/subjective.py`
- Function: `print_subjective_followup()` (lines 20-58)
- **Decision:** When `objective_backlog > 0`, quality nudges are suppressed
- **Rationale (line 28-29):** "the queue already surfaces subjective work when it becomes actionable"
- Always shows integrity warnings regardless of backlog

#### B. **During queue building:** Stale marking
- File: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/helpers.py` (line 450)
- File: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/planning/scorecard_projection.py` (lines 103-107)
- Sets `stale_review: True` flag on subjective queue items when dimension has `needs_review_refresh` marker
- File: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/review/preflight.py` (line 90)
- **Explicitly filters out** stale_review items from blocking backlog count

#### C. **In status dimension table rendering:**
- File: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/status_parts/render.py` (lines 56-74)
- File: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/status_parts/render_dimensions.py` (lines 118-120)
- Shows `[stale]` tag, and conditionally shows rerun command based on `objective_backlog` (line 65)

### STALE DIMENSION LIFECYCLE

**Marked as stale** when:
- Review finding is resolved (fixed/wontfix) → `refresh_reason: "review_finding_{status}"`
- Mechanical findings change mid-review → `refresh_reason: "mechanical_findings_changed"`
(Set by: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_state/resolution.py` and merge.py)

**Tracked by:**
- `needs_review_refresh: bool`
- `stale_since: ISO timestamp`
- `refresh_reason: str`

**Cleared by:**
- `review_rerun_preflight()` after gate passes (lines 253-261)
- Or explicitly via `clear_stale_subjective_entries()`

### INCONSISTENCIES & DUPLICATIONS

1. **Inconsistent naming:**
   - "stale" vs "needs_review_refresh" vs "refresh_reason"
   - Test file mentions "issue #157" but logic involves subjective actionability (issue #181)

2. **Multiple places computing the same decision:**
   - Review preflight checks: _count_open_subjective_queue_items() + _blocking_scored_dimensions()
   - Status rendering checks: objective_backlog parameter passed to show_subjective_followup()
   - Each has slightly different logic for what "should we surface subjective work" means

3. **Long functions doing multiple things:**
   - `review_rerun_preflight()` (200-262): Gate check → Clear stale → Save state
   - `_count_open_subjective_queue_items()` (66-100): Normalizes dimensions + builds queue + counts filtered items

4. **Dead/unused parameters:**
   - `_blocking_scored_dimensions()` has `normalized_dimensions` parameter that duplicates logic in caller

5. **Duplicated dimension key normalization:**
   - `_normalize_dimension_key()` in preflight.py (lines 60-63)
   - `_canonical_subjective_dimension_key()` in scorecard_projection.py (lines 120-128)
   - Similar logic in work_queue helpers

### RELATED TEST FILES

- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/tests/commands/test_review_preflight.py` (716 lines)
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/tests/commands/test_scan_preflight.py` (154 lines)
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/tests/commands/test_subjective_actionability_gate.py` (140 lines)
- Extensive coverage of edge cases (first run vs rerun, per-dimension targeting, stale filtering)

### KEY DESIGN INSIGHT

The "subjective gating policy" actually has **two layers**:
1. **Preflight layer:** Prevents review reruns when backlog exists (backlog = objective + open subjective queue)
2. **Actionability layer:** Suppresses subjective nudges during rendering when objective backlog > 0

These work together: preflight keeps you from running reviews while backlog exists, and actionability layer hides subjective suggestions when you should focus on objective work. Stale items are special—they're marked as work but DON'T block preflight (they ARE the work you should do).

## Comprehensive Analysis: Subjective Dimension State Computation Layer

Based on my thorough search of the desloppify codebase, here's the complete map of the subjective dimension state computation layer:

### Core Functions and Their Relationships

#### 1. **Classification Functions in `stale_dimensions.py`**

**`_current_stale_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]`** (Line 73-91)
- **Purpose**: Returns set of `subjective::<slug>` IDs that are currently stale
- **Data source**: `dimension_scores` + `subjective_assessments`
- **Logic**:
  - Calls `scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)` to get scored entries
  - Checks each entry's `stale` flag (set by checking if `dim_key` exists in `subjective_assessments` with `needs_review_refresh: True`)
  - Converts dimension keys to IDs via `slugify()` with `SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX`
- **Caching**: None (called repeatedly per operation)
- **Used by**: `sync_unscored_dimensions()`, `sync_stale_dimensions()`, `current_under_target_ids()`, auto_cluster logic

**`current_unscored_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]`** (Line 94-138)
- **Purpose**: Returns set of `subjective::<slug>` IDs that are currently unscored (placeholder)
- **Data source**: Dual-path fallback:
  1. Primary: `subjective_assessments` dict with `placeholder: True`
  2. Fallback: `dimension_scores` with `subjective_assessment.placeholder: True` (when assessments empty)
- **Logic**:
  - If `subjective_assessments` is populated and non-empty, iterate its entries
  - Check each entry for `placeholder: True` flag
  - Fall back to scanning `dimension_scores` directly for entries with `detectors.subjective_assessment.placeholder: True`
  - Convert to IDs via slugify
- **Note**: Cannot use `scorecard_subjective_entries()` here because it "intentionally hides placeholders"
- **Used by**: Everywhere (initial-review gating)

**`current_under_target_ids(state: StateModel, *, target_strict: float = 95.0) -> set[str]`** (Line 141-176)
- **Purpose**: Returns dimensions that are under target but neither stale nor unscored
- **Data source**: `dimension_scores` via `scorecard_subjective_entries()`
- **Logic**:
  1. Get stale and unscored IDs to exclude
  2. Iterate scorecard entries filtering:
     - NOT `placeholder` and NOT `stale`
     - Check `strict` score < `target_strict` (default 95%)
  3. Return IDs not in stale or unscored sets
- **Default target**: 95.0 (configurable in auto_cluster)
- **Used by**: Auto-clustering only (optional cluster creation)

---

#### 2. **Score Fetching Helper**

**`subjective_strict_scores(state: dict) -> dict[str, float]`** (Line 282-306 in helpers.py)
- **Purpose**: Returns mapping of dimension aliases to `strict` scores
- **Data source**: `dimension_scores` via `scorecard_subjective_entries()`
- **Output keys**: Multiple aliases per dimension:
  - Display name (lowercased)
  - CLI keys from `entry.cli_keys`
  - Slugified variants
  - Dimension key (canonical and slugified)
- **Example output**: `{"naming_quality": 75.0, "naming quality": 75.0, "naming_quality": 75.0, ...}`
- **Used by**: `build_finding_items()` in ranking to attach subjective scores to review findings

---

#### 3. **Work Item Builder**

**`build_subjective_items(state: dict, findings: dict, *, threshold: float = 100.0) -> list[dict]`** (Line 344-453 in helpers.py)
- **Purpose**: Creates synthetic subjective work items for queue display
- **Classification logic** (lines 418-421):
  ```python
  is_unassessed = bool(entry.get("placeholder")) or (
      name in unassessed_dims
      or (strict_val <= 0.0 and int(entry.get("issues", 0)) == 0)
  )
  is_stale = bool(entry.get("stale"))
  ```
- **Item attributes set**:
  - `kind: "subjective_dimension"`
  - `initial_review: is_unassessed` (high priority, tier -3)
  - `stale_review: is_stale and not is_unassessed`
  - `id: f"subjective::{slugify(dim_key)}"`
- **Filtering**: Items with `strict_val >= threshold` are skipped
- **Merging strategy**: 
  - Scorecard entries (excludes placeholders)
  - Plus `_unassessed_entries_from_dim_scores()` for placeholders missed by scorecard pipeline
  - Deduplicates by dimension_key
- **Open review detection** (lines 377-385):
  - Counts `review` detector findings per dimension
  - Sets `open_review_findings` in detail
- **Used by**: `build_work_queue()` in core.py

---

#### 4. **Scoreboard Computation**

**`scorecard_subjective_entries(state: dict, *, dim_scores: dict = None) -> list[dict]`** (Line 70-122 in scorecard_projection.py)
- **Purpose**: Central entry point for subjective dimension scoring
- **Data sources**:
  - `scorecard_dimension_rows(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)` — canonical dimension listing
  - `subjective_assessments` — for stale flag computation
- **Entry structure**:
  ```python
  {
    "name": str,                    # Display name
    "score": float,                 # Raw score
    "strict": float,                # Clamped/strict score
    "checks": int,
    "issues": int,
    "tier": int,
    "placeholder": bool,            # Computed from metadata
    "stale": bool,                  # = assessments[dim_key].needs_review_refresh
    "dimension_key": str,           # Canonical key
    "cli_keys": list[str],          # Review command keys
  }
  ```
- **Placeholder computation** (lines 94-101):
  ```python
  placeholder = bool(
      assessment_meta.get("placeholder")
      or (score == 0 and issues == 0 and checks == 0)
  )
  ```
- **Stale computation** (lines 103-107):
  ```python
  stale = bool(
      dim_key
      and isinstance(assessments.get(dim_key), dict)
      and assessments[dim_key].get("needs_review_refresh")
  )
  ```

---

### State Data Structures

#### `dimension_scores` (keyed by dimension display name)
```python
{
    "Naming Quality": {
        "score": 75.0,
        "strict": 75.0,
        "checks": 1,
        "issues": 0,
        "tier": 4,
        "detectors": {
            "subjective_assessment": {
                "dimension_key": "naming_quality",
                "placeholder": False,  # or True for unscored
                "components": [...]    # for composite dims
            }
        }
    }
}
```

#### `subjective_assessments` (keyed by dimension_key)
```python
{
    "naming_quality": {
        "score": 75.0,
        "placeholder": False,           # or True for unscored
        "needs_review_refresh": True,   # marks as stale
        "refresh_reason": "...",
        "stale_since": "ISO timestamp",
        "source": "review_...",
    }
}
```

---

### Classification Decision Tree

```
For each dimension in dimension_scores:
  1. Is it subjective?
     └─ Check: "subjective_assessment" in detectors OR lowered_name in subjective_display_names
  
  2. Compute placeholder:
     └─ assessment.placeholder OR (score==0 AND issues==0 AND checks==0)
  
  3. Compute stale:
     └─ subjective_assessments[dim_key].needs_review_refresh == True
  
  4. Compute strict:
     └─ dimension.strict OR dimension.score
  
  5. Classify item:
     ├─ If placeholder → "initial_review" (unscored)
     ├─ Else if stale → "stale_review" (needs refresh)
     ├─ Else if strict < target → "under_target" (optional, objective-backlog dependent)
     └─ Else → not included (at target)
```

---

### Queue Gating Logic

**In `build_work_queue()` (core.py lines 86-110)**:
```python
# Count objective findings
objective_count = sum(
    1 for item in finding_items
    if item.get("detector") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
)

# Gate subjective items
if include_subjective and status in {"open", "all"} and not chronic:
    subjective_items = build_subjective_items(...)
    for item in subjective_items:
        # Initial review: always surface
        if item.get("initial_review"):
            all_items.append(item)
        # Stale/under-target: only when objective backlog empty
        elif objective_count > 0:
            continue  # skip
        else:
            all_items.append(item)
```

**In plan sync** (`stale_dimensions.py` lines 278-291):
```python
has_real_items = any(
    f.get("status") == "open"
    and f.get("detector") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
    and not f.get("suppressed")
    for f in state.get("findings", {}).values()
)
if not has_real_items and stale_ids:
    # Inject stale dimensions at back of queue
```

---

### NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS Constant

**Definition** (stale_dimensions.py line 36-38):
```python
NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS: frozenset[str] = frozenset({
    "review", "concerns", "subjective_review", "subjective_assessment",
})
```

**Usage sites**:
1. `stale_dimensions.py`: Gating stale dimension injection (3 places)
2. `auto_cluster.py`: Determining when to inject under-target cluster
3. `_work_queue/core.py`: Counting objective findings for gating
4. Indirect: Ranking uses this for impact computation

---

### SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX Constant

**Value**: `"subjective::"`

**ID format**: `subjective::<dimension_slug>`
- Example: `subjective::naming_quality`, `subjective::error_handling`

**Usage**:
- Queue order identification in plan.json
- Item ID generation in synthetic work items
- All three classification functions generate IDs with this prefix

---

### Caching and Performance

**No persistent caching** of classification results:
- `_current_stale_ids()` — recomputed every call
- `current_unscored_ids()` — recomputed every call (dual-path fallback adds minimal cost)
- `current_under_target_ids()` — recomputed every call
- `subjective_strict_scores()` — recomputed per work-queue build (called once per command)

**Cost analysis**:
- Each classification iterates all dimension_scores (usually < 10 dimensions)
- Scorecard pipeline is the heaviest operation (calls dimension_rows, projections)
- Acceptable because invoked only per command, not per-item

---

### Inconsistencies and Edge Cases

1. **Scorecard hiding placeholders**: `scorecard_subjective_entries()` excludes placeholder entries from output, requiring `build_subjective_items()` to merge in `_unassessed_entries_from_dim_scores()` separately.

2. **Dual-path fallback in `current_unscored_ids()`**: 
   - Prefers `subjective_assessments` (primary)
   - Falls back to `dimension_scores` when assessments empty
   - This handles transition period after scan before reviews run

3. **Under-target optional injection**:
   - Requires `target_strict` parameter (default 95%)
   - Only creates cluster if ≥2 items (MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE)
   - Only injected when objective backlog empty (gated)
   - Can be evicted when objective work returns (lines 529-536 in auto_cluster.py)

4. **Stale detection multi-level**:
   - Must have `dimension_key` in metadata
   - Must exist as entry in `subjective_assessments`
   - Must have `needs_review_refresh: True`
   - Missing any level → not stale

---

### Data Flow Diagram

```
state.json
  ├─ dimension_scores
  │   └─ [dim_name].detectors.subjective_assessment.{placeholder, dimension_key}
  │
  └─ subjective_assessments
      └─ [dimension_key].{placeholder, needs_review_refresh}

                         ↓

scorecard_subjective_entries()
  ├─ Reads dimension_scores via scorecard_dimension_rows()
  ├─ Overlays subjective_assessments for stale flagging
  └─ Returns [entry{placeholder, stale, strict, dimension_key, cli_keys}]

                         ↓

Classification functions:
  ├─ _current_stale_ids() → set[ID] where entry.stale
  ├─ current_unscored_ids() → set[ID] where entry.placeholder
  ├─ current_under_target_ids() → set[ID] where !stale, !placeholder, score < target
  └─ subjective_strict_scores() → dict[alias → strict_score]

                         ↓

build_subjective_items()
  ├─ Merges scorecard entries + _unassessed_entries_from_dim_scores()
  ├─ Classifies each as: initial_review | stale_review | under_target | none
  ├─ Counts open review findings per dimension
  └─ Returns [{kind: "subjective_dimension", initial_review, stale_review, ...}]

                         ↓

build_work_queue()
  ├─ Builds finding_items
  ├─ Counts objective_count (excluding NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS)
  ├─ Adds subjective_items:
  │   ├─ Always: initial_review items
  │   └─ Only if objective_count == 0: stale_review, under_target
  ├─ Applies plan ordering via _apply_plan_order()
  └─ Collapses clusters if needed

                         ↓

Output: WorkQueueResult
  └─ items: [finding items + subjective items + workflow items]
```

---

### Summary Table

| Function | Input | Output | Caller(s) | Cost |
|----------|-------|--------|-----------|------|
| `_current_stale_ids()` | state | set[str] | sync funcs, auto_cluster | O(dimensions) |
| `current_unscored_ids()` | state | set[str] | sync funcs, auto_cluster, all | O(assessments ∪ dimensions) |
| `current_under_target_ids()` | state, target | set[str] | auto_cluster only | O(dimensions) |
| `scorecard_subjective_entries()` | state | list[dict] | build_subjective_items, _current_stale_ids, subjective_strict_scores | O(dimension_rows) |
| `subjective_strict_scores()` | state | dict[str→float] | ranking only | O(subjective_entries) |
| `build_subjective_items()` | state, findings, threshold | list[dict] | work_queue core | O(dimensions + review_findings) |

## Summary of Subjective Dimension Visibility Gating Decisions

Based on my thorough analysis of the codebase, I've identified **ALL** locations where "should subjective work surface?" decisions are made. These are scattered across the planning engine, work queue construction, and display rendering. Here's the complete inventory:

---

## 1. **Plan Queue Synchronization** — `desloppify/engine/_plan/stale_dimensions.py`

### `sync_unscored_dimensions()` (lines 203-241)
**Decision:** Inject unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions unconditionally at front of queue
- **Inputs:** `current_unscored_ids(state)` — dimensions with `placeholder=True`
- **Action:** Always prepend unscored IDs after any promoted items
- **Policy Comment:** "unconditionally prepend...so initial reviews are the first priority"
- **Prune Logic:** Removes subjective IDs that are no longer unscored AND not stale (leaves stale for stale_sync to manage)

### `sync_stale_dimensions()` (lines 248-292)
**Decision:** Conditionally inject stale (previously-scored, now-stale) dimensions ONLY when objective backlog is empty
- **Inputs:** `_current_stale_ids(state)` — dimensions with `stale=True`
- **Gate Computation (lines 279-284):** 
  ```python
  has_real_items = any(
      f.get("status") == "open"
      and f.get("detector") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
      and not f.get("suppressed")
      for f in state.get("findings", {}).values()
  )
  if not has_real_items and stale_ids:  # GATE: no objective work remaining
      # inject stale IDs to queue
  ```
- **Policy Comment:** "append stale...when no objective items remain"

### `sync_create_plan_needed()` (lines 395-440)
**Decision:** Inject `workflow::create-plan` only when:
1. No unscored dimensions remain
2. At least one objective finding exists
3. No triage stages pending
- **Dependency Logic (lines 418-431):** Explicitly gates on drained unscored AND presence of objective work

### `current_unscored_ids()` (lines 94-138)
**Input Identification:** Returns `subjective::*` IDs that are currently unscored placeholders
- **Sources:** `subjective_assessments` with `placeholder=True` (primary), falls back to `dimension_scores`

### `current_under_target_ids()` (lines 141-176)
**Input Identification:** Returns `subjective::*` IDs that are under target threshold but not stale or unscored
- **Excludes:** Stale IDs and unscored IDs (managed separately)

### `NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS` (lines 36-38)
**Constant Definition:**
```python
NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS: frozenset[str] = frozenset({
    "review", "concerns", "subjective_review", "subjective_assessment",
})
```
- **Usage:** Every place that decides "is objective backlog empty?" uses this to filter

---

## 2. **Auto-Clustering** — `desloppify/engine/_plan/auto_cluster.py`

### `_sync_subjective_clusters()` (lines 407-538)
**Decision:** Create/update/delete clusters for unscored, stale, and under-target dimensions

#### Unscored cluster (lines 435-449):
- **Gate:** Min size 1 (always created if unscored items exist)
- **Action:** Creates `auto/initial-review` cluster for all unscored dimensions

#### Stale cluster (lines 451-468):
- **Gate:** Min size 2
- **Action:** Creates `auto/stale-review` cluster for all stale dimensions

#### Under-target cluster (lines 470-527):
- **Gate Computation (lines 492-497):**
  ```python
  has_objective_items = any(
      f.get("status") == "open"
      and f.get("detector") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
      and not f.get("suppressed")
      for f in findings.values()
  )
  if not has_objective_items and len(under_target_queue_ids) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE:
      # Create optional under-target cluster
  ```
- **Eviction Logic (lines 529-536):** When objective backlog returns, ALL under-target IDs are removed from queue_order
- **Comment:** "only inject under-target items when no objective findings remain — mirror the guard used by sync_stale_dimensions()"

---

## 3. **Work Queue Construction** — `desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py`

### `build_work_queue()` (lines 59-158)
**Decision:** Gate whether non-initial subjective items appear in the work queue

#### Gate Logic (lines 86-110):
```python
objective_count = sum(
    1 for item in finding_items
    if item.get("detector") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
)

if (
    resolved_options.include_subjective
    and status in {"open", "all"}
    and not resolved_options.chronic
):
    subjective_items = build_subjective_items(...)
    for item in subjective_items:
        if not scope_matches(item, resolved_options.scope):
            continue
        # Non-initial subjective items only surface when objective
        # backlog is drained — stale reruns AND under-target dims
        # should wait until all mechanical work is done.
        if not item.get("initial_review") and objective_count > 0:
            continue  # GATE: skip non-initial when objective work exists
        all_items.append(item)
```

**Policy Comment:** "Non-initial subjective items only surface when objective backlog is drained"

### Subjective Item Tagging — `desloppify/engine/_work_queue/helpers.py`

#### `build_subjective_items()` (lines 344-453)
**Decision:** Tag each subjective item with `initial_review` and `stale_review` flags

- **initial_review computation (lines 418-421):**
  ```python
  is_unassessed = bool(entry.get("placeholder")) or (
      name in unassessed_dims
      or (strict_val <= 0.0 and int(entry.get("issues", 0)) == 0)
  )
  ```
- **stale_review computation (line 422):** `is_stale = bool(entry.get("stale"))`
- **Item fields (lines 449-450):** Both flags attached to item dict for use by gating logic

---

## 4. **Ranking & Sorting** — `desloppify/engine/_work_queue/ranking.py`

### `item_sort_key()` (lines 143-191)
**Decision:** Assign tier -3 to initial-review subjective items vs tier 1 for other subjective items

```python
# Initial-review subjective items: highest priority (tier -3)
if kind == "subjective_dimension" and item.get("initial_review"):
    return (-3, 0, subjective_score_value(item), item.get("id", ""))

# ... other tiers (-2, -1, 0) for workflow and clusters ...

# Regular subjective items: tier 1 (after findings)
if kind == "subjective_dimension" or item.get("is_subjective"):
    return (
        1,
        -impact,
        subjective_score_value(item),
        item.get("id", ""),
    )
```

---

## 5. **Review Preflight Guard** — `desloppify/app/commands/review/preflight.py`

### `review_rerun_preflight()` (lines 200-262)
**Decision:** Block review reruns when objective OR subjective backlog exists

#### Gate Logic (lines 228-250):
1. Gets scored dimensions (those with prior review scores)
2. Only gates those dimensions being targeted
3. Computes objective backlog: `include_subjective=False`
4. Computes subjective backlog: `_count_open_subjective_queue_items()`
5. **Blocks if:** `objective_total > 0 or subjective_total > 0`

#### Subjective Backlog Count (lines 66-100):
```python
def _count_open_subjective_queue_items(state, *, dimensions=None):
    queue = build_work_queue(
        state,
        options=QueueBuildOptions(
            status="open",
            include_subjective=True,
            count=None,
        ),
    )
    count = 0
    for item in queue.get("items", []):
        if item.get("kind") != "subjective_dimension":
            continue
        # Stale reruns are not blocking backlog — they ARE the work
        if item.get("stale_review"):
            continue  # GATE EXCEPTION: stale is the work, not blocking work
        if normalized_dims is None:
            count += 1
```

_objective_backlog = 0
if _breakdown is not None:
    _objective_backlog = max(0, _breakdown.queue_total - _breakdown.subjective)

show_dimension_table(state, dim_scores, objective_backlog=_objective_backlog)

### `desloppify/app/commands/helpers/subjective.py` (lines 20-58)
**Decision:** Suppress quality nudges when objective backlog > 0

```python
def print_subjective_followup(followup, *, objective_backlog: int = 0):
    if followup.low_assessed and objective_backlog <= 0:
        # Print quality improvement suggestions
    if followup.integrity_lines:
        # Always print integrity warnings (anti-gaming)
```

**Policy Comment:** "When *objective_backlog* > 0, quality nudges are suppressed entirely — the queue already surfaces subjective work when it becomes actionable."

**Decision:** Move disappeared findings to superseded (not directly subjective, but manages plan state)

- Synthetic IDs (prefixes: `triage::`, `workflow::`, `subjective::`) are exempted (lines 158-161)
- Synced separately by stale_dimensions module

## 8. **Scan Workflow Orchestration** — `desloppify/app/commands/scan/scan_workflow.py`

### Plan Sync After Scan (lines 112-150, 192-215)
**Orchestration Order:**
1. `reconcile_plan_after_scan()` — prunes dead findings
2. `sync_unscored_dimensions()` — unconditional injection at front
3. `sync_stale_dimensions()` — conditional injection when objective drained
4. `auto_cluster_findings()` — creates clusters with gating
5. `sync_create_plan_needed()` — injects workflow item

## 9. **Configuration** — `desloppify/engine/planning/queue_policy.py`

### `_subjective_threshold()` (lines 15-24)
**Input:** `config.get("target_strict_score", 95.0)`
- Maps to `subjective_threshold` parameter in QueueBuildOptions
- Used in `build_subjective_items()` to filter items below threshold
- Default 95.0, clamped to [0.0, 100.0]

## Key Policy Consolidation Points

### The Three-Tier Injection Policy:

| Item Kind | Condition | Location |
|-----------|-----------|----------|
| **Unscored (placeholder)** | ALWAYS | `sync_unscored_dimensions()` front of queue |
| **Stale (needs re-review)** | `objective_count == 0` | `sync_stale_dimensions()` back of queue |
| **Under-target (improving)** | `objective_count == 0` AND min size 2 | `_sync_subjective_clusters()` back of queue (optional) |

### The Objective-Count Gate:

Used in **5 places**:
1. `sync_stale_dimensions()` — line 279
2. `_sync_subjective_clusters()` — line 492
3. `build_work_queue()` — line 108
4. `print_subjective_followup()` — line 36
5. `_render_dimension_legend()` — line 65

Computed as:
```python
objective_count = sum(
    1 for f in findings.values()
    if f.get("status") == "open"
    and f.get("detector") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
    and not f.get("suppressed")
)
```

### Exceptions to the Gate:
- **Stale review in preflight:** Counted as the work itself, not blocking work (line 90 in preflight.py)
- **Integrity warnings:** Always shown regardless of backlog (line 52 in subjective.py)

## Ready for Refactoring

All 9 decision points are now documented. For consolidation, I recommend creating a new module `desloppify/engine/_policy/subjective_visibility.py` with a single policy function that encapsulates:
- Objective backlog computation (with NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS)
- Three-tier injection conditions
- All threshold/minimum size checks
- Exception handling (stale review, integrity warnings)

This would replace the scattered logic currently spread across 5+ files.

> DEVELOPER

Based on thorough codebase exploration, I need to design a refactoring plan to consolidate scattered "should subjective work surface?" logic into a single policy module.

## Current State (Problems)

The "should subjective work surface?" decision is made independently in 5+ places:

### 1. Objective backlog computation — duplicated in 5 places
Each uses this pattern but with slightly different variable names:
```python
any/sum(
    f.get("status") == "open"
    and f.get("detector") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
    and not f.get("suppressed")
    for f in findings.values()
)
```
Locations:
- `stale_dimensions.py:279` (sync_stale_dimensions — `has_real_items`)
- `auto_cluster.py:492` (_sync_subjective_clusters — `has_objective_items`)
- `core.py:87` (build_work_queue — `objective_count`)
- `subjective.py:36` (print_subjective_followup — `objective_backlog` param)
- `status_cmd.py:120` (cmd_status — `_objective_backlog` computed from breakdown)

### 2. Three-tier gating policy — scattered
- **Unscored (initial_review)**: ALWAYS surface. Implemented in:
  - sync_unscored_dimensions() — unconditional injection
  - build_work_queue() — `initial_review` items always pass
  - item_sort_key() — tier -3 priority
- **Stale**: Only when objective backlog empty. Implemented in:
  - sync_stale_dimensions() — `has_real_items` guard
  - auto_cluster.py — `stale_state_ids` filtering (Bug 1 fix)
  - build_work_queue() — blocked when `not initial_review and objective_count > 0`
- **Under-target**: Only when objective backlog empty. Implemented in:
  - auto_cluster.py — `has_objective_items` guard + eviction (Bug 2 fix)
  - build_work_queue() — same gating as stale (falls through)

### 3. Dimension key normalization — duplicated
- `_normalize_dimension_key()` in preflight.py
- `_canonical_subjective_dimension_key()` in scorecard_projection.py
- Slugify patterns in work_queue helpers

### 4. Classification functions — reasonably centralized but consumers duplicate decisions
`_current_stale_ids()`, `current_unscored_ids()`, `current_under_target_ids()` all live in stale_dimensions.py, which is good. But their consumers each independently compute the backlog check and decide what to do.

### 5. Review preflight — related policy
`review_rerun_preflight()` in preflight.py gates review reruns when "backlog exists" (both objective + open subjective queue). It explicitly excludes stale_review items from the blocking count because stale items ARE the work.

## Design Proposal

### New module: `desloppify/engine/_plan/subjective_policy.py`

A single policy class that encapsulates ALL the decisions:

```python
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SubjectiveVisibility:
    """Single source of truth for subjective dimension visibility."""
    
    # Classification sets
    stale_ids: frozenset[str]
    unscored_ids: frozenset[str]
    under_target_ids: frozenset[str]
    
    # Backlog state
    objective_open_count: int
    
    @property
    def has_objective_backlog(self) -> bool:
        return self.objective_open_count > 0
    
    # Policy decisions
    @property
    def surface_unscored(self) -> bool:
        """Unscored dims always surface (onboarding priority)."""
        return True
    
    @property
    def surface_stale(self) -> bool:
        """Stale dims only surface when objective backlog is drained."""
        return not self.has_objective_backlog
    
    @property
    def surface_under_target(self) -> bool:
        """Under-target dims only surface when objective backlog is drained."""
        return not self.has_objective_backlog
    
    def should_surface(self, item_id: str, *, initial_review: bool = False) -> bool:
        """Should a subjective item appear in the work queue?"""
        if initial_review or item_id in self.unscored_ids:
            return True
        return not self.has_objective_backlog
    
    def should_inject_to_queue(self, item_id: str) -> bool:
        """Should a subjective ID be injected into plan queue_order?"""
        if item_id in self.unscored_ids:
            return True
        if item_id in self.stale_ids:
            return self.surface_stale
        if item_id in self.under_target_ids:
            return self.surface_under_target
        return False
    
    def should_evict_from_queue(self, item_id: str) -> bool:
        """Should a subjective ID be evicted from plan queue_order?"""
        if item_id in self.unscored_ids:
            return False  # never evict unscored
        if item_id in self.under_target_ids and self.has_objective_backlog:
            return True
        return False
```

### Factory function
```python
def compute_subjective_visibility(
    state: StateModel,
    *,
    target_strict: float = 95.0,
) -> SubjectiveVisibility:
    """Compute the current subjective visibility policy from state."""
    findings = state.get("findings", {})
    objective_count = sum(
        1 for f in findings.values()
        if f.get("status") == "open"
        and f.get("detector") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
        and not f.get("suppressed")
    )
    return SubjectiveVisibility(
        stale_ids=frozenset(_current_stale_ids(state)),
        unscored_ids=frozenset(current_unscored_ids(state)),
        under_target_ids=frozenset(current_under_target_ids(state, target_strict=target_strict)),
        objective_open_count=objective_count,
    )
```

## Consumer Refactoring

### 1. `sync_stale_dimensions()` — use `policy.surface_stale`
Replace:
```python
has_real_items = any(...)
if not has_real_items and stale_ids:
```
With:
```python
if policy.surface_stale and stale_ids:
```

### 2. `_sync_subjective_clusters()` — use `policy` throughout
Replace 3 separate computations with policy lookups:
- `stale_state_ids` → `policy.stale_ids`
- `has_objective_items` → `policy.has_objective_backlog`
- Under-target eviction → `policy.should_evict_from_queue()`

### 3. `build_work_queue()` — use `policy.should_surface()`
Replace:
```python
objective_count = sum(...)
if not item.get("initial_review") and objective_count > 0:
    continue
```
With:
```python
if not policy.should_surface(item["id"], initial_review=item.get("initial_review", False)):
    continue
```

### 4. `print_subjective_followup()` — use `policy.has_objective_backlog`
Replace: `objective_backlog > 0` parameter with `policy.has_objective_backlog`

### 5. Review preflight — use `policy.has_objective_backlog`
The preflight already has distinct logic (counts both objective AND open subjective queue items as blocking). It should still use `policy.has_objective_backlog` for the objective part.

## Also clean up:
- Move `NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS` to the new policy module (it's the policy's concept)
- Keep `SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX` in stale_dimensions.py (it's an ID format, not policy)
- Consider: should classification functions move to the policy module too? They're already in stale_dimensions.py which is getting overloaded.

## Key constraints:
- The policy object must be frozen/immutable — compute once, use everywhere in a single operation
- Must not break the existing scan workflow order (sync functions run sequentially)
- Must maintain backward compatibility for all public APIs
- Tests should be updated to use the policy where they currently construct ad-hoc checks

## Questions to consider:
1. Should the policy be computed once in scan_workflow and passed down, or should each consumer compute it?
   - Recommendation: Compute once and pass down. The scan workflow already loads state once.
2. Should classification functions move from stale_dimensions.py to the new policy module?
   - Recommendation: Move them. stale_dimensions.py is overloaded with ID helpers + sync functions + classification.
3. Should the preflight use the same policy object?
   - Recommendation: Yes for objective_backlog, but keep its own subjective queue counting (different purpose).

   147→

   149→    but whose score hasn't reached the target yet.

   152→    from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (

   161→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   162→
   163→    under_target: set[str] = set()
   164→    for entry in scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
   165→        if entry.get("placeholder") or entry.get("stale"):
   166→            continue
   167→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   168→        if strict_val >= target_strict:
   169→            continue
   170→        dim_key = entry.get("dimension_key", "")
   171→        if not dim_key:
   172→            continue
   173→        fid = f"{SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX}{slugify(dim_key)}"
   174→        if fid not in stale_ids and fid not in unscored_ids:
   175→            under_target.add(fid)
   176→    return under_target

   178→
   179→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   180→# Promoted-aware insertion helper
   181→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   182→
   183→def _after_promoted(order: list[str], plan: PlanModel) -> int:
   184→    """Return the insertion index just after the last promoted item in *order*.
   185→
   186→    When no promoted items are present (or none are in *order*), returns 0
   187→    so callers fall back to existing front-of-queue behavior.
   188→    """
   189→    promoted = set(plan.get("promoted_ids", []))
   190→    if not promoted:
   191→        return 0
   192→    last_idx = -1
   193→    for i, fid in enumerate(order):
   194→        if fid in promoted:
   195→            last_idx = i
   196→    return last_idx + 1 if last_idx >= 0 else 0
   197→
   198→
   199→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   200→# Unscored dimension sync (front of queue, unconditional)
   201→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   202→
   203→def sync_unscored_dimensions(
   204→    plan: PlanModel,
   205→    state: StateModel,
   206→) -> UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
   207→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions.
   208→
   209→    1. **Prune** — remove ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are
   210→       no longer unscored AND not stale (avoids pruning stale IDs — that is
   211→       ``sync_stale_dimensions``' responsibility).
   212→    2. **Inject** — unconditionally prepend currently-unscored IDs to the
   213→       *front* of ``queue_order`` so initial reviews are the first priority.
   214→    """
   215→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   216→    result = UnscoredDimensionSyncResult()
   217→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   218→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   219→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   220→
   221→    # --- Cleanup: prune subjective IDs that are no longer unscored --------
   222→    # Only prune IDs that are neither unscored nor stale (stale sync owns those).
   223→    to_remove: list[str] = [
   224→        fid for fid in order
   225→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   226→        and fid not in unscored_ids
   227→        and fid not in stale_ids
   228→    ]
   229→    for fid in to_remove:
   230→        order.remove(fid)
   231→        result.pruned.append(fid)
   232→
   233→    # --- Inject: prepend unscored IDs after any promoted items -------------
   234→    existing = set(order)
   235→    insert_at = _after_promoted(order, plan)
   236→    for uid in reversed(sorted(unscored_ids)):
   237→        if uid not in existing:
   238→            order.insert(insert_at, uid)
   239→            result.injected.append(uid)
   240→
   241→    return result
   242→
   243→
   244→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   245→# Stale dimension sync (back of queue, conditional)
   246→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   247→
   248→def sync_stale_dimensions(
   249→    plan: PlanModel,
   250→    state: StateModel,
   251→) -> StaleDimensionSyncResult:
   252→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with stale subjective dimensions.
   253→
   254→    1. Remove any ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are no
   255→       longer stale and not unscored (avoids pruning IDs owned by
   256→       ``sync_unscored_dimensions``).
   257→    2. If no objective items remain after cleanup, inject all currently-stale
   258→       dimension IDs so the plan surfaces them as actionable work.
   259→    """
   260→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   261→    result = StaleDimensionSyncResult()
   262→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   263→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   264→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   265→
   266→    # --- Cleanup: prune resolved subjective IDs --------------------------
   267→    # Only prune IDs that are neither stale nor unscored.
   268→    to_remove: list[str] = [
   269→        fid for fid in order
   270→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   271→        and fid not in stale_ids
   272→        and fid not in unscored_ids
   273→    ]
   274→    for fid in to_remove:
   275→        order.remove(fid)
   276→        result.pruned.append(fid)
   277→
   278→    # --- Inject: populate when no objective items remain -----------------
   279→    has_real_items = any(
   280→        f.get("status") == "open"
   281→        and f.get("detector") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   282→        and not f.get("suppressed")
   283→        for f in state.get("findings", {}).values()
   284→    )
   285→    if not has_real_items and stale_ids:
   286→        existing = set(order)
   287→        for sid in sorted(stale_ids):
   288→            if sid not in existing:
   289→                order.append(sid)
   290→                result.injected.append(sid)
   291→
   292→    return result
   293→
   294→
   295→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   296→# Triage snapshot hash + sync
   297→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   298→
   299→def review_finding_snapshot_hash(state: StateModel) -> str:
   300→    """Hash open review finding IDs to detect changes.
   301→
   302→    Returns empty string when there are no open review findings.
   303→    """
   304→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   305→    review_ids = sorted(
   306→        fid for fid, f in findings.items()
   307→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   308→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   309→    )
   310→    if not review_ids:
   311→        return ""
   312→    return hashlib.sha256("|".join(review_ids).encode()).hexdigest()[:16]
   313→
   314→
   315→@dataclass
   316→class TriageSyncResult:
   317→    """What changed during a triage sync."""
   318→
   319→    injected: bool = False
   320→    pruned: bool = False
   321→
   322→    @property
   323→    def changes(self) -> int:
   324→        return int(self.injected) + int(self.pruned)
   325→
   326→
   327→def sync_triage_needed(
   328→    plan: PlanModel,
   329→    state: StateModel,
   330→) -> TriageSyncResult:
   331→    """Inject 4 triage stage IDs at front of queue when review findings change.
   332→
   333→    Only injects stages not already confirmed in ``epic_triage_meta``.
   334→    Never auto-prunes — only explicit completion removes them.
   335→
   336→    When findings are *resolved* (current IDs are a subset of previously
   337→    triaged IDs), the snapshot hash is updated silently — no re-triage
   338→    is needed since the user is working through the plan.
   339→    """
   340→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   341→    result = TriageSyncResult()
   342→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   343→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   344→    confirmed = set(meta.get("triage_stages", {}).keys())
   345→
   346→    # Check if any triage stage is already in queue
   347→    already_present = any(sid in order for sid in TRIAGE_IDS)
   348→
   349→    current_hash = review_finding_snapshot_hash(state)
   350→    last_hash = meta.get("finding_snapshot_hash", "")
   351→
   352→    if current_hash and current_hash != last_hash and not already_present:
   353→        # Distinguish "new findings appeared" from "findings were resolved".
   354→        # Only re-triage when genuinely new findings exist.
   355→        findings = state.get("findings", {})
   356→        current_review_ids = {
   357→            fid for fid, f in findings.items()
   358→            if f.get("status") == "open"
   359→            and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   360→        }
   361→        triaged_ids = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
   362→        new_since_triage = current_review_ids - triaged_ids
   363→
   364→        if new_since_triage:
   365→            # New review findings appeared — re-triage needed
   366→            insert_at = _after_promoted(order, plan)
   367→            stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "commit")
   368→            existing = set(order)
   369→            injected_count = 0
   370→            for sid, name in zip(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS, stage_names):
   371→                if name not in confirmed and sid not in existing:
   372→                    order.insert(insert_at + injected_count, sid)
   373→                    injected_count += 1
   374→            if injected_count:
   375→                result.injected = True
   376→        else:
   377→            # Only resolved findings changed the hash — update silently
   378→            meta["finding_snapshot_hash"] = current_hash
   379→            plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
   380→
   381→    return result
   382→
   383→
   384→@dataclass
   385→class CreatePlanSyncResult:
   386→    """What changed during a create-plan sync."""
   387→
   388→    injected: bool = False
   389→
   390→    @property
   391→    def changes(self) -> int:
   392→        return int(self.injected)
   393→
   394→
   395→def sync_create_plan_needed(
   396→    plan: PlanModel,
   397→    state: StateModel,
   398→) -> CreatePlanSyncResult:
   399→    """Inject ``workflow::create-plan`` when reviews complete + objective backlog exists.
   400→
   401→    Only injects when:
   402→    - No unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions remain
   403→    - At least one objective finding exists
   404→    - ``workflow::create-plan`` is not already in the queue
   405→    - No triage stages are pending
   406→    """
   407→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   408→    result = CreatePlanSyncResult()
   409→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   410→
   411→    if WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID in order:
   412→        return result
   413→
   414→    # Don't inject if triage stages are pending
   415→    if any(sid in order for sid in TRIAGE_IDS):
   416→        return result
   417→
   418→    # Check that no unscored dimensions remain
   419→    unscored = current_unscored_ids(state)
   420→    if unscored:
   421→        return result
   422→
   423→    # Check that objective findings exist
   424→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   425→    has_objective = any(
   426→        f.get("status") == "open"
   427→        and f.get("detector") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   428→        for f in findings.values()
   429→    )
   430→    if not has_objective:
   431→        return result
   432→
   433→    # Insert after any subjective items, before findings
   434→    insert_at = 0
   435→    for i, fid in enumerate(order):
   436→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) or fid.startswith(TRIAGE_PREFIX):
   437→            insert_at = i + 1
   438→    order.insert(insert_at, WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID)
   439→    result.injected = True
   440→    return result
   441→
   442→
   443→def compute_new_finding_ids(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
   444→    """Return the set of open review/concerns finding IDs added since last triage.
   445→
   446→    Returns an empty set when no prior triage has recorded ``triaged_ids``.
   447→    """
   448→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   449→    triaged = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", meta.get("synthesized_ids", [])))
   450→    current = {
   451→        fid for fid, f in state.get("findings", {}).items()
   452→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   453→    }
   454→    return current - triaged if triaged else set()
   455→
   456→
   457→def is_triage_stale(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> bool:
   458→    """Side-effect-free check: is triage needed?
   459→
   460→    Returns True when any ``triage::*`` stage ID is in the queue OR
   461→    genuinely *new* review findings appeared since the last triage.
   462→
   463→    When findings are merely resolved (current IDs are a subset of
   464→    previously triaged IDs), triage is NOT stale — the user is working
   465→    through the plan.
   466→    """
   467→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   468→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   469→    if order & TRIAGE_IDS:
   470→        return True
   471→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   472→    last_hash = meta.get("finding_snapshot_hash", "")
   473→    if not last_hash:
   474→        return False
   475→    current_hash = review_finding_snapshot_hash(state)
   476→    if not current_hash or current_hash == last_hash:
   477→        return False
   478→    # Hash changed — check if new findings appeared or only resolutions
   479→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   480→    current_review_ids = {
   481→        fid for fid, f in findings.items()
   482→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   483→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   484→    }
   485→    triaged_ids = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
   486→    new_since_triage = current_review_ids - triaged_ids
   487→    return bool(new_since_triage)
   488→
   489→
   490→
   491→__all__ = [
   492→    "NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS",
   493→    "SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX",
   494→    "TRIAGE_ID",
   495→    "TRIAGE_IDS",
   496→    "TRIAGE_PREFIX",
   497→    "TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS",
   498→    "SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES",
   499→    "WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID",
   500→    "WORKFLOW_PREFIX",
   501→    "CreatePlanSyncResult",
   502→    "StaleDimensionSyncResult",
   503→    "TriageSyncResult",
   504→    "UnscoredDimensionSyncResult",
   505→    "current_under_target_ids",
   506→    "current_unscored_ids",
   507→    "compute_new_finding_ids",
   508→    "is_triage_stale",
   509→    "review_finding_snapshot_hash",
   510→    "sync_create_plan_needed",
   511→    "sync_stale_dimensions",
   512→    "sync_triage_needed",
   513→    "sync_unscored_dimensions",
   514→]
   515→

    30→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    31→# Grouping key computation
    32→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    33→
    34→def _extract_subtype(finding: dict) -> str | None:
    35→    """Extract the subtype/kind from a finding.
    36→
    37→    Checks detail.kind first, then falls back to parsing the finding ID
    38→    (format: ``detector::file::subtype`` or ``detector::file::symbol::subtype``).
    39→    """
    40→    detail = finding.get("detail") or {}
    41→    kind = detail.get("kind")
    42→    if kind:
    43→        return kind
    44→
    45→    # Parse from finding ID — third segment is often the subtype
    46→    fid = finding.get("id", "")
    47→    parts = fid.split("::")
    48→    if len(parts) >= 3:
    49→        # For IDs like smells::file.py::silent_except, the subtype is parts[2]
    50→        # For IDs like dict_keys::file.py::key::phantom_read, subtype is last part
    51→        candidate = parts[-1]
    52→        # Skip if it looks like a file path or symbol name (contains / or .)
    53→        if "/" not in candidate and "." not in candidate:
    54→            return candidate
    55→    return None
    56→
    57→
    58→def _grouping_key(finding: dict, meta: DetectorMeta | None) -> str | None:
    59→    """Compute a deterministic grouping key for a finding.
    60→
    61→    Returns None if the finding should not be auto-clustered.
    62→    """
    63→    detector = finding.get("detector", "")
    64→
    65→    if meta is None:
    66→        # Unknown detector — group by detector name
    67→        return f"detector::{detector}"
    68→
    69→    # Review findings → group by dimension
    70→    if detector in ("review", "subjective_review"):
    71→        detail = finding.get("detail") or {}
    72→        dimension = detail.get("dimension", "")
    73→        if dimension:
    74→            return f"review::{dimension}"
    75→        return f"detector::{detector}"
    76→
    77→    # Per-file detectors (structural, responsibility_cohesion)
    78→    if meta.needs_judgment and detector in (
    79→        "structural", "responsibility_cohesion",
    80→    ):
    81→        file = finding.get("file", "")
    82→        if file:
    83→            basename = os.path.basename(file)
    84→            return f"file::{detector}::{basename}"
    85→
    86→    # Needs-judgment detectors — group by subtype when available
    87→    if meta.needs_judgment:
    88→        subtype = _extract_subtype(finding)
    89→        if subtype:
    90→            return f"typed::{detector}::{subtype}"
    91→
    92→    # Pure auto-fix (no judgment needed) → all findings by detector
    93→    if meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and not meta.needs_judgment:
    94→        return f"auto::{detector}"
    95→
    96→    # Everything else → by detector
    97→    return f"detector::{detector}"
    98→
    99→
   100→def _cluster_name_from_key(key: str) -> str:
   101→    """Convert a grouping key to a cluster name with auto/ prefix."""
   102→    # auto::unused → auto/unused
   103→    # typed::dict_keys::phantom_read → auto/dict_keys-phantom_read
   104→    # file::structural::big_file.py → auto/structural-big_file.py
   105→    # review::abstraction_fitness → auto/review-abstraction_fitness
   106→    # detector::security → auto/security
   107→    parts = key.split("::")
   108→    if len(parts) == 2:
   109→        prefix_type = parts[0]
   110→        # For review keys, keep the prefix for clarity
   111→        if prefix_type == "review":
   112→            return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}review-{parts[1]}"
   113→        return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}{parts[1]}"
   114→    if len(parts) == 3:
   115→        return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}{parts[1]}-{parts[2]}"
   116→    return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}{key.replace('::', '-')}"
   117→
   118→
   119→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   120→# Description and action generation
   121→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   122→
   123→def _generate_description(
   124→    cluster_name: str,
   125→    members: list[dict],
   126→    meta: DetectorMeta | None,
   127→    subtype: str | None,
   128→) -> str:
   129→    """Generate a human-readable cluster description."""
   130→    count = len(members)
   131→    detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
   132→
   133→    if detector in ("review", "subjective_review"):
   134→        detail = (members[0].get("detail") or {}) if members else {}
   135→        dimension = detail.get("dimension", detector)
   136→        return f"Address {count} {dimension} review findings"
   137→
   138→    if detector == "structural":
   139→        files = {os.path.basename(m.get("file", "")) for m in members}
   140→        if len(files) == 1:
   141→            return f"Decompose {next(iter(files))}"
   142→        return f"Decompose {count} large files"
   143→
   144→    display = meta.display if meta else detector
   145→    if subtype:
   146→        label = subtype.replace("_", " ")
   147→        return f"Fix {count} {label} issues"
   148→
   149→    if meta and meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and not meta.needs_judgment:
   150→        return f"Remove {count} {display} findings"
   151→
   152→    return f"Fix {count} {display} issues"
   153→
   154→
   155→def _subtype_has_fixer(meta: DetectorMeta, subtype: str | None) -> str | None:
   156→    """Check if a subtype maps to a specific fixer."""
   157→    if not meta.fixers or not subtype:
   158→        return None
   159→    # Convention: fixer name is subtype with _ replaced by -
   160→    fixer_name = subtype.replace("_", "-")
   161→    if fixer_name in meta.fixers:
   162→        return fixer_name
   163→    # Also check if subtype is a substring (e.g. "unused" matches "unused-imports")
   164→    for fixer in meta.fixers:
   165→        if subtype in fixer:
   166→            return fixer
   167→    return None
   168→
   169→
   170→def _strip_guidance_examples(guidance: str) -> str:
   171→    """Strip subtype-specific examples from guidance text.
   172→
   173→    Many guidance strings have format "verb — specific examples".
   174→    Strip after the dash to get the core action:
   175→      "fix code smells — dead useEffect, empty if chains" → "fix code smells"
   176→    """
   177→    if " — " in guidance:
   178→        return guidance.split(" — ", 1)[0].strip()
   179→    return guidance
   180→
   181→
   182→_ACTION_TYPE_TEMPLATES: dict[str, str] = {
   183→    "reorganize": "reorganize with desloppify move",
   184→    "refactor": "review and refactor each finding",
   185→    "manual_fix": "review and fix each finding",
   186→}
   187→
   188→
   189→def _generate_action(
   190→    meta: DetectorMeta | None,
   191→    subtype: str | None,
   192→) -> str:
   193→    """Generate an action string from detector metadata.
   194→
   195→    Always returns a non-empty string — every cluster gets an action.
   196→    """
   197→    if meta is None:
   198→        return "review and fix each finding"
   199→
   200→    # For detectors with subtypes, only suggest a fixer if the subtype matches
   201→    if subtype and meta.fixers:
   202→        matched_fixer = _subtype_has_fixer(meta, subtype)
   203→        if matched_fixer:
   204→            return f"desloppify fix {matched_fixer} --dry-run"
   205→    elif meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and meta.fixers and not meta.needs_judgment:
   206→        # Pure auto-fix detector, no subtypes
   207→        return f"desloppify fix {meta.fixers[0]} --dry-run"
   208→
   209→    if meta.tool == "move":
   210→        return "desloppify move"
   211→
   212→    # Guidance available — use it (strip examples for subtyped detectors)
   213→    if meta.guidance:
   214→        if subtype:
   215→            return _strip_guidance_examples(meta.guidance)
   216→        return meta.guidance
   217→
   218→    # Final fallback: action_type template
   219→    return _ACTION_TYPE_TEMPLATES.get(
   220→        meta.action_type, "review and fix each finding"
   221→    )
   222→
