   354→def _configure_lang_runtime(
   355→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   356→    config: dict[str, object],
   357→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   358→    lang: LangRun | None,
   359→) -> LangRun | None:
   360→    """Populate runtime context and threshold overrides for a selected language."""
   361→    if not lang:
   362→        return None
   363→
   364→    lang_options = resolve_lang_runtime_options(args, lang)
   365→    lang_settings = resolve_lang_settings(config, lang)
   366→    runtime_lang = make_lang_run(
   367→        lang,
   368→        overrides=LangRunOverrides(
   369→            review_cache=state.get("review_cache", {}),
   370→            review_max_age_days=config.get("review_max_age_days", 30),
   371→            runtime_settings=lang_settings,
   372→            runtime_options=lang_options,
   373→            large_threshold_override=config.get("large_files_threshold", 0),
   374→            props_threshold_override=config.get("props_threshold", 0),
   375→        ),
   376→    )
   377→
   378→    lang_capabilities = _state_lang_capabilities(state)
   379→    lang_capabilities[runtime_lang.name] = {
   380→        "fixers": sorted(runtime_lang.fixers.keys()),
   381→        "typecheck_cmd": runtime_lang.typecheck_cmd,
   382→    }
   383→    return runtime_lang
   384→
   385→
   386→def _reset_subjective_assessments_for_scan_reset(
   387→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   388→    *,
   389→    lang_name: str | None = None,
   390→) -> int:
   391→    """Reset known subjective dimensions to 0 so the next scan starts fresh."""
   392→    assessments = _state_subjective_assessments(state)
   393→
   394→    reset_keys = {
   395→        key.strip()
   396→        for key in assessments
   397→        if isinstance(key, str) and key.strip()
   398→    }
   399→    reset_keys.update(_subjective_reset_dimensions(lang_name=lang_name))
   400→
   401→    now = state_mod.utc_now()
   402→    for key in sorted(reset_keys):
   403→        payload = assessments.get(key)
   404→        if isinstance(payload, dict):
   405→            payload["score"] = 0.0
   406→            payload["source"] = "scan_reset_subjective"
   407→            payload["assessed_at"] = now
   408→            payload["reset_by"] = "scan_reset_subjective"
   409→            payload["placeholder"] = True
   410→            payload.pop("integrity_penalty", None)
   411→            payload.pop("components", None)
   412→            payload.pop("component_scores", None)
   413→            continue
   414→        assessments[key] = {
   415→            "score": 0.0,
   416→            "source": "scan_reset_subjective",
   417→            "assessed_at": now,
   418→            "reset_by": "scan_reset_subjective",
   419→            "placeholder": True,
   420→        }
   421→    return len(reset_keys)
   422→
   423→
   424→def _expire_provisional_manual_override_assessments(
   425→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   426→) -> int:
   427→    """Expire provisional manual-override assessments at scan start."""
   428→    assessments = _state_subjective_assessments(state)
   429→
   430→    now = state_mod.utc_now()
   431→    expired = 0
   432→    for payload in assessments.values():
   433→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
   434→            continue
   435→        if payload.get("provisional_override") is not True:
   436→            continue
   437→        payload["score"] = 0.0
   438→        payload["source"] = "manual_override_expired"
   439→        payload["assessed_at"] = now
   440→        payload["reset_by"] = "manual_override_expired"
   441→        payload["placeholder"] = True
   442→        payload.pop("provisional_override", None)
   443→        payload.pop("provisional_until_scan", None)
   444→        payload.pop("integrity_penalty", None)
   445→        payload.pop("components", None)
   446→        payload.pop("component_scores", None)
   447→        expired += 1
   448→    return expired
   449→
   450→
   451→def prepare_scan_runtime(args) -> ScanRuntime:
   452→    """Resolve state/config/language and apply scan-time runtime settings."""
   453→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   454→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   455→    state = runtime.state if isinstance(runtime.state, dict) else {}
   456→    state_mod.ensure_state_defaults(state)
   457→    path = Path(args.path)
   458→    config = runtime.config if isinstance(runtime.config, dict) else {}
   459→    lang_config = resolve_lang(args)
   460→    reset_subjective_count = 0
   461→    expired_manual_override_count = _expire_provisional_manual_override_assessments(
   462→        state
   463→    )
   464→    if getattr(args, "reset_subjective", False):
   465→        reset_subjective_count = _reset_subjective_assessments_for_scan_reset(
   466→            state,
   467→            lang_name=getattr(lang_config, "name", None),
   468→        )
   469→
   470→    include_slow = not getattr(args, "skip_slow", False)
   471→    profile = _resolve_scan_profile(getattr(args, "profile", None), lang_config)
   472→    effective_include_slow = _effective_include_slow(include_slow, profile)
   473→
   474→    lang = _configure_lang_runtime(args, config, state, lang_config)
   475→    coverage_warnings = _seed_runtime_coverage_warnings(lang)
   476→    zone_overrides_raw = config.get("zone_overrides")
   477→    zone_overrides = zone_overrides_raw if isinstance(zone_overrides_raw, dict) else None
   478→
   479→    return ScanRuntime(
   480→        args=args,
   481→        state_path=state_file,
   482→        state=state,
   483→        path=path,
   484→        config=config,
   485→        lang=lang,
   486→        lang_label=f" ({lang.name})" if lang else "",
   487→        profile=profile,
   488→        effective_include_slow=effective_include_slow,
   489→        zone_overrides=zone_overrides,
   490→        reset_subjective_count=reset_subjective_count,
   491→        expired_manual_override_count=expired_manual_override_count,
   492→        coverage_warnings=coverage_warnings,
   493→    )
   494→
   495→
   496→def _augment_with_stale_exclusion_findings(
   497→    findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   498→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   499→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   500→    """Append stale exclude findings when excluded dirs are unreferenced."""
   501→    extra_exclusions = get_exclusions()
   502→    if not (extra_exclusions and runtime.lang and runtime.lang.file_finder):
   503→        return findings
   504→
   505→    scanned_files = runtime.lang.file_finder(runtime.path)
   506→    stale = _audit_excluded_dirs(
   507→        extra_exclusions, scanned_files, PROJECT_ROOT
   508→    )
   509→    if not stale:
   510→        return findings
   511→
   512→    augmented = list(findings)
   513→    augmented.extend(stale)
   514→    for stale_finding in stale:
   515→        print(colorize(f"  ℹ {stale_finding['summary']}", "dim"))
   516→    return augmented
   517→
   518→
   519→def _augment_with_stale_wontfix_findings(
   520→    findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   521→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   522→    *,
   523→    decay_scans: int,
   524→) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], int]:
   525→    """Append re-triage findings for stale/worsening wontfix debt."""
   526→    return _augment_stale_wontfix_impl(
   527→        findings,
   528→        state=runtime.state,
   529→        scan_path=runtime.path,
   530→        project_root=PROJECT_ROOT,
   531→        decay_scans=decay_scans,
   532→    )
   533→
   534→
   535→def run_scan_generation(
   536→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   537→) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], dict[str, object], dict[str, object] | None]:
   538→    """Run detector pipeline and return findings, potentials, and codebase metrics."""
   539→    enable_file_cache()
   540→    enable_parse_cache()
   541→    try:
   542→        findings, potentials = plan_mod.generate_findings(
   543→            runtime.path,
   544→            lang=runtime.lang,
   545→            options=PlanScanOptions(
   546→                include_slow=runtime.effective_include_slow,
   547→                zone_overrides=runtime.zone_overrides,
   548→                profile=runtime.profile,
   549→            ),
   550→        )
   551→    finally:
   552→        disable_parse_cache()
   553→        disable_file_cache()
   554→
   555→    codebase_metrics = _collect_codebase_metrics(runtime.lang, runtime.path)
   556→    _warn_explicit_lang_with_no_files(
   557→        runtime.args, runtime.lang, runtime.path, codebase_metrics
   558→    )
   559→    findings = _augment_with_stale_exclusion_findings(findings, runtime)
   560→    decay_scans = _coerce_int(
   561→        runtime.config.get("wontfix_decay_scans"),
   562→        default=_WONTFIX_DECAY_SCANS_DEFAULT,
   563→    )
   564→    findings, monitored_wontfix = _augment_with_stale_wontfix_findings(
   565→        findings,
   566→        runtime,
   567→        decay_scans=max(decay_scans, 0),
   568→    )
   569→    potentials["stale_wontfix"] = monitored_wontfix
   570→    return findings, potentials, codebase_metrics
   571→
   572→
   573→def merge_scan_results(
   574→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   575→    findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   576→    potentials: dict[str, object],
   577→    codebase_metrics: dict[str, object] | None,
   578→) -> ScanMergeResult:
   579→    """Merge findings into persistent state and return diff + previous score snapshot."""
   580→    scan_path_rel = rel(str(runtime.path))
   581→    prev_scan_path = runtime.state.get("scan_path")
   582→    path_changed = prev_scan_path is not None and prev_scan_path != scan_path_rel
   583→
   584→    if not path_changed:
   585→        prev = state_mod.score_snapshot(runtime.state)
   586→    else:
   587→        prev = state_mod.ScoreSnapshot(None, None, None, None)
   588→    prev_dim_scores = (
   589→        runtime.state.get("dimension_scores", {}) if not path_changed else {}
   590→    )
   591→
   592→    if runtime.lang and runtime.lang.zone_map is not None:
   593→        runtime.state["zone_distribution"] = runtime.lang.zone_map.counts()
   594→    _persist_scan_coverage(runtime.state, runtime.lang)
   595→
   596→    target_score = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config, fallback=95.0)
   597→
   598→    diff = state_mod.merge_scan(
   599→        runtime.state,
   600→        findings,
   601→        options=state_mod.MergeScanOptions(
   602→            lang=runtime.lang.name if runtime.lang else None,
   603→            scan_path=scan_path_rel,
   604→            force_resolve=getattr(runtime.args, "force_resolve", False),
   605→            exclude=get_exclusions(),
   606→            potentials=potentials,
   607→            codebase_metrics=codebase_metrics,
   608→            include_slow=runtime.effective_include_slow,
   609→            ignore=runtime.config.get("ignore", []),
   610→            subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   611→        ),
   612→    )
   613→
   614→    issues_mod.expire_stale_holistic(
   615→        runtime.state, runtime.config.get("holistic_max_age_days", 30)
   616→    )
   617→    state_mod.save_state(
   618→        runtime.state,
   619→        runtime.state_path,
   620→        subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   621→    )
   622→
   623→    _clear_needs_rescan_flag(runtime.config)
   624→    _reconcile_plan_post_scan(runtime)
   625→
   626→    return ScanMergeResult(
   627→        diff=diff,
   628→        prev_overall=prev.overall,
   629→        prev_objective=prev.objective,
   630→        prev_strict=prev.strict,
   631→        prev_verified=prev.verified,
   632→        prev_dim_scores=prev_dim_scores,
   633→    )
   634→
   635→
   636→def resolve_noise_snapshot(
   637→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   638→    config: dict[str, object],
   639→) -> ScanNoiseSnapshot:
   640→    """Resolve noise budget settings and hidden finding counters."""
   641→    noise_budget, global_noise_budget, budget_warning = (
   642→        state_mod.resolve_finding_noise_settings(config)
   643→    )
   644→    findings_by_id = _state_findings(state)
   645→    open_findings = [
   646→        finding
   647→        for finding in state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   648→            findings_by_id, state.get("scan_path")
   649→        ).values()
   650→        if finding.get("status") == "open"
   651→    ]
   652→    _, hidden_by_detector = state_mod.apply_finding_noise_budget(
   653→        open_findings,
   654→        budget=noise_budget,
   655→        global_budget=global_noise_budget,
   656→    )
   657→
   658→    return ScanNoiseSnapshot(
   659→        noise_budget=noise_budget,
   660→        global_noise_budget=global_noise_budget,
   661→        budget_warning=budget_warning,
   662→        hidden_by_detector=hidden_by_detector,
   663→        hidden_total=sum(hidden_by_detector.values()),
   664→    )
   665→
   666→
   667→def persist_reminder_history(
   668→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   669→    narrative: dict[str, object],
   670→) -> None:
   671→    """Persist reminder history emitted by narrative computation."""
   672→    if not (narrative and "reminder_history" in narrative):
   673→        return
   674→
   675→    runtime.state["reminder_history"] = narrative["reminder_history"]
   676→    target_score = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config, fallback=95.0)
   677→    state_mod.save_state(
   678→        runtime.state,
   679→        runtime.state_path,
   680→        subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   681→    )
   682→
   683→
   684→__all__ = [
   685→    "ScanStateContractError",
   686→    "ScanMergeResult",
   687→    "ScanNoiseSnapshot",
   688→    "ScanRuntime",
   689→    "merge_scan_results",
   690→    "persist_reminder_history",
   691→    "prepare_scan_runtime",
   692→    "resolve_noise_snapshot",
   693→    "run_scan_generation",
   694→]
   695→

1→"""Ranking and grouping helpers for work queue selection."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
     6→    ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY,
     7→    detail_dict,
     8→    is_review_finding,
     9→    is_subjective_finding,
    10→    primary_command_for_finding,
    11→    review_finding_weight,
    12→    scope_matches,
    13→    slugify,
    14→    status_matches,
    15→    subjective_strict_scores,
    16→    supported_fixers_for_item,
    17→)
    18→from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
    19→from desloppify.engine.planning.common import CONFIDENCE_ORDER
    20→from desloppify.state import path_scoped_findings
    21→
    22→
    23→def enrich_with_impact(items: list[dict], dimension_scores: dict) -> None:
    24→    """Stamp ``estimated_impact`` on each item based on dimension-level headroom.
    25→
    26→    Impact = ``overall_per_point * headroom`` where headroom = ``100 - score``.
    27→    Items in dimensions with more score headroom sort first.
    28→    """
    29→    if not dimension_scores:
    30→        for item in items:
    31→            item["estimated_impact"] = 0.0
    32→        return
    33→
    34→    from desloppify.engine._scoring.results.health import compute_health_breakdown
    35→    from desloppify.engine._scoring.results.impact import get_dimension_for_detector
    36→
    37→    breakdown = compute_health_breakdown(dimension_scores)
    38→    entries = breakdown.get("entries", [])
    39→
    40→    # Build lookup: normalized dimension name -> {per_point, headroom}
    41→    dim_impact: dict[str, dict[str, float]] = {}
    42→    for entry in entries:
    43→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
    44→        if not name:
    45→            continue
    46→        per_point = float(entry.get("overall_per_point", 0.0))
    47→        score = float(entry.get("score", 0.0))
    48→        headroom = 100.0 - score
    49→        dim_impact[name.lower()] = {"per_point": per_point, "headroom": headroom}
    50→
    51→    for item in items:
    52→        impact = _compute_item_impact(item, dim_impact, get_dimension_for_detector)
    53→        item["estimated_impact"] = impact
    54→
    55→
    56→def _compute_item_impact(
    57→    item: dict,
    58→    dim_impact: dict[str, dict[str, float]],
    59→    get_dimension_for_detector,
    60→) -> float:
    61→    """Compute impact value for a single queue item."""
    62→    kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
    63→
    64→    # Subjective items (synthetic dimensions or subjective findings):
    65→    # look up by detail.dimension_name
    66→    if kind == "subjective_dimension" or item.get("is_subjective"):
    67→        dim_name = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
    68→        entry = dim_impact.get(dim_name.lower())
    69→        if entry:
    70→            return entry["per_point"] * entry["headroom"]
    71→        return 0.0
    72→
    73→    # Mechanical findings: use detector -> dimension mapping
    74→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
    75→    if detector:
    76→        dimension = get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
    77→        if dimension:
    78→            entry = dim_impact.get(dimension.name.lower())
    79→            if entry:
    80→                return entry["per_point"] * entry["headroom"]
    81→
    82→    return 0.0
    83→
    84→
    85→def subjective_score_value(item: dict) -> float:
    86→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
    87→        detail = detail_dict(item)
    88→        return float(detail.get("strict_score", item.get("subjective_score", 100.0)))
    89→    return float(item.get("subjective_score", 100.0))
    90→
    91→
    92→def build_finding_items(
    93→    state: dict,
    94→    *,
    95→    scan_path: str | None,
    96→    status_filter: str,
    97→    scope: str | None,
    98→    chronic: bool,
    99→) -> list[dict]:
   100→    scoped = path_scoped_findings(state.get("findings", {}), scan_path)
   101→    subjective_scores = subjective_strict_scores(state)
   102→    out: list[dict] = []
   103→
   104→    for finding_id, finding in scoped.items():
   105→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
   106→            continue
   107→        if not status_matches(finding.get("status", "open"), status_filter):
   108→            continue
   109→        if chronic and not (
   110→            finding.get("status") == "open" and finding.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2
   111→        ):
   112→            continue
   113→
   114→        # Evidence-only: skip findings below standalone confidence threshold
   115→        detector = finding.get("detector", "")
   116→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   117→        if meta and meta.standalone_threshold:
   118→            threshold_rank = CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(meta.standalone_threshold, 9)
   119→            finding_rank = CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(finding.get("confidence", "low"), 9)
   120→            if finding_rank > threshold_rank:
   121→                continue
   122→
   123→        item = dict(finding)
   124→        item["id"] = finding_id
   125→        item["kind"] = "finding"
   126→        item["is_review"] = is_review_finding(item)
   127→        item["is_subjective"] = is_subjective_finding(item)
   128→        item["review_weight"] = (
   129→            review_finding_weight(item) if item["is_review"] else None
   130→        )
   131→        subjective_score = None
   132→        if item["is_subjective"]:
   133→            detail = detail_dict(finding)
   134→            dim_name = detail.get("dimension_name", "")
   135→            dim_key = detail.get("dimension", "") or slugify(dim_name)
   136→            subjective_score = subjective_scores.get(
   137→                dim_key, subjective_scores.get(dim_name.lower(), 100.0)
   138→            )
   139→        item["subjective_score"] = subjective_score
   140→        supported_fixers = supported_fixers_for_item(state, item)
   141→        item["primary_command"] = primary_command_for_finding(
   142→            item,
   143→            supported_fixers=supported_fixers,
   144→        )
   145→
   146→        if not scope_matches(item, scope):
   147→            continue
   148→        out.append(item)
   149→
   150→    return out
   151→
   152→
   153→def item_sort_key(item: dict) -> tuple:
   154→    kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   155→
   156→    # Initial-review subjective items: highest priority (tier -3)
   157→    if kind == "subjective_dimension" and item.get("initial_review"):
   158→        return (-3, 0, subjective_score_value(item), item.get("id", ""))
   159→
   160→    # Triage stage items: tier -2, stage order, blocked after unblocked
   161→    if kind == "workflow_stage":
   162→        blocked_penalty = 1 if item.get("is_blocked") else 0
   163→        stage_index = int(item.get("stage_index", 0))
   164→        return (-2, blocked_penalty, stage_index, item.get("id", ""))
   165→
   166→    # Workflow action items (e.g. create-plan): tier -1
   167→    if kind == "workflow_action":
   168→        return (-1, 0, 0, item.get("id", ""))
   169→
   170→    if kind == "cluster":
   171→        # Clusters sort before individual findings, ordered by action type
   172→        action_pri = ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY.get(
   173→            item.get("action_type", "manual_fix"), 3
   174→        )
   175→        return (
   176→            0,
   177→            action_pri,
   178→            -int(item.get("member_count", 0)),
   179→            item.get("id", ""),
   180→        )
   181→
   182→    impact = item.get("estimated_impact", 0.0)
   183→
   184→    if kind == "subjective_dimension" or item.get("is_subjective"):
   185→        return (
   186→            1,
   187→            -impact,
   188→            subjective_score_value(item),
   189→            item.get("id", ""),
   190→        )
   191→
   192→    detail = detail_dict(item)
   193→    review_weight = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
   194→    return (
   195→        1,
   196→        -impact,
   197→        CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(item.get("confidence", "low"), 9),
   198→        -review_weight,
   199→        -int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   200→        item.get("id", ""),
   201→    )
   202→
   203→
   204→def item_explain(item: dict) -> dict:

   213→            "ranking_factors": ["blocked_penalty asc", "stage_index asc"],
   214→        }
   215→
   216→    if kind == "workflow_action":
   217→        return {

   220→            "ranking_factors": ["id asc"],
   221→        }
   222→
   223→    if kind == "cluster":
   224→        return {
   225→            "kind": "cluster",
   226→            "estimated_impact": item.get("estimated_impact", 0.0),
   227→            "action_type": item.get("action_type", "manual_fix"),
   228→            "member_count": item.get("member_count", 0),
   229→            "policy": "Clusters sort before individual findings, ordered by action type then size.",
   230→            "ranking_factors": ["action_type asc", "member_count desc", "id asc"],
   231→        }
   232→
   233→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
   234→        initial = item.get("initial_review", False)
   235→        return {
   236→            "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   237→            "estimated_impact": item.get("estimated_impact", 0.0),
   238→            "subjective_score": subjective_score_value(item),
   239→            "initial_review": initial,
   240→            "policy": (
   241→                "Initial review items sort first (onboarding priority)."
   242→                if initial else
   243→                "Sorted by dimension impact (score headroom × weight), then subjective score."
   244→            ),
   245→            "ranking_factors": ["estimated_impact desc", "subjective_score asc", "id asc"],
   246→        }
   247→
   248→    detail = detail_dict(item)
   249→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "low")
   250→    is_subjective = bool(item.get("is_subjective"))
   251→    is_review = bool(item.get("is_review"))
   252→    ranking_factors: list[str]

   255→    elif is_review:
   256→        ranking_factors = [

   258→            "confidence asc",
   259→            "review_weight desc",
   260→            "count desc",
   261→            "id asc",
   262→        ]
   263→    else:
   264→        ranking_factors = ["estimated_impact desc", "confidence asc", "count desc", "id asc"]
   265→    explain = {

   268→        "confidence": confidence,

   270→        "count": int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   271→        "id": item.get("id", ""),
   272→        "ranking_factors": ranking_factors,

   275→        explain["review_weight"] = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
   276→    if is_subjective:
   277→        explain["policy"] = (

   279→        )

   284→def group_queue_items(items: list[dict], group: str) -> dict[str, list[dict]]:
   285→    """Group queue items for alternate output modes."""
   286→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   287→    for item in items:
   288→        if group == "file":
   289→            key = item.get("file", "")
   290→        elif group == "detector":
   291→            key = item.get("detector", "")
   292→        elif group == "cluster":
   293→            plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   294→            key = plan_cluster["name"] if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) else "(unclustered)"
   295→        else:
   296→            key = "items"
   297→        grouped.setdefault(key, []).append(item)
   298→    return grouped
   299→
   300→

   303→    "enrich_with_impact",
   304→    "item_explain",
   305→    "item_sort_key",
   306→    "subjective_score_value",
   307→    "group_queue_items",

   255→def show_subjective_followup(
   256→    state: dict, target_strict_score: float, *, objective_backlog: int = 0,
   257→) -> None:
   258→    """Show subjective follow-up guidance for the current state."""
   259→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   260→    if not dim_scores:
   261→        return
   262→
   263→    subjective = reporting_dimensions_mod.scorecard_subjective_entries(
   264→        state,
   265→        dim_scores=dim_scores,
   266→    )
   267→    if not subjective:
   268→        return
   269→
   270→    followup = reporting_dimensions_mod.build_subjective_followup(
   271→        state,
   272→        subjective,
   273→        threshold=target_strict_score,
   274→        max_quality_items=3,
   275→        max_integrity_items=5,
   276→    )
   277→    if print_subjective_followup(followup, objective_backlog=objective_backlog):
   278→        print()
   279→

1→"""Plan-aware queue progress and frozen score display helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→
     7→from desloppify.core.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
     8→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     9→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import is_subjective_queue_item
    10→
    11→
    12→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    13→# QueueBreakdown — single source of truth for queue numbers
    14→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    15→
    16→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    17→class QueueBreakdown:
    18→    """All numbers needed to render the standardized queue display."""
    19→
    20→    queue_total: int = 0
    21→    plan_ordered: int = 0
    22→    skipped: int = 0
    23→    subjective: int = 0
    24→    workflow: int = 0
    25→    focus_cluster: str | None = None
    26→    focus_cluster_count: int = 0
    27→    focus_cluster_total: int = 0
    28→
    29→
    30→def plan_aware_queue_breakdown(
    31→    state: dict,
    32→    plan: dict | None = None,
    33→) -> QueueBreakdown:
    34→    """Build a full :class:`QueueBreakdown` from a single ``build_work_queue`` call."""
    35→    from desloppify.engine.work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
    36→
    37→    result = build_work_queue(
    38→        state,
    39→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
    40→            status="open",
    41→            count=None,
    42→            plan=plan,
    43→            collapse_clusters=True,
    44→            include_skipped=False,
    45→        ),
    46→    )
    47→
    48→    queue_total = result["total"]
    49→
    50→    # Count subjective and workflow items in the queue.
    51→    # Collapsed clusters whose members are all subjective count as subjective.
    52→    items = result.get("items", [])
    53→    subjective = sum(
    54→        1 for item in items
    55→        if is_subjective_queue_item(item)
    56→    )
    57→    workflow = sum(
    58→        1 for item in items
    59→        if item.get("kind") in ("workflow_stage", "workflow_action")
    60→    )
    61→
    62→    # Plan-derived counts
    63→    plan_ordered = 0
    64→    skipped = 0
    65→    if plan:
    66→        skipped = len(plan.get("skipped", {}))
    67→        # plan_ordered = items that are in queue_order minus skipped
    68→        queue_order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    69→        skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    70→        plan_ordered = sum(1 for fid in queue_order if fid not in skipped_ids)
    71→
    72→    # Focus cluster info
    73→    focus_cluster = None
    74→    focus_cluster_count = 0
    75→    focus_cluster_total = 0
    76→    if plan:
    77→        active = plan.get("active_cluster")
    78→        if active:
    79→            focus_cluster = active
    80→            cluster_data = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(active, {})
    81→            focus_cluster_total = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
    82→            # Count how many cluster members are still in the queue
    83→            cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
    84→            open_findings = {
    85→                fid
    86→                for fid, f in state.get("findings", {}).items()
    87→                if f.get("status") == "open"
    88→            }
    89→            focus_cluster_count = len(cluster_member_ids & open_findings)
    90→
    91→    return QueueBreakdown(
    92→        queue_total=queue_total,
    93→        plan_ordered=plan_ordered,
    94→        skipped=skipped,
    95→        subjective=subjective,
    96→        workflow=workflow,
    97→        focus_cluster=focus_cluster,
    98→        focus_cluster_count=focus_cluster_count,
    99→        focus_cluster_total=focus_cluster_total,
   100→    )
   101→
   102→
   103→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   104→# Formatting helpers — single source of truth for queue display
   105→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   106→
   107→def format_queue_headline(breakdown: QueueBreakdown) -> str:
   108→    """The one-line Queue summary. Same format everywhere.
   109→
   110→    Examples::
   111→
   112→        Queue: 1934 items (292 planned · 23 skipped)
   113→        Queue: 1934 items
   114→    """
   115→    n = breakdown.queue_total
   116→    label = f"Queue: {n} item{'s' if n != 1 else ''}"
   117→
   118→    # Parenthesized segments
   119→    segments: list[str] = []
   120→    if breakdown.workflow > 0:
   121→        segments.append(f"{breakdown.workflow} planning step{'s' if breakdown.workflow != 1 else ''}")
   122→    if breakdown.plan_ordered > 0:
   123→        segments.append(f"{breakdown.plan_ordered} planned")
   124→    if breakdown.skipped > 0:
   125→        segments.append(f"{breakdown.skipped} skipped")
   126→    if breakdown.subjective > 0:
   127→        segments.append(f"{breakdown.subjective} subjective")
   128→    if segments:
   129→        sep = " \u00b7 "
   130→        detail = sep.join(segments)
   131→        return f"{label} ({detail})"
   132→    return label
   133→
   134→
   135→def format_queue_block(
   136→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown,
   137→    *,
   138→    frozen_score: float | None = None,
   139→) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
   140→    """Full queue block: focus banner + queue line + contextual hints.
   141→
   142→    Returns a list of ``(text, style)`` pairs ready for ``colorize()``.
   143→    """
   144→    lines: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
   145→
   146→    # Focus banner (prominent, separate)
   147→    if breakdown.focus_cluster:
   148→        focus_line = (
   149→            f"  Focus: `{breakdown.focus_cluster}` "
   150→            f"\u2014 {breakdown.focus_cluster_count}/{breakdown.focus_cluster_total}"
   151→            f" items remaining"
   152→        )
   153→        lines.append((focus_line, "cyan"))
   154→
   155→    # Frozen score line
   156→    if frozen_score is not None:
   157→        lines.append((
   158→            f"  Score (frozen at plan start): strict {frozen_score:.1f}/100",
   159→            "cyan",
   160→        ))
   161→
   162→    # Queue headline — always the same
   163→    lines.append((f"  {format_queue_headline(breakdown)}", "bold"))
   164→
   165→    # Contextual hints (dim)
   166→    if breakdown.focus_cluster:
   167→        lines.append((
   168→            f"  Unfocus: `desloppify plan focus --clear`"
   169→            f" \u00b7 Cluster details: `desloppify next --cluster {breakdown.focus_cluster} --count 10`",
   170→            "dim",
   171→        ))
   172→    elif breakdown.plan_ordered > 0 or breakdown.skipped > 0:
   173→        lines.append((
   174→            "  Details: `desloppify plan queue`"
   175→            " \u00b7 Skip: `desloppify plan skip <id>`",
   176→            "dim",
   177→        ))
   178→    else:
   179→        lines.append((
   180→            "  Start planning: `desloppify plan`",
   181→            "dim",
   182→        ))
   183→
   184→    return lines
   185→
   186→
   187→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   188→# Legacy / backward-compatible helpers
   189→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   190→
   191→def plan_aware_queue_count(state: dict, plan: dict | None = None) -> int:
   192→    """Count remaining plan-aware queue items (skips excluded, clusters collapsed)."""
   193→    from desloppify.engine.work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
   194→
   195→    result = build_work_queue(
   196→        state,
   197→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   198→            status="open",
   199→            count=None,
   200→            plan=plan,
   201→            collapse_clusters=True,
   202→            include_skipped=False,
   203→        ),
   204→    )
   205→    return result["total"]
   206→
   207→
   208→def get_plan_start_strict(plan: dict | None) -> float | None:
   209→    """Extract the frozen plan-start strict score, or None if unset."""
   210→    if not plan:
   211→        return None
   212→    return plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict")
   213→
   214→
   215→def print_frozen_score_with_queue_context(
   216→    plan: dict,
   217→    queue_remaining: int,
   218→    *,
   219→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown | None = None,
   220→) -> None:
   221→    """Show frozen plan-start score + queue progress.
   222→
   223→    When *breakdown* is provided, uses the standardized queue block.
   224→    Otherwise falls back to the legacy two-line format.
   225→    """
   226→    scores = plan.get("plan_start_scores", {})
   227→    strict = scores.get("strict")
   228→    if strict is None:
   229→        return
   230→
   231→    if breakdown is not None:
   232→        block = format_queue_block(breakdown, frozen_score=strict)
   233→        print()
   234→        for text, style in block:
   235→            print(colorize(text, style))
   236→        if queue_remaining > 0:
   237→            print(colorize(
   238→                "  Score will not update until the queue is clear and you run `desloppify scan`.",
   239→                "dim",
   240→            ))
   241→        return
   242→
   243→    # Legacy fallback
   244→    print(
   245→        colorize(
   246→            f"\n  Score (frozen at plan start): strict {strict:.1f}/100",
   247→            "cyan",
   248→        )
   249→    )
   250→    print(
   251→        colorize(
   252→            f"  Queue: {queue_remaining} item{'s' if queue_remaining != 1 else ''}"
   253→            " remaining. Score will not update until the queue is clear and you run `desloppify scan`.",
   254→            "dim",
   255→        )
   256→    )
   257→
   258→
   259→def _print_objective_drained_banner(
   260→    frozen_strict: float,
   261→    remaining: int,
   262→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown,
   263→) -> None:
   264→    """Show a phase-transition banner when objective work is drained."""
   265→    kind_labels: list[str] = []
   266→    if breakdown.subjective > 0:
   267→        kind_labels.append("subjective")
   268→    if breakdown.workflow > 0:
   269→        kind_labels.append("workflow")
   270→    kind_desc = " + ".join(kind_labels) if kind_labels else "non-objective"
   271→    print(colorize(
   272→        f"\n  Objective queue complete (plan-start was {frozen_strict:.1f})."
   273→        f" {remaining} {kind_desc} item{'s' if remaining != 1 else ''} remain.",
   274→        "cyan",
   275→    ))
   276→    print(colorize(
   277→        "  Run `desloppify next` for remaining work,"
   278→        " then `desloppify scan` to finalize.",
   279→        "dim",
   280→    ))
   281→
   282→
   283→def print_execution_or_reveal(
   284→    state: dict,
   285→    prev,
   286→    plan: dict | None,
   287→) -> None:
   288→    """Context-aware score display: frozen plan-start score or live scores.
   289→
   290→    Three display modes:
   291→
   292→    1. **Frozen** — objective queue items remain → show frozen plan-start score.
   293→    2. **Phase transition** — objective drained, subjective/workflow remains
   294→       → show live scores + transition banner.
   295→    3. **Live** — no active plan cycle or queue is clear → show live scores.
   296→    """
   297→    frozen_strict: float | None = None
   298→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown | None = None
   299→
   300→    if plan and plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict") is not None:
   301→        frozen_strict = plan["plan_start_scores"]["strict"]
   302→        try:
   303→            breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)
   304→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   305→            pass
   306→
   307→    remaining = breakdown.queue_total if breakdown else 0
   308→
   309→    # Frozen: objective work remains — show frozen plan-start score and return
   310→    if remaining > 0 and frozen_strict is not None:
   311→        objective_remaining = remaining - breakdown.subjective - breakdown.workflow
   312→        if objective_remaining > 0:
   313→            print_frozen_score_with_queue_context(
   314→                plan, remaining, breakdown=breakdown,
   315→            )
   316→            return
   317→
   318→    # Live scores (or phase transition): show current scores
   319→    from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score_update import print_score_update
   320→
   321→    print_score_update(state, prev)
   322→
   323→    # Phase transition: objective drained but subjective/workflow remains
   324→    if remaining > 0 and frozen_strict is not None:
   325→        _print_objective_drained_banner(frozen_strict, remaining, breakdown)
   326→
   327→
   328→def show_score_with_plan_context(state: dict, prev) -> None:
   329→    """Load plan (best-effort) and show frozen or live score context.
   330→
   331→    Encapsulates the common load_plan + PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS + reveal
   332→    choreography so command modules don't each repeat it.
   333→    """
   334→    from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
   335→
   336→    try:
   337→        plan = load_plan()
   338→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   339→        plan = None
   340→    print_execution_or_reveal(state, prev, plan)
   341→
   342→
   343→__all__ = [
   344→    "QueueBreakdown",
   345→    "format_queue_block",
   346→    "format_queue_headline",
   347→    "get_plan_start_strict",
   348→    "plan_aware_queue_breakdown",
   349→    "plan_aware_queue_count",
   350→    "print_execution_or_reveal",
   351→    "print_frozen_score_with_queue_context",
   352→    "show_score_with_plan_context",
   353→]
   354→

    33→
    34→def _display_fallback(dim_name: str) -> str:
    35→    words = dim_name.replace("_", " ")
    36→    return words[0].upper() + words[1:] if words else words
    37→
    38→
    39→def _normalize_dimension_key(dim_name: object) -> str:
    40→    if not isinstance(dim_name, str):
    41→        return ""
    42→    return "_".join(dim_name.strip().lower().replace("-", "_").split())
    43→
    44→
    45→def _primary_lang_from_findings(findings: dict) -> str | None:
    46→    counts: dict[str, int] = {}
    47→    for finding in findings.values():
    48→        if not isinstance(finding, dict):
    49→            continue
    50→        raw_lang = finding.get("lang")
    51→        if not isinstance(raw_lang, str) or not raw_lang.strip():
    52→            continue
    53→        key = raw_lang.strip().lower()
    54→        counts[key] = counts.get(key, 0) + 1
    55→    if not counts:
    56→        return None
    57→    return max(counts, key=counts.get)
    58→
    59→

    37→
    38→def cmd_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    39→    """Show all findings for a file, directory, detector, or pattern."""
    40→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    41→    state = runtime.state
    42→    config = runtime.config
    43→
    44→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    45→        return
    46→
    47→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
    48→    if skill_warning:
    49→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
    50→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(config)
    51→    if config_warning:
    52→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
    53→
    54→    print_triage_guardrail_info(state=state)
    55→
    56→    pattern_raw = getattr(args, "pattern", "")
    57→    show_code = getattr(args, "code", False)
    58→    chronic = getattr(args, "chronic", False)
    59→    ok, pattern, status_filter, scope = resolve_show_scope(args)
    60→    if not ok or pattern is None:
    61→        return
    62→
    63→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
    64→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
    65→    narrative = compute_narrative(
    66→        state,
    67→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="show"),
    68→    )
    69→
    70→    entity = resolve_entity(pattern, state)
    71→
    72→    if entity.kind == "special_view" and entity.pattern.strip().lower() == "concerns":
    73→        _show_concerns(state, lang_name)
    74→        return
    75→
    76→    if entity.kind == "dimension" and entity.is_subjective:
    77→        _render_subjective_dimension(state, config, entity, pattern_raw)
    78→        return
    79→
    80→    if entity.kind == "dimension" and not entity.is_subjective:
    81→        matches = _load_dimension_findings(state, entity, status_filter)
    82→        if not matches:
    83→            _render_clean_mechanical_dimension(state, entity)
    84→            return
    85→        pattern = entity.display_name
    86→    else:
    87→        matches = load_matches(
    88→            state, scope=scope, status_filter=status_filter, chronic=chronic
    89→        )
    90→
    91→    if not matches:
    92→        _render_no_matches(entity, pattern, status_filter, narrative, state, config)
    93→        return
    94→
    95→    no_budget = getattr(args, "no_budget", False)
    96→    (
    97→        surfaced_matches,
    98→        hidden_by_detector,
    99→        noise_budget,
   100→        global_noise_budget,
   101→        budget_warning,
   102→    ) = resolve_noise(
   103→        config,
   104→        matches,
   105→        no_budget=no_budget,
   106→    )
   107→    hidden_total = sum(hidden_by_detector.values())
   108→
   109→    payload = build_show_payload(
   110→        surfaced_matches,
   111→        pattern,
   112→        status_filter,
   113→        ShowPayloadMeta(
   114→            total_matches=len(matches),
   115→            hidden_by_detector=hidden_by_detector,
   116→            noise_budget=noise_budget,
   117→            global_noise_budget=global_noise_budget,
   118→        ),
   119→    )
   120→    write_query({"command": "show", **payload, "narrative": narrative})
   121→
   122→    output_file = getattr(args, "output", None)
   123→    if output_file:
   124→        if write_show_output_file(output_file, payload, len(surfaced_matches)):
   125→            return
   126→        raise SystemExit(1)
   127→
   128→    top = getattr(args, "top", 20) or 20
   129→    render_findings(
   130→        surfaced_matches,
   131→        pattern=pattern,
   132→        status_filter=status_filter,
   133→        show_code=show_code,
   134→        top=top,
   135→        hidden_by_detector=hidden_by_detector,
   136→        hidden_total=hidden_total,
   137→        noise_budget=noise_budget,
   138→        global_noise_budget=global_noise_budget,
   139→        budget_warning=budget_warning,
   140→    )
   141→    try:
   142→        plan = load_plan()
   143→        plan_active = plan if (plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("clusters")) else None
   144→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   145→        plan_active = None
   146→    show_agent_plan(narrative, surfaced_matches, plan=plan_active)
   147→    show_subjective_followup(
   148→        state,
   149→        target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0),
   150→    )
   151→
   152→    _render_subjective_views_guide(entity)
   153→
   154→
   155→__all__ = ["cmd_show"]

1→"""Tests for stale and unscored subjective dimension sync in the plan."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._plan.reconcile import reconcile_plan_after_scan
     6→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import empty_plan
     7→from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (
     8→    sync_stale_dimensions,
     9→    sync_unscored_dimensions,
    10→)
    11→
    12→
    13→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    14→# Helpers
    15→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    16→
    17→def _plan_with_queue(*ids: str) -> dict:
    18→    plan = empty_plan()
    19→    plan["queue_order"] = list(ids)
    20→    return plan
    21→
    22→
    23→def _state_with_stale_dimensions(*dim_keys: str, score: float = 50.0) -> dict:
    24→    """Build a minimal state with stale subjective dimensions."""
    25→    dim_scores: dict = {}
    26→    assessments: dict = {}
    27→    for dim_key in dim_keys:
    28→        dim_scores[dim_key] = {
    29→            "score": score,
    30→            "strict": score,
    31→            "checks": 1,
    32→            "issues": 0,
    33→            "detectors": {
    34→                "subjective_assessment": {
    35→                    "dimension_key": dim_key,
    36→                    "placeholder": False,
    37→                }
    38→            },
    39→        }
    40→        assessments[dim_key] = {
    41→            "score": score,
    42→            "needs_review_refresh": True,
    43→            "refresh_reason": "mechanical_findings_changed",
    44→            "stale_since": "2025-01-01T00:00:00+00:00",
    45→        }
    46→    return {
    47→        "findings": {},
    48→        "scan_count": 5,
    49→        "dimension_scores": dim_scores,
    50→        "subjective_assessments": assessments,
    51→    }
    52→
    53→
    54→def _state_with_unscored_dimensions(*dim_keys: str) -> dict:
    55→    """Build a minimal state with unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions."""
    56→    dim_scores: dict = {}
    57→    assessments: dict = {}
    58→    for dim_key in dim_keys:
    59→        dim_scores[dim_key] = {
    60→            "score": 0,
    61→            "strict": 0,
    62→            "checks": 1,
    63→            "issues": 0,
    64→            "detectors": {
    65→                "subjective_assessment": {
    66→                    "dimension_key": dim_key,
    67→                    "placeholder": True,
    68→                }
    69→            },
    70→        }
    71→        assessments[dim_key] = {
    72→            "score": 0.0,
    73→            "source": "scan_reset_subjective",
    74→            "placeholder": True,
    75→        }
    76→    return {
    77→        "findings": {},
    78→        "scan_count": 1,
    79→        "dimension_scores": dim_scores,
    80→        "subjective_assessments": assessments,
    81→    }
    82→
    83→
    84→def _state_with_mixed_dimensions(
    85→    unscored: list[str],
    86→    stale: list[str],
    87→) -> dict:
    88→    """Build a state with both unscored and stale dimensions."""
    89→    state = _state_with_unscored_dimensions(*unscored)
    90→    stale_state = _state_with_stale_dimensions(*stale)
    91→    state["dimension_scores"].update(stale_state["dimension_scores"])
    92→    state["subjective_assessments"].update(stale_state["subjective_assessments"])
    93→    return state
    94→
    95→
    96→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    97→# Unscored dimension sync
    98→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    99→
   100→def test_unscored_injected_at_front():
   101→    """Unscored IDs are prepended before existing items."""
   102→    plan = _plan_with_queue("some_finding::file.py::abc123")
   103→    state = _state_with_unscored_dimensions("design_coherence", "error_consistency")
   104→
   105→    result = sync_unscored_dimensions(plan, state)
   106→    assert len(result.injected) == 2
   107→    # Unscored dims should be at the front, before the real finding
   108→    assert plan["queue_order"][-1] == "some_finding::file.py::abc123"
   109→    assert plan["queue_order"][0].startswith("subjective::")
   110→    assert plan["queue_order"][1].startswith("subjective::")
   111→
   112→
   113→def test_unscored_injection_unconditional():
   114→    """Unscored dims inject even when objective items exist in queue."""
   115→    plan = _plan_with_queue(
   116→        "some_finding::file.py::abc123",
   117→        "another::file.py::def456",
   118→    )
   119→    state = _state_with_unscored_dimensions("design_coherence")
   120→
   121→    result = sync_unscored_dimensions(plan, state)
   122→    assert len(result.injected) == 1
   123→    assert plan["queue_order"][0] == "subjective::design_coherence"
   124→    assert len(plan["queue_order"]) == 3  # 1 unscored + 2 existing
   125→
   126→
   127→def test_unscored_pruned_after_review():
   128→    """Once a dimension is scored, it is removed from the queue."""
   129→    plan = _plan_with_queue(
   130→        "subjective::design_coherence",
   131→        "subjective::error_consistency",
   132→        "some_finding::file.py::abc123",
   133→    )
   134→    # Only error_consistency is still unscored; design_coherence was scored
   135→    state = _state_with_unscored_dimensions("error_consistency")
   136→
   137→    result = sync_unscored_dimensions(plan, state)
   138→    assert "subjective::design_coherence" in result.pruned
   139→    assert "subjective::error_consistency" in plan["queue_order"]
   140→    assert "subjective::design_coherence" not in plan["queue_order"]
   141→
   142→
   143→def test_stale_sync_does_not_prune_unscored_ids():
   144→    """Stale sync must not remove IDs that are still unscored."""
   145→    plan = _plan_with_queue(
   146→        "subjective::design_coherence",  # unscored
   147→        "subjective::error_consistency",  # stale
   148→    )
   149→    state = _state_with_mixed_dimensions(
   150→        unscored=["design_coherence"],
   151→        stale=["error_consistency"],
   152→    )
   153→
   154→    result = sync_stale_dimensions(plan, state)
   155→    # design_coherence is unscored (not stale), but stale sync should NOT prune it
   156→    assert "subjective::design_coherence" not in result.pruned
   157→    assert "subjective::design_coherence" in plan["queue_order"]
   158→    assert "subjective::error_consistency" in plan["queue_order"]
   159→
   160→
   161→def test_unscored_sync_does_not_prune_stale_ids():
   162→    """Unscored sync must not remove IDs that are stale."""
   163→    plan = _plan_with_queue(
   164→        "subjective::design_coherence",  # unscored
   165→        "subjective::error_consistency",  # stale
   166→    )
   167→    state = _state_with_mixed_dimensions(
   168→        unscored=["design_coherence"],
   169→        stale=["error_consistency"],
   170→    )
   171→
   172→    result = sync_unscored_dimensions(plan, state)
   173→    assert "subjective::error_consistency" not in result.pruned
   174→    assert "subjective::error_consistency" in plan["queue_order"]
   175→
   176→
   177→def test_unscored_no_injection_when_no_dimension_scores():
   178→    plan = _plan_with_queue()
   179→    state = {"findings": {}, "scan_count": 1}
   180→
   181→    result = sync_unscored_dimensions(plan, state)
   182→    assert result.injected == []
   183→    assert result.pruned == []
   184→
   185→
   186→def test_unscored_no_duplicates():
   187→    """Already-present unscored IDs are not duplicated."""
   188→    plan = _plan_with_queue("subjective::design_coherence")

   196→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   197→# Injection: empty queue + stale dimensions
   198→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   199→
   200→def test_injects_when_queue_empty():
   201→    plan = _plan_with_queue()

   245→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   246→# Cleanup: prune resolved stale IDs
   247→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   248→
   249→def test_prunes_resolved_dimension_ids():
   250→    plan = _plan_with_queue(
   251→        "subjective::design_coherence",
   252→        "subjective::error_consistency",
   253→    )

   262→def test_prune_does_not_touch_real_finding_ids():

   264→        "structural::file.py::abc123",

   275→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   276→# Full lifecycle: inject → refresh → prune → re-inject
   277→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   278→
   279→def test_full_lifecycle():
   280→    plan = _plan_with_queue()
   281→    state = _state_with_stale_dimensions("design_coherence", "error_consistency")
   282→
   283→    # 1. Empty queue, stale dims → inject both
   284→    r1 = sync_stale_dimensions(plan, state)
   285→    assert len(r1.injected) == 2
   286→    assert plan["queue_order"] == [
   287→        "subjective::design_coherence",
   288→        "subjective::error_consistency",
   289→    ]
   290→
   291→    # 2. User refreshes design_coherence (no longer stale)
   292→    state["subjective_assessments"]["design_coherence"]["needs_review_refresh"] = False
   293→
   294→    r2 = sync_stale_dimensions(plan, state)
   295→    assert r2.pruned == ["subjective::design_coherence"]
   296→    # error_consistency still there — queue not empty, so no new injection
   297→    assert plan["queue_order"] == ["subjective::error_consistency"]
   298→    assert r2.injected == []
   299→
   300→    # 3. User refreshes error_consistency too → queue empties, nothing stale
   301→    state["subjective_assessments"]["error_consistency"]["needs_review_refresh"] = False
   302→
   303→    r3 = sync_stale_dimensions(plan, state)
   304→    assert r3.pruned == ["subjective::error_consistency"]
   305→    assert plan["queue_order"] == []
   306→    assert r3.injected == []
   307→
   308→    # 4. New mechanical change makes design_coherence stale again
   309→    state["subjective_assessments"]["design_coherence"]["needs_review_refresh"] = True
   310→
   311→    r4 = sync_stale_dimensions(plan, state)
   312→    assert r4.injected == ["subjective::design_coherence"]
   313→    assert plan["queue_order"] == ["subjective::design_coherence"]
   314→

   317→# Reconcile must not supersede synthetic IDs

   320→def test_reconcile_ignores_synthetic_ids():

   350→# Auto-clustering of stale subjective dimensions
   351→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   352→
   353→def test_stale_cluster_created():
   354→    """When >=2 stale dims exist, auto_cluster_findings creates a cluster."""
   355→    from desloppify.engine._plan.auto_cluster import auto_cluster_findings
   356→
   357→    plan = _plan_with_queue(
   358→        "subjective::design_coherence",
   359→        "subjective::error_consistency",
   360→    )
   361→    state = _state_with_stale_dimensions("design_coherence", "error_consistency")
   362→
   363→    changes = auto_cluster_findings(plan, state)
   364→    assert changes >= 1
   365→    assert "auto/stale-review" in plan["clusters"]
   366→
   367→    cluster = plan["clusters"]["auto/stale-review"]
   368→    assert cluster["auto"] is True
   369→    assert cluster["cluster_key"] == "subjective::stale"
   370→    assert set(cluster["finding_ids"]) == {
   371→        "subjective::design_coherence",
   372→        "subjective::error_consistency",
   373→    }
   374→    assert "Re-review 2 stale" in cluster["description"]
   375→    assert "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions" in cluster["action"]
   376→    assert "design_coherence" in cluster["action"]
   377→    assert "error_consistency" in cluster["action"]
   378→    assert "--force-review-rerun" not in cluster["action"]
   379→
   380→
   381→def test_stale_cluster_deleted_when_fresh():
   382→    """When all dims are refreshed, the stale cluster is deleted."""
   383→    from desloppify.engine._plan.auto_cluster import auto_cluster_findings
   384→
   385→    plan = _plan_with_queue(
   386→        "subjective::design_coherence",

   396→    plan["queue_order"] = []
   397→
   398→    auto_cluster_findings(plan, state)
   399→    assert "auto/stale-review" not in plan["clusters"]
   400→

   402→def test_stale_cluster_updated():
   403→    """When the stale set changes, the cluster membership updates."""
   404→    from desloppify.engine._plan.auto_cluster import auto_cluster_findings

   406→    plan = _plan_with_queue(

   440→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   441→# Promoted items: system insertions go after user-moved items
   442→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   443→
   444→def test_unscored_respects_promoted_items():
   445→    """User-moved item stays at the top when unscored dims are injected."""
   446→    plan = _plan_with_queue("finding_a", "finding_b")
   447→    plan["promoted_ids"] = ["finding_a"]
   448→    state = _state_with_unscored_dimensions("design_coherence")
   449→
   450→    result = sync_unscored_dimensions(plan, state)
   451→    assert len(result.injected) == 1
   452→    # finding_a should still be first (promoted), then the unscored dim
   453→    assert plan["queue_order"][0] == "finding_a"
   454→    assert plan["queue_order"][1] == "subjective::design_coherence"
   455→    assert plan["queue_order"][2] == "finding_b"
   456→
   457→
   458→def test_unscored_multiple_promoted_items():
   459→    """Multiple promoted items all stay ahead of injected unscored dims."""
   460→    plan = _plan_with_queue("finding_a", "finding_b", "finding_c")
   461→    plan["promoted_ids"] = ["finding_a", "finding_b"]
   462→    state = _state_with_unscored_dimensions("design_coherence", "error_consistency")
   463→

   467→    assert plan["queue_order"][0] == "finding_a"
   468→    assert plan["queue_order"][1] == "finding_b"
   469→    # Unscored dims injected after promoted items
   470→    assert all(
   471→        fid.startswith("subjective::")
   472→        for fid in plan["queue_order"][2:4]
   473→    )
   474→    assert plan["queue_order"][4] == "finding_c"

   490→def test_triage_respects_promoted_items():

   492→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (

   514→    assert all(sid in plan["queue_order"] for sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS)

   106→def triage_phase_banner(plan: PlanModel) -> str:
   107→    """Return a banner string when triage stage IDs are in the queue."""
   108→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   109→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   110→    has_triage = any(sid in order for sid in TRIAGE_IDS)
   111→    if not has_triage:
   112→        return ""
   113→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   114→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   115→    completed = [s for s in ("observe", "reflect", "organize") if s in stages]
   116→    if completed:
   117→        return (
   118→            f"TRIAGE MODE ({len(completed)}/4 stages complete) — "
   119→            "complete all stages to exit. Run: desloppify plan triage"
   120→        )
   121→    return (
   122→        "TRIAGE MODE — review findings need analysis before fixing. "
   123→        "Run: desloppify plan triage"
   124→    )
   125→

    79→for _dimension_key, _display_name in _LEGACY_DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
    80→    _weight = _LEGACY_SUBJECTIVE_WEIGHTS_BY_DISPLAY.get(
    81→        " ".join(_display_name.strip().lower().split())

    86→
    87→def _normalize_dimension_name(name: str) -> str:
    88→    return "_".join(str(name).strip().lower().replace("-", "_").split())
    89→
    90→
    91→def _title_display_name(dimension_key: str) -> str:
    92→    return dimension_key.replace("_", " ").title()
    93→
    94→
    95→def _normalize_lang_name(lang_name: str | None) -> str | None:
    96→    if not isinstance(lang_name, str):
    97→        return None
    98→    cleaned = lang_name.strip().lower()
    99→    return cleaned or None
   100→
   101→
   102→def _extract_prompt_meta(entry: object) -> dict[str, object]:
   103→    if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   104→        return {}
   105→    meta = entry.get("meta")
   106→    if not isinstance(meta, dict):
   107→        return {}
   108→    out: dict[str, object] = {}
   109→
   110→    if isinstance(meta.get("display_name"), str) and meta["display_name"].strip():
   111→        out["display_name"] = meta["display_name"].strip()
   112→
   113→    weight = meta.get("weight")
   114→    if is_numeric(weight):
   115→        out["weight"] = max(0.0, float(weight))
   116→
   117→    enabled = meta.get("enabled_by_default")
   118→    if isinstance(enabled, bool):
   119→        out["enabled_by_default"] = enabled
   120→
   121→    reset_on_scan = meta.get("reset_on_scan")
   122→    if isinstance(reset_on_scan, bool):
   123→        out["reset_on_scan"] = reset_on_scan
   124→
   125→    return out
   126→
   127→
   128→def _merge_prompt_display_and_weights(
   129→    payload: dict[str, object],
   130→    *,
   131→    prompt_meta: dict[str, object],
   132→    override_existing: bool,
   133→) -> None:
   134→    if "display_name" in prompt_meta and (
   135→        override_existing or "display_name" not in payload
   136→    ):
   137→        payload["display_name"] = prompt_meta["display_name"]
   138→    if "weight" in prompt_meta and (override_existing or "weight" not in payload):
   139→        payload["weight"] = prompt_meta["weight"]
   140→    if "reset_on_scan" in prompt_meta and (
   141→        override_existing or "reset_on_scan" not in payload
   142→    ):
   143→        payload["reset_on_scan"] = prompt_meta["reset_on_scan"]
   144→
   145→
   146→def _merge_enabled_by_default_flag(
   147→    payload: dict[str, object],
   148→    *,
   149→    prompt_meta: dict[str, object],
   150→    override_existing: bool,
   151→    default_enabled: bool,
   152→) -> None:
   153→    if default_enabled:
   154→        payload["enabled_by_default"] = True
   155→    if "enabled_by_default" not in prompt_meta:
   156→        return
   157→    prompt_enabled = bool(prompt_meta["enabled_by_default"])
   158→    if override_existing:
   159→        payload["enabled_by_default"] = prompt_enabled
   160→        return
   161→    payload["enabled_by_default"] = bool(
   162→        payload.get("enabled_by_default", False) or prompt_enabled
   163→    )
   164→
   165→
   166→def _normalized_default_dimensions(dimensions: list[str]) -> set[str]:
   167→    return {
   168→        _normalize_dimension_name(dim)
   169→        for dim in dimensions
   170→        if isinstance(dim, str) and dim.strip()
   171→    }
   172→
   173→
   174→def _merge_dimension_meta(
   175→    target: dict[str, dict[str, object]],
   176→    *,
   177→    dimensions: list[str],
   178→    prompts: dict[str, dict[str, object]],
   179→    override_existing: bool = False,
   180→) -> None:
   181→    defaults = _normalized_default_dimensions(dimensions)
   182→
   183→    for raw_dim, entry in prompts.items():
   184→        dim = _normalize_dimension_name(raw_dim)
   185→        if not dim:
   186→            continue
   187→
   188→        payload = target.setdefault(dim, {})
   189→        prompt_meta = _extract_prompt_meta(entry)
   190→        _merge_prompt_display_and_weights(
   191→            payload,
   192→            prompt_meta=prompt_meta,
   193→            override_existing=override_existing,
   194→        )
   195→        _merge_enabled_by_default_flag(
   196→            payload,
   197→            prompt_meta=prompt_meta,
   198→            override_existing=override_existing,
   199→            default_enabled=dim in defaults,
   200→        )
   201→
   202→
   203→def _default_available_languages() -> list[str]:
   204→    try:
   205→        return list(_available_langs())
   206→    except (ImportError, ValueError, TypeError, RuntimeError):
   207→        return []
   208→
   209→
   210→def _default_load_dimensions_payload() -> tuple[
   211→    list[str], dict[str, dict[str, object]], str
   212→]:
   213→    return _load_dimensions()
   214→
   215→
   216→def _default_load_dimensions_payload_for_lang(
   217→    lang_name: str,
   218→) -> tuple[list[str], dict[str, dict[str, object]], str]:
   219→    return _load_dimensions_for_lang(lang_name)
   220→
   221→
   222→class _SubjectiveProviderState:
   223→    def __init__(self) -> None:
   224→        self.available_languages_provider: Callable[[], list[str]] = (
   225→            _default_available_languages
   226→        )
   227→        self.load_dimensions_payload_provider: Callable[
   228→            [], tuple[list[str], dict[str, dict[str, object]], str]
   229→        ] = _default_load_dimensions_payload
   230→        self.load_dimensions_payload_for_lang_provider: Callable[
   231→            [str], tuple[list[str], dict[str, dict[str, object]], str]
   232→        ] = _default_load_dimensions_payload_for_lang
   233→
   234→
   235→_PROVIDER_STATE = _SubjectiveProviderState()
   236→
   237→
   238→def _available_languages() -> list[str]:
   239→    return _PROVIDER_STATE.available_languages_provider()
   240→
   241→
   242→def _load_dimensions_payload() -> tuple[list[str], dict[str, dict[str, object]], str]:
   243→    return _PROVIDER_STATE.load_dimensions_payload_provider()
   244→
   245→
   246→def _load_dimensions_payload_for_lang(
   247→    lang_name: str,
   248→) -> tuple[list[str], dict[str, dict[str, object]], str]:
   249→    return _PROVIDER_STATE.load_dimensions_payload_for_lang_provider(lang_name)
   250→
   251→
   252→def _clear_subjective_dimension_caches() -> None:
   253→    default_dimension_keys.cache_clear()
   254→    default_dimension_keys_for_lang.cache_clear()
   255→    load_subjective_dimension_metadata.cache_clear()
   256→    load_subjective_dimension_metadata_for_lang.cache_clear()
   257→
   258→
   259→def configure_subjective_dimension_providers(
   260→    *,
   261→    available_languages_provider: Callable[[], list[str]] | None = None,
   262→    load_dimensions_payload_provider: Callable[
   263→        [], tuple[list[str], dict[str, dict[str, object]], str]
   264→    ]
   265→    | None = None,
   266→    load_dimensions_payload_for_lang_provider: Callable[
   267→        [str], tuple[list[str], dict[str, dict[str, object]], str]
   268→    ]
   269→    | None = None,
   270→) -> None:
   271→    """Configure metadata providers for subjective-dimension lookups."""
   272→    state = _PROVIDER_STATE
   273→
   274→    changed = False
   275→    if (
   276→        available_languages_provider is not None
   277→        and available_languages_provider is not state.available_languages_provider
   278→    ):
   279→        state.available_languages_provider = available_languages_provider
   280→        changed = True
   281→    if (
   282→        load_dimensions_payload_provider is not None
   283→        and load_dimensions_payload_provider
   284→        is not state.load_dimensions_payload_provider
   285→    ):
   286→        state.load_dimensions_payload_provider = load_dimensions_payload_provider
   287→        changed = True
   288→    if (
   289→        load_dimensions_payload_for_lang_provider is not None
   290→        and load_dimensions_payload_for_lang_provider
   291→        is not state.load_dimensions_payload_for_lang_provider
   292→    ):
   293→        state.load_dimensions_payload_for_lang_provider = (
   294→            load_dimensions_payload_for_lang_provider
   295→        )
   296→        changed = True
   297→
   298→    if changed:
   299→        _clear_subjective_dimension_caches()
   300→
   301→
   302→def reset_subjective_dimension_providers() -> None:
   303→    """Reset metadata providers to built-in defaults."""
   304→    configure_subjective_dimension_providers(
   305→        available_languages_provider=_default_available_languages,
   306→        load_dimensions_payload_provider=_default_load_dimensions_payload,
   307→        load_dimensions_payload_for_lang_provider=_default_load_dimensions_payload_for_lang,
   308→    )
   309→
   310→
   311→def _normalize_dimension_list(values: list[str]) -> tuple[str, ...]:
   312→    normalized: list[str] = []
   313→    for raw in values:
   314→        if not isinstance(raw, str):
   315→            continue
   316→        dim = _normalize_dimension_name(raw)
   317→        if not dim or dim in normalized:
   318→            continue
   319→        normalized.append(dim)
   320→    return tuple(normalized)
   321→
   322→
   323→@lru_cache(maxsize=1)
   324→def default_dimension_keys() -> tuple[str, ...]:
   325→    """Return canonical default subjective dimension keys."""
   326→    try:
   327→        dims, _, _ = _load_dimensions_payload()
   328→    except (ImportError, ValueError, RuntimeError) as exc:
   329→        logger.debug("Failed to load default subjective dimensions: %s", exc)
   330→        return tuple(_LEGACY_DISPLAY_NAMES.keys())
   331→    return _normalize_dimension_list(dims)
   332→
   333→
   334→@lru_cache(maxsize=16)
   335→def default_dimension_keys_for_lang(lang_name: str | None) -> tuple[str, ...]:
   336→    """Return default subjective dimension keys for a specific language."""
   337→    normalized = _normalize_lang_name(lang_name)
   338→    if normalized is None:
   339→        return default_dimension_keys()
   340→    try:
   341→        dims, _, _ = _load_dimensions_payload_for_lang(normalized)
   342→    except (ImportError, ValueError, RuntimeError) as exc:
   343→        logger.debug(
   344→            "Failed to load subjective dimensions for lang %s: %s",
   345→            normalized,
   346→            exc,
   347→        )
   348→        return default_dimension_keys()
   349→    return _normalize_dimension_list(dims)
   350→
   351→
   352→def _build_subjective_dimension_metadata(
   353→    *,
   354→    lang_name: str | None,
   355→) -> dict[str, dict[str, object]]:
   356→    out: dict[str, dict[str, object]] = {}
   357→
   358→    try:
   359→        shared_defaults, shared_prompts, _ = _load_dimensions_payload()
   360→    except (ImportError, ValueError, RuntimeError) as exc:
   361→        logger.debug("Failed to load shared subjective dimension payload: %s", exc)
   362→        shared_defaults, shared_prompts = [], {}
   363→    _merge_dimension_meta(out, dimensions=shared_defaults, prompts=shared_prompts)
   364→
   365→    langs = (
   366→        [lang_name]
   367→        if isinstance(lang_name, str) and lang_name.strip()
   368→        else _available_languages()
   369→    )
   370→    for name in langs:
   371→        try:
   372→            lang_defaults, lang_prompts, _ = _load_dimensions_payload_for_lang(name)
   373→            _merge_dimension_meta(
   374→                out,
   375→                dimensions=lang_defaults,
   376→                prompts=lang_prompts,
   377→                override_existing=bool(lang_name),
   378→            )
   379→        except (ValueError, RuntimeError) as exc:
   380→            logger.debug("Failed to load dimensions for lang %s: %s", name, exc)
   381→            continue
   382→
   383→    for dim, payload in out.items():
   384→        payload.setdefault(
   385→            "display_name",
   386→            _LEGACY_DISPLAY_NAMES.get(dim, _title_display_name(dim)),
   387→        )
   388→        payload.setdefault("weight", _LEGACY_WEIGHT_BY_DIMENSION.get(dim, 1.0))
   389→        payload.setdefault("enabled_by_default", False)
   390→        if dim in _LEGACY_DISPLAY_NAMES:
   391→            payload.setdefault("reset_on_scan", dim in _LEGACY_RESET_ON_SCAN_DIMENSIONS)
   392→        else:
   393→            payload.setdefault("reset_on_scan", True)
   394→
   395→    # Preserve legacy dimensions even if a payload temporarily drops one.
   396→    for dim, display in _LEGACY_DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
   397→        payload = out.setdefault(dim, {})
   398→        payload.setdefault("display_name", display)
   399→        payload.setdefault("weight", _LEGACY_WEIGHT_BY_DIMENSION.get(dim, 1.0))
   400→        payload.setdefault("enabled_by_default", True)
   401→        payload.setdefault("reset_on_scan", dim in _LEGACY_RESET_ON_SCAN_DIMENSIONS)
   402→
   403→    return out
   404→
   405→
   406→@lru_cache(maxsize=1)
   407→def load_subjective_dimension_metadata() -> dict[str, dict[str, object]]:
   408→    """Return merged metadata across all known dimensions/languages."""
   409→    return _build_subjective_dimension_metadata(lang_name=None)
   410→
   411→
   412→@lru_cache(maxsize=16)
   413→def load_subjective_dimension_metadata_for_lang(
   414→    lang_name: str | None,
   415→) -> dict[str, dict[str, object]]:
   416→    """Return merged metadata for one language (with language overrides)."""
   417→    normalized = _normalize_lang_name(lang_name)
   418→    return _build_subjective_dimension_metadata(lang_name=normalized)
   419→
   420→
   421→def _metadata_registry(lang_name: str | None) -> dict[str, dict[str, object]]:
   422→    normalized = _normalize_lang_name(lang_name)
   423→    if normalized is None:
   424→        return load_subjective_dimension_metadata()
   425→    return load_subjective_dimension_metadata_for_lang(normalized)
   426→
   427→
   428→def get_dimension_metadata(
   429→    dimension_name: str, *, lang_name: str | None = None
   430→) -> dict[str, object]:
   431→    """Return metadata for one dimension key (with sane defaults)."""
   432→    dim = _normalize_dimension_name(dimension_name)
   433→    all_meta = _metadata_registry(lang_name)
   434→    payload = dict(all_meta.get(dim, {}))
   435→
   436→    payload.setdefault("display_name", _title_display_name(dim))
   437→    payload.setdefault("weight", 1.0)
   438→    payload.setdefault("enabled_by_default", False)
   439→    payload.setdefault("reset_on_scan", True)
   440→    return payload
   441→
   442→
   443→def dimension_display_name(dimension_name: str, *, lang_name: str | None = None) -> str:
   444→    meta = get_dimension_metadata(dimension_name, lang_name=lang_name)
   445→    return str(meta.get("display_name", _title_display_name(dimension_name)))
   446→
   447→
   448→def dimension_weight(dimension_name: str, *, lang_name: str | None = None) -> float:
   449→    meta = get_dimension_metadata(dimension_name, lang_name=lang_name)
   450→    raw = meta.get("weight", 1.0)
   451→    if is_numeric(raw):
   452→        return max(0.0, float(raw))
   453→    return 1.0
   454→
   455→
   456→def default_display_names_map(*, lang_name: str | None = None) -> dict[str, str]:
   457→    """Display-name map for default subjective dimensions."""
   458→    out: dict[str, str] = {}
   459→    for dim, payload in _metadata_registry(lang_name).items():
   460→        if not bool(payload.get("enabled_by_default", False)):
   461→            continue
   462→        out[dim] = str(payload.get("display_name", _title_display_name(dim)))
   463→    return out
   464→
   465→
   466→def resettable_default_dimensions(*, lang_name: str | None = None) -> tuple[str, ...]:
   467→    """Default subjective dimensions that should be reset by scan reset."""
   468→    out = []
   469→    for dim, payload in _metadata_registry(lang_name).items():
   470→        if not bool(payload.get("enabled_by_default", False)):
   471→            continue
   472→        if not bool(payload.get("reset_on_scan", True)):
   473→            continue
   474→        out.append(dim)
   475→    return tuple(sorted(set(out)))
   476→
   477→
   478→__all__ = [
   479→    "DISPLAY_NAMES",
   480→    "configure_subjective_dimension_providers",
   481→    "default_dimension_keys",
   482→    "default_dimension_keys_for_lang",
   483→    "default_display_names_map",
   484→    "dimension_display_name",
   485→    "dimension_weight",
   486→    "get_dimension_metadata",
   487→    "load_subjective_dimension_metadata",
   488→    "load_subjective_dimension_metadata_for_lang",
   489→    "reset_subjective_dimension_providers",
   490→    "resettable_default_dimensions",
   491→]
   492→
