## Detailed Implementation Plan: Consolidate Subjective Visibility Policy

### Problem Summary

The "should subjective work surface?" decision is made independently in 6+ locations with duplicated objective-backlog computation, scattered three-tier gating policy, and duplicated dimension-key normalization. This creates maintenance burden and risk of policy drift.

### Inventory of All Duplication Sites

**Objective backlog computation (6 sites):**

1. `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/stale_dimensions.py:279` -- `has_real_items` in `sync_stale_dimensions()`
2. `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/stale_dimensions.py:425` -- `has_objective` in `sync_create_plan_needed()`
3. `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/auto_cluster.py:492` -- `has_objective_items` in `_sync_subjective_clusters()`
4. `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py:87` -- `objective_count` in `build_work_queue()`
5. `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/helpers/subjective.py:36` -- `objective_backlog` parameter in `print_subjective_followup()`
6. `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/status_cmd.py:116-118` -- `_objective_backlog` computed from `QueueBreakdown`

**Dimension key normalization (3 independent implementations):**

1. `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/review/preflight.py:60` -- `_normalize_dimension_key()` using `re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9_]+", "_", text).strip("_")`
2. `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_scoring/subjective/core.py:39` -- `_normalize_dimension_key()` using `"_".join(name.strip().lower().replace("-", "_").split())`
3. `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/helpers.py:116` -- `slugify()` using `re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9_]+", "_", text.lower()).strip("_")`

Plus `_canonical_subjective_dimension_key()` at helpers.py:120 which wraps slugify with DISPLAY_NAMES lookup.

---

### Phase 1: Create the Policy Module

**New file: `desloppify/engine/_plan/subjective_policy.py`**

This module has two parts: a frozen dataclass capturing the computed policy state, and a factory function that computes it from state.

```python
"""Single source of truth for subjective dimension visibility policy.

Centralizes the 'should subjective work surface?' decision that was
previously scattered across sync_stale_dimensions, auto_cluster,
build_work_queue, and display helpers.

The policy is:
- **Unscored** (initial_review): ALWAYS surface -- onboarding priority.
- **Stale**: Only surface when objective backlog is drained.
- **Under-target**: Only surface when objective backlog is drained.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass

from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel

# Detectors whose findings are NOT objective mechanical work.
# Used to decide when the objective backlog is drained.
NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS: frozenset[str] = frozenset({
    "review", "concerns", "subjective_review", "subjective_assessment",
})


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SubjectiveVisibility:
    """Immutable snapshot of subjective dimension visibility decisions.
    
    Compute once per operation, pass to all consumers.
    """
    
    stale_ids: frozenset[str]
    unscored_ids: frozenset[str]
    under_target_ids: frozenset[str]
    objective_open_count: int
    
    @property
    def has_objective_backlog(self) -> bool:
        return self.objective_open_count > 0
    
    @property
    def surface_stale(self) -> bool:
        """Stale dims only surface when objective backlog is drained."""
        return not self.has_objective_backlog
    
    @property
    def surface_under_target(self) -> bool:
        """Under-target dims only surface when objective backlog is drained."""
        return not self.has_objective_backlog
    
    def should_surface(self, item_id: str, *, initial_review: bool = False) -> bool:
        """Should a subjective item appear in the work queue?"""
        if initial_review or item_id in self.unscored_ids:
            return True
        return not self.has_objective_backlog
    
    def should_inject_to_queue(self, item_id: str) -> bool:
        """Should a subjective ID be injected into plan queue_order?"""
        if item_id in self.unscored_ids:
            return True
        if item_id in self.stale_ids:
            return self.surface_stale
        if item_id in self.under_target_ids:
            return self.surface_under_target
        return False
    
    def should_evict_from_queue(self, item_id: str) -> bool:
        """Should a subjective ID be evicted from plan queue_order?"""
        if item_id in self.unscored_ids:
            return False
        if item_id in self.under_target_ids and self.has_objective_backlog:
            return True
        return False


def count_objective_open(state: StateModel) -> int:
    """Count open, non-suppressed, objective findings."""
    return sum(
        1 for f in state.get("findings", {}).values()
        if f.get("status") == "open"
        and f.get("detector") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
        and not f.get("suppressed")
    )


def compute_subjective_visibility(
    state: StateModel,
    *,
    target_strict: float = 95.0,
) -> SubjectiveVisibility:
    """Compute the current subjective visibility policy from state.
    
    This is the single factory function. Call once per scan/command,
    pass the result to all consumers.
    """
    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (
        _current_stale_ids,
        current_under_target_ids,
        current_unscored_ids,
    )

    return SubjectiveVisibility(
        stale_ids=frozenset(_current_stale_ids(state)),
        unscored_ids=frozenset(current_unscored_ids(state)),
        under_target_ids=frozenset(
            current_under_target_ids(state, target_strict=target_strict)
        ),
        objective_open_count=count_objective_open(state),
    )
```

**Key design decisions:**

- `NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS` moves here from `stale_dimensions.py` (with a re-export alias at the old location for backward compatibility).
- The lazy imports of `_current_stale_ids`, `current_unscored_ids`, `current_under_target_ids` inside `compute_subjective_visibility()` avoid circular imports since `stale_dimensions.py` will import from this module for the constant.
- `count_objective_open()` is a standalone helper so `sync_create_plan_needed` can use it too (it does not suppress findings, unlike the backlog check in work queue which filters by scope).
- The dataclass is frozen/immutable -- compute once, use everywhere in a single operation.

### Phase 3: Refactor `sync_stale_dimensions()` to Accept Policy

**File: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/stale_dimensions.py`**

Current code at line 279:
```python
has_real_items = any(
    f.get("status") == "open"
    and f.get("detector") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
    and not f.get("suppressed")
    for f in state.get("findings", {}).values()
)
if not has_real_items and stale_ids:
```

New signature adds optional `policy` parameter:
```python
def sync_stale_dimensions(
    plan: PlanModel,
    state: StateModel,
    *,
    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
) -> StaleDimensionSyncResult:
```

Replace the backlog check:
```python
from desloppify.engine._plan.subjective_policy import (
    SubjectiveVisibility,
    compute_subjective_visibility,
)

# Use provided policy or compute one
_policy = policy or compute_subjective_visibility(state)

# ... (prune logic stays the same) ...

# --- Inject: populate when no objective items remain -----------------
if _policy.surface_stale and stale_ids:
    existing = set(order)
    for sid in sorted(stale_ids):
        if sid not in existing:
            order.append(sid)
            result.injected.append(sid)
```

Similarly update `sync_create_plan_needed()` (line 425):
```python
def sync_create_plan_needed(
    plan: PlanModel,
    state: StateModel,
    *,
    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None,
) -> CreatePlanSyncResult:
```

Replace:
```python
has_objective = any(
    f.get("status") == "open"
    and f.get("detector") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
    for f in findings.values()
)
if not has_objective:
    return result
```

With:
```python
_policy = policy or compute_subjective_visibility(state)
if not _policy.has_objective_backlog:
    return result
```

Note: `sync_create_plan_needed` previously did not check `suppressed`. This is a subtle semantic difference -- the policy's `count_objective_open()` checks `not f.get("suppressed")`. This is actually the correct behavior (suppressed findings shouldn't count as backlog), so this is a bug fix.

### Phase 4: Refactor `_sync_subjective_clusters()` to Accept Policy

### Phase 5: Refactor `build_work_queue()` to Accept Policy

**File: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py`**

Add `policy` to `QueueBuildOptions`:
```python
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class QueueBuildOptions:
    # ... existing fields ...
    policy: SubjectiveVisibility | None = None
```

Replace lines 87-108 in `build_work_queue()`:
```python
# Current:
objective_count = sum(
    1 for item in finding_items
    if item.get("detector") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
)
# ...
if not item.get("initial_review") and objective_count > 0:
    continue
```

With:
```python
_policy = resolved_options.policy

for item in subjective_items:
    if not scope_matches(item, resolved_options.scope):
        continue
    if _policy is not None:
        if not _policy.should_surface(
            item["id"], initial_review=item.get("initial_review", False)
        ):
            continue
    else:
        # Fallback: compute inline (backward compat for callers without policy)
        objective_count = sum(
            1 for it in finding_items
            if it.get("detector") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
        )
        if not item.get("initial_review") and objective_count > 0:
            continue
    all_items.append(item)
```

Important: The work queue's `objective_count` is computed from `finding_items` (already scope-filtered), while the policy's `objective_open_count` is computed from all findings. This is a semantic difference. The policy should be the source of truth -- the scoped count was never intentionally different. For the first iteration, keep the fallback for callers that don't provide a policy, but document the intent to converge.

### Phase 7: Wire Policy Through Scan Workflow

**File: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/scan/scan_workflow.py`**

In `_reconcile_plan_post_scan()`, compute the policy once and pass it to all sync functions:

```python
def _reconcile_plan_post_scan(runtime: "ScanRuntime") -> None:
    try:
        plan_path = ...
        plan = load_plan(plan_path)
        dirty = False

        from desloppify.engine._plan.subjective_policy import compute_subjective_visibility
        _target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config, fallback=95.0)
        policy = compute_subjective_visibility(runtime.state, target_strict=_target_strict)

        if _apply_plan_reconciliation(plan, runtime.state, reconcile_plan_after_scan):
            dirty = True

        # Pass policy to sync functions
        unscored_changed = _sync_unscored_dimensions(plan, runtime.state, sync_unscored_dimensions)
        # ...
        stale_changed = _sync_stale_dimensions(plan, runtime.state,
            lambda p, s: sync_stale_dimensions(p, s, policy=policy))
        # ...
        auto_changed = _sync_auto_clusters(
            plan, runtime.state, target_strict=_target_strict, policy=policy)
        # ...
```

Wait -- the scan_workflow wraps sync functions through local helper functions that take a `sync_fn` callable. The cleanest approach is to update those local helpers:

```python
def _sync_stale_dimensions(plan, state, sync_fn, *, policy=None) -> bool:
    sync = sync_fn(plan, state, policy=policy) if policy else sync_fn(plan, state)
    # ... rest unchanged
```

Or better -- just call the sync functions directly now rather than through indirection. The indirection was useful when the functions were simpler, but adding `policy` to the lambda wrappers is awkward. Update the scan_workflow callers to call sync functions directly with the policy kwarg.

### Phase 10 (Deferred): Consolidate Dimension Key Normalization

This is related but separable work. The three normalization functions differ subtly:

| Location | Implementation |
|---|---|
| `preflight.py:60` | `re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9_]+", "_", text).strip("_")` |
| `subjective/core.py:39` | `"_".join(name.strip().lower().replace("-", "_").split())` |
| `helpers.py:116` (slugify) | `re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9_]+", "_", text.lower()).strip("_")` |

The `preflight` version and `slugify` are effectively identical. The `subjective/core.py` version differs for edge cases (consecutive spaces, mixed hyphens). Recommendation: adopt `slugify` as the canonical implementation and have preflight and subjective/core delegate to it. But this is a separate cleanup -- do NOT bundle it with the policy consolidation to keep the PR focused.

### Implementation Sequence

| Step | Description | Risk | Files Changed |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Create `subjective_policy.py` with `SubjectiveVisibility`, `compute_subjective_visibility`, `count_objective_open`, and `NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS` | Low -- new file, no existing behavior changes | 1 new file |
| 2 | Move `NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS` origin, update imports | Low -- constant value unchanged | `stale_dimensions.py`, `auto_cluster.py`, `core.py` |
| 3 | Add `policy=` param to `sync_stale_dimensions()` and `sync_create_plan_needed()` | Medium -- optional param, backward compat | `stale_dimensions.py` |
| 4 | Add `policy=` param to `auto_cluster_findings()` and `_sync_subjective_clusters()` | Medium -- internal function, passes through | `auto_cluster.py` |
| 5 | Add `policy` to `QueueBuildOptions` and use in `build_work_queue()` | Medium -- fallback preserves old behavior | `core.py` |
| 6 | Update display-layer functions to accept `policy` | Low -- additive optional param | `subjective.py`, `render.py`, `show/render.py`, `show/cmd.py`, `status_cmd.py` |
| 7 | Wire policy through `_reconcile_plan_post_scan()` | Medium -- orchestration change | `scan_workflow.py` |
| 8 | Update `engine/plan.py` facade re-exports | Low -- additive | `plan.py` |
| 9 | Write new tests, verify existing tests pass | Low | Test files |

**Steps 1-2 can be done first and independently verified. Steps 3-5 are the core refactoring. Steps 6-7 are the wiring. Step 9 should be interleaved -- write the new policy tests after step 1, update test expectations after steps 3-5.**

---

### Risks and Mitigations

1. **Semantic difference in scope filtering**: `build_work_queue` currently computes `objective_count` from scope-filtered `finding_items`, while the policy computes from all findings. Mitigation: Keep the inline fallback in step 5 for callers without a policy. Document the intent to converge. The "all findings" semantics is more correct -- you don't want stale-subjective gating to change based on what `--scope` you pass to `show`.

2. **`sync_create_plan_needed` bug fix**: The existing code does not check `f.get("suppressed")` in its backlog count. The policy does. This is a minor behavioral change that is actually a bug fix (suppressed findings should not count as backlog). Mitigation: Call it out in the PR description.

3. **Circular imports**: `subjective_policy.py` needs `stale_dimensions.py` for classification functions, and `stale_dimensions.py` will import from `subjective_policy.py` for `NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS`. Mitigation: Use lazy imports in `compute_subjective_visibility()` (already shown in the design).

4. **Backward compatibility**: All existing public APIs keep their old parameters. The `policy` parameter is always optional with a `None` default. Old callers continue to work unchanged.

4. Errors and fixes:
   - **`test_stale_cluster_updated` failed**: After Bug 1 fix, the test added `convention_drift` to `queue_order` but not to state's stale dimensions. With the fix, the ID correctly wasn't recognized as stale. Fixed by providing `state2 = _state_with_stale_dimensions("design_coherence", "error_consistency", "convention_drift")`.
   - **`next` command still showed subjective items after Bug 1+2 fixes**: Investigation revealed a third bug — `build_work_queue()` in `core.py` injects synthetic subjective items at query time independently of plan queue_order, and only gated `stale_review` items, not under-target items.
   - **`python -m desloppify` used stale site-packages copy**: When running from Reigh directory, Python loaded the installed v0.8.0 from `~/.pyenv/versions/3.11.11/lib/python3.11/site-packages/desloppify/` instead of local source. The installed copy had old `stale_review` check. Fixed with `pip install -e .`.
   - **Clearing `__pycache__` didn't help**: Even after clearing pycache, the site-packages copy was still loaded because of Python's import path resolution (CWD not in sys.path when running from Reigh).
   - **`test_subjective_items_do_not_starve_objective_queue_head` failed in TWO locations**: Both `tests/review/work_queue_cases.py` and `tests/review/integration/work_queue_cases.py` had the same test. Had to update both.
   - **Active cluster focus** on `subjective-high-impact` was filtering the queue to only 4 items during testing — had to clear `plan["active_cluster"]`.

5. Problem Solving:
   - **Solved**: Bug 1 (stale cluster membership misclassifying under-target as stale)
   - **Solved**: Bug 2 (under-target IDs persisting in queue when objective backlog returns)
   - **Solved**: Bug 3 (synthetic subjective items bypassing gating in build_work_queue)
   - **Solved**: All 3853 tests pass, Reigh `next` shows objective structural finding as #1
   - **Root cause identified**: Three independent systems (plan sync, auto-clustering, work queue building) each independently decide "should subjective work surface?" without sharing a policy, leading to drift and inconsistency
   - **Currently planning**: Consolidation of scattered gating into a single `SubjectiveVisibility` policy module
